We previously reported a State Saturation Trap: threshold-on-state triggers over a continuous affect engine become near-constant alarms on SWE-bench debugging agents (Modgil 2026). A post-release audit found the engine received dt=0 between actions, so its exponential decay never operated: the published trap is a pure-accumulator result. We correct the record (erratum, v2) and treat the flaw as an experiment. The key variable it exposes is whether a monitor's dynamics are calibrated in sample time (per observation, as in CUSUM) or wall-clock time (half-lives in seconds, as in affect models and EMA baselines). On fixed-rate streams these coincide; on agent streams, where inter-action time varies by orders of magnitude, they do not. A pre-registered sweep over uniform intervals (dt in {0..600}s) on 20 trajectories shows the wall-clock level trigger has two regimes: at dt<=1s a constant alarm (20/20; median 18 firings); at dt>=60s silent. Every critical dt lies in (1,30]s. Real agent runs measure latency at median 1.53s (p90 2.33s); real coding cadence sits inside the trap regime, vindicating the empirical finding under a corrected mechanism. The structure is a property of the calibration class, not the engine: a minimal wall-clock accumulator over the raw error stream reproduces the same cliff, while a sample-time CUSUM over the identical stream is exactly dt-invariant (20/20). A rising-edge trigger with hysteresis fires 0-3 times per trajectory in every condition. We conclude that wall-clock-calibrated leaky-integrator monitors admit no regime in which they act as moment detectors on agent streams; transition detection escapes the trap at every cadence, but does not recover human intervention timing.

</details>


### [75] [Interpretable and Verifiable Hardware Generation with LLM-Driven Stepwise Refinement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19387)
*You Li,Samuel Mandell,David Z. Pan*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出一种结合LLM创造力与形式化方法严格性的硬件生成框架，通过迭代应用变换规则将设计规范转换为正确性有保证的RTL程序。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在RTL生成中存在幻觉问题（引入语义和逻辑错误），硬件工程师因芯片设计制造的高风险而不愿依赖LLM。

Method: 设计一套涵盖多种设计决策和硬件特征的变换规则，LLM智能体通过迭代应用这些规则，将设计规范转化为正确性有保证的RTL程序。

Result: 实验结果表明该框架在有效性和效率方面表现良好。

Conclusion: 该框架成功将LLM的创造力和广泛知识与形式化方法的可解释性和数学严谨性结合，实现了正确性有保证的RTL生成。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have achieved remarkable success in software development. However, they are susceptible to hallucinations, meaning that they can introduce subtle semantic and logical errors. Due to the high stakes in chip design and manufacturing, hardware engineers are still reluctant to rely on LLMs for register-transfer level (RTL) generation. In this paper, we propose a hardware generation framework that combines the creativity and broad knowledge of LLMs with the explainability and mathematical rigor of formal methods. Specifically, we devise a set of transformation rules that cover various design decisions and hardware features. By iteratively applying these rules, an LLM agent can convert a design specification into an RTL program with guaranteed correctness. Experimental results demonstrate the effectiveness and efficiency of the framework.

</details>


### [76] [Beyond the GUI Paradigm: Do Mobile Agents Need the Phone Screen?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19388)
*Li Gu,Zihuan Jiang,Linqiang Guo,Zhixiang Chi,Ziqiang Wang,Huan Liu,Yuanhao Yu,Tse-Hsun Chen,Yang Wang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文论证命令行界面（CLI）应作为移动智能体的第一等交互范式，实验表明Claude Code等CLI智能体在AndroidWorld和MobileWorld上超越所有可复现的GUI基线，并提出CLI-Advantage任务套件以覆盖GUI无法处理的日常用户意图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前移动智能体研究过度聚焦GUI范式，忽略了移动平台同样存在的CLI接口。CLI能直接访问设备服务和数据，应获得与GUI同等的重视。

Method: 在AndroidWorld和MobileWorld基准上，评估三种编码智能体（Claude Code、Terminus-2、mini-swe-agent）在四种模型API下的表现，与三种可复现的GUI基线对比；构建CLI-Advantage任务套件（45个模板，5个类别）并给出oracle CLI解决方案。

Result: Claude Code (Opus 4.7)在AndroidWorld达71.8%、MobileWorld达51.9%，超越所有GUI基线；oracle CLI解达到88.8%和86.3%；CLI智能体在所有5个任务类别上优于GUI基线，且平均步骤数更少（10.7 vs. 18.6）。

Conclusion: CLI范式是移动智能体有前景的方向，现有结果仍远低于oracle上限，未来提升空间巨大；将开源全部实现以推动后续研究。

Abstract: Recent advances in mobile agents are dominated by the GUI paradigm, in which agents perceive UI information and emit screen interactions. However, mobile platforms also expose a command-line interface (CLI) that provides direct access to device services and data. We argue CLI deserves first-class consideration alongside GUI. We evaluate three coding agents (Claude Code, Terminus-2, mini-swe-agent) across four model APIs on AndroidWorld and MobileWorld without any mobile-specific post-training, comparing against three reproducible GUI baselines (GUI-Owl-1.5-32B, MAI-UI, Qwen3-VL-32B). Claude Code (Opus 4.7) reaches 71.8\% and 51.9\%, outperforming every reproducible GUI baseline (69.3/68.1/57.8\% on AndroidWorld; 43.2/26.3/13.3\% on MobileWorld), while every other CLI configuration remains competitive. To establish the paradigm's ceiling, we provide oracle CLI solutions that reach 88.8\% on AndroidWorld (103/116 tasks CLI-solvable) and 86.3\% on MobileWorld (101/117 tasks CLI-solvable), indicating substantial room for future improvement. To cover everyday user intents beyond the GUI scope, we introduce the \textbf{CLI-Advantage Task Suite}, comprising 45 templates across five categories: bulk operations, multi-condition filtering, aggregation, cross-app workflows, and hidden device state. Every CLI agent outperforms every GUI baseline in all five categories, with substantially fewer steps per task (10.7 vs.\ 18.6). To support future research on mobile CLI agents, we will open-source agent implementations, oracle solutions, the CLI-Advantage suite, and evaluation infrastructure.

</details>


### [77] [Execution-bound advisory automation for agentic AI: a reproducible AIBOM-driven CSAF-VEX framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19390)
*Petar Radanliev,Omar Santos,Carsten Maple,Kay Atefi*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出了一个协议驱动的框架，将SBOM和AIBOM工件与确定性环境捕获及结构化运行时遥测绑定，通过静态工件、观测到的激活条件和执行策略计算可利用性，生成经加密签名并通过确定性回放验证的CSAF VEX公告，并在约10000个组件条目的合成Agentic AI工作负载上进行了评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有软件供应链安全方法缺乏将SBOM/AIBOM工件与运行时行为动态关联的机制，无法准确判断组件在实际运行环境中的可利用性，导致安全公告的精确性和可信度不足。

Method: 提出协议驱动框架：(1) 将SBOM和AIBOM工件与确定性环境捕获和结构化运行时遥测绑定；(2) 从声明工件、观测激活条件和执行策略三个维度计算可利用性；(3) 基于静态和运行时证据生成CSAF VEX公告，经加密签名后通过确定性回放验证。

Result: 在约10000个组件条目的合成Agentic AI工作负载上进行了评估，规模从50到5000个组件不等，使用了OSV、GitHub Advisory、KEV和EPSS数据集。

Conclusion: 该框架通过将SBOM/AIBOM工件与运行时遥测相结合，实现了基于证据的可利用性计算和可验证的VEX公告生成，提升了软件供应链安全评估的准确性和可信度。

Abstract: A protocol driven framework is presented that binds SBOM and AIBOM artefacts to deterministic environment capture and structured runtime telemetry. Exploitability is computed from declared artefacts, observed activation conditions, and enforced execution policies. CSAF VEX advisories are generated from combined static and runtime evidence, cryptographically signed, and validated through deterministic replay. Evaluation uses approximately 10000 component entries across synthetic Agentic AI workloads 50 to 5000 components, incorporating OSV, GitHub Advisory, KEV, and EPSS datasets.

</details>


### [78] [DevOps and General Developers: Insights from Stack Overflow's 2023 Survey](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19395)
*Hasan Abdulla,Fatema AlJazeeri,Fawzi AlBalooshi,Jaflah Al-Ammary*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本研究通过Stack Overflow 2023开发者调查数据，定量分析了DevOps专家与普通软件开发者在工具、技术、方法论及人口统计学方面的差异，发现两者在技术偏好上无显著差异，但存在年龄分布和工作模式上的不同。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究DevOps专家与普通软件开发者在当前软件开发环境中的不同角色，包括工具使用、技术偏好、方法论及人口统计学特征的差异，以及两者在该领域的独特贡献与挑战。

Method: 采用定量分析方法，基于Stack Overflow 2023开发者调查数据，使用Python的Pandas库进行数据分析，对DevOps专家与普通开发者的技术偏好、人口统计信息和专业经验进行对比分析。

Result: DevOps专家与普通软件开发者在工具和技术偏好上无显著差异，两者都使用Docker和Kubernetes等工具；普通开发者使用更多样化的工具；年龄分布上普通开发者更年轻，DevOps专家多为职业生涯中期；两者都在适应远程和混合工作模式。

Conclusion: DevOps专家与普通开发者角色互补而非对立，DevOps的重要性日益增长；研究为学术界和行业人士理解软件开发生态中角色动态演变提供了有价值的参考。

Abstract: Purpose: To investigate the distinct roles of DevOps specialists and general software developers, examining their varying use of tools, technologies, methodologies, and demographics in the current software development environment. In addition, to differentiate these two professional groups regarding their unique contributions and challenges in the field.
  Design/Methodology/Approach: The research uses a quantitative approach to analyze data from the Stack Overflow 2023 Developer Survey. It focuses on a comparative analysis of technological preferences, demographic information, and professional experiences between DevOps specialists and general developers, highlighting key trends and differences. The data analysis was conducted using Python's Pandas library for data analysis.
  Findings: The research indicates no significant difference in the tool and technology preferences between DevOps specialists and general software developers, highlighting their complementary roles. DevOps specialists and general software developers use tools like Docker and Kubernetes, emphasizing efficiency and automation. While general developers employ diverse tools for various role demands, demographic trends show younger general developers and mid-career DevOps professionals. This age range reflects growing experience in DevOps, and both groups are adapting to remote and hybrid work models in the evolving tech industry.
  Practical Implications: This research offers perspectives on the dynamic roles within software development, emphasizing the growing importance of DevOps. It is a valuable resource for academic and industry professionals to understand the evolving dynamics in software development roles.
  Originality/Value: This research fills a significant gap in the existing literature regarding the evolving dynamics of software development roles.

</details>


### [79] [JustDiag!: A Diagnostic Justification Engine for Accountable Root Cause Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19407)
*Tingzhu Bi,Xinrui Jiang,Xun Zhang,Pengcheng Su,Congjie He,Jinglin Li,Ping Wang,Meng Ma*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: JustDiag是一个面向高可靠性运维场景的根因分析诊断理由引擎，通过显式维护证据、发现、竞争假设、冲突和下一步检查等过程状态，实现可问责的RCA。在66个真实事件上，相比对照组，JustDiag在最终答案质量和过程质量上均表现更优，但接受略低的终止完成率，以换取更校准的不闭合处理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大语言模型生成的根因分析虽然流畅，但仅凭流畅的最终答案不足以在高风险运维中实现可问责性；工程师需要了解支持诊断的证据、考虑过的替代假设、存在的矛盾以及系统是否保留了不确定性。

Method: 提出JustDiag诊断理由引擎，显式维护包含证据、发现、竞争假设、冲突和下一步检查的过程状态；采用双层评估协议，分别评分最终答案质量和过程质量。

Result: 在66个真实事件上，相比无诊断理由的对照组，JustDiag在结果分数和过程分数上均更强，同时因更校准的不闭合判断而接受略低的终止完成率。

Conclusion: 可问责的根因分析需要显式的诊断理由工件和过程感知的评估，而不仅仅是流畅的最终答案。

Abstract: Large language models can produce fluent root cause analyses, but fluent final answers alone are insufficient evidence for accountability in high-stakes operations. In real incident response, engineers need to know what evidence supported a diagnosis, which alternatives were considered, where contradictions remained, and whether the system resolved the case or preserved uncertainty. We address this gap with JustDiag, a diagnostic justification engine for RCA that maintains an explicit process state over evidence, findings, competing hypotheses, conflicts, and next checks. We evaluated the system on 66 real-world incidents using a two-layer protocol that separately scores final-answer quality and process quality. Relative to a matched control without diagnostic justification, JustDiag achieved stronger outcome and process scores, while accepting slightly lower terminal completion due to more calibrated non-closure. These results suggest that accountable RCA requires explicit diagnostic justification artifacts and process-aware evaluation, not only fluent final answers.

</details>


### [80] [FAPO: Fully Autonomous Prompt Optimization of Multi-Step LLM Pipelines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19605)
*Paul Kassianik,Baturay Saglam,Huaibo Zhao,Blaine Nelson,Supriti Vijay,Aman Priyanshu,Amin Karbasi*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: FAPO是一种全自主LLM流水线优化框架，通过诊断中间步骤故障并自动选择提示优化或结构变更，在6个基准测试和3个模型上平均比基线GEPA提升14.1个百分点，尤其在需要结构性变更时提升达33.8个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多步骤LLM流水线中检索、推理和格式化步骤之间存在交互故障，仅靠提示优化无法定位流水线中的瓶颈环节。

Method: FAPO框架在标准化代码库中让Claude Code优化LLM流水线：评估流水线、检查中间步骤、诊断故障、提出局部范围修改、重复验证变体以优化评分函数。首先尝试提示编辑，当提示优化不足且归因分析识别出结构性瓶颈时，才在允许范围内改变链式结构。

Result: 在6个基准测试和3个任务模型的18项比较中，FAPO在15项中击败基线GEPA；其中11项具有非重叠的均值±试验标准差范围；平均增益+14.1个百分点。在需要结构性变更的6项比较中全部获胜，平均增益+33.8个百分点。在安全任务CTIBench-RCM上，仅提示优化就使GPT-5提升+4.0pp，Foundation-Sec-8B-Instruct提升+7.1pp，Foundation-Sec-8B-Reasoning提升+2.0pp。

Conclusion: FAPO是一种针对通用和安全任务的流水线优化技术，达到了当前最优水平。

Abstract: Multi-step LLM pipelines fail through interactions among retrieval, reasoning, and formatting steps, so prompt-only optimization can miss bottlenecks in the chain. We present FAPO (Fully Autonomous Prompt Optimization), a framework that lets Claude Code optimize an LLM pipeline inside a standardized codebase. FAPO evaluates a pipeline, inspects intermediate steps, diagnoses failures, proposes scoped changes, and validates variants repeatedly to optimize against a score function. It first tries prompt edits and, only when prompt optimization appears insufficient, changes chain structure within the permitted scope when attribution identifies a structural bottleneck. Across six benchmarks and three task models, FAPO beats the baseline GEPA in 15 of 18 model-benchmark comparisons. In 11 model-benchmark comparisons, FAPO wins with non-overlapping mean $\pm$ trial-standard-deviation ranges, and the mean FAPO-GEPA gain is +14.1 pp. In the six HoVer and IFBench comparisons where prompt-first search escalated to structural changes, FAPO wins all six with a mean gain of +33.8 pp. FAPO also improves performance on security tasks: on CTIBench-RCM, a security CVE-to-CWE task, prompt-only FAPO lifts test accuracy by +4.0 pp on GPT-5, +7.1 pp on Foundation-Sec-8B-Instruct, and +2.0 pp on Foundation-Sec-8B-Reasoning. These results position FAPO as a state-of-the-art pipeline optimization technique for both general-purpose and security-focused tasks.

</details>


### [81] [StaminaBench: Stress-Testing Coding Agents over 100 Interaction Turns](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19613)
*Vlad Sobal,Shuo Yang,Yuting Zhang,Wei Xia,Stefano Soatto*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: StaminaBench是一个衡量编码智能体在连续交互轮次（变更请求）中 stamina 的基准测试。实验发现，所有测试模型在5-6轮内均失败，传递测试反馈并允许重试可将通过轮次提升最多12倍，且好的harness对性能至关重要。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准测试多采用任务解决率（fraction-of-tasks-solved）指标，这与实际 vibe-coding 场景中会话持续数十甚至数百轮次的模式不匹配。因此需要一个新的基准来评估编码智能体在连续多轮变更请求下的持久能力。

Method: 构建 StaminaBench：智能体实现REST API服务器并通过程序化生成的可调数量（实验中为100个）的后续变更请求进行修改。测试完全程序化生成（无LLM参与），变更序列来自硬编码或LLM驱动的采样器，限定在结构化动作空间内。智能体与服务器在隔离环境中通过HTTP通信，实现黑盒、语言无关的测试。评估了6种agent harness搭配7种开源LLM，在20个场景中各进行100轮测试。

Result: (1) 所有测试模型在5-6轮内失败，确认无充分测试的vibe-coding方式会产生bug；(2) 将测试反馈传回智能体并允许重试可将通过轮次提升最多12倍；(3) 好的harness对性能至关重要：强模型在最佳和最差harness间差距达6倍，弱模型在任何harness下均失败。

Conclusion: StaminaBench能够有效衡量编码智能体的多轮持久能力，揭示了现有LLM在连续变更场景下的脆弱性，并展示了测试反馈和harness选择的重要性。基准测试和生成任务已开源以促进相关研究。

Abstract: We introduce StaminaBench, a benchmark that measures the stamina of coding agents: how many consecutive interaction turns (change requests) they can handle before failing. Unlike the prevailing fraction-of-tasks-solved metric, this matches real vibe-coding where sessions run dozens or hundreds of turns. In StaminaBench, agents implement a REST API server and modify it across a tunable number of procedurally generated follow-up change requests - 100 in our experiments, resulting in codebases of up to 6,000 lines. Tests are generated fully programmatically without LLM involvement, ensuring reproducibility and reliability; change sequences are drawn from either a hardcoded or LLM-driven sampler, both constrained to a structured action space to ensure changes are valid. The agent and the server run in an isolated environment and communicate with the benchmark through HTTP, making testing fully black-box and language-agnostic. We evaluate six agent harnesses paired with seven open-source LLMs across 20 scenarios of 100 turns each and find that: (1) all the tested models fail within 5-6 turns, confirming that vibe-coding-style programming without thorough testing produces bugs; (2) passing test feedback back to the agent and allowing it to retry improves passed turn count by up to 12x; and (3) a good harness is required for strong performance: stronger models exhibit up to a 6x gap between their best and worst harness, while weaker models fail with any harness. We release the benchmark and the generated tasks to enable further research into multi-turn coding agent behavior. Benchmark code and data: github.com/amazon-science/StaminaBench.

</details>


### [82] [Before the Pull Request: Mining Multi-Agent Coordination](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19616)
*Dipankar Sarkar*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出grite——一种基于git的去中心化协调协议，通过记录代理在PR之前的工作声明、分配和冲突，将重复工作从78%降至0%，吞吐量提升3倍以上，并可从事件日志中自动追溯多种失败模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自治编码代理大量创建PR，但被接受率低，PR级别的遥测无法解释协作与信任缺口，缺失的信号在于PR之前多代理如何声明、分配和冲突于共享工作。

Method: 提出grite，一种开源的去中心化协调协议，无需中央服务器，将仅追加、签名的事件日志存储在git本身中，记录代理间的协调过程。

Result: (i) 共享协调协议将重复工作从78%降至0%，有效吞吐量提升3倍以上；(ii) 所有代理的日志副本收敛到相同状态，无写操作静默丢失，而基于文件的跟踪器会丢失并发写入；(iii) 日志可作为可挖掘工件，自动恢复冲突编辑、锁饥饿、冗余发现、竞速关闭等失败模式，其中多种在PR历史中不可见。

Conclusion: grite通过git内嵌的去中心化协调日志，有效解决了多代理协作中的重复与冲突问题，提供了可审计的协调过程记录。

Abstract: Autonomous coding agents now open millions of pull requests, yet large-scale studies find their PRs are produced faster but accepted less often - a coordination and trust gap that pull-request-level telemetry cannot explain. We argue the missing signal lives before the PR, in how concurrent agents claim, divide, and collide over shared work. We study this process through grite, our open-source coordination substrate that needs no central server and stores its records inside git itself, so its append-only, signed event log captures the coordination process directly. We show that (i) this shared substrate reduces duplicate and conflicting work at bounded overhead - the share of work that merely re-does a teammate's task falls from 78% to 0% while useful throughput more than triples; (ii) every agent's copy of the log converges to the same state with no write silently dropped, where a file-based tracker loses concurrent writes; and (iii) the log is a mineable artefact from which concrete failure modes - conflicting edits, lock starvation, redundant rediscovery, race-to-close - are automatically recoverable with provenance, several invisible in pull-request history. We release the dataset, harness, and mining toolkit.

</details>


### [83] [Prompt Quality and Pull Request Outcomes: A Stage-Based Empirical Study of LLM-Assisted Development](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19644)
*Richard Sserunjogi,Daniel Ogenrwot,John Businge*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本研究分析了265个开发者-ChatGPT交互案例，通过上下文、具体性和验证三个维度对提示结构进行编码，发现提示特性在AI辅助软件工程工作流中具有不同阶段依赖效应：具体性最能预测代码生成，验证最能预测代码采纳，上下文最能预测集成深度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管LLM驱动的工具（如ChatGPT）越来越多地用于协作式软件工程工作流，但提示结构如何影响下游拉取请求（PR）结果尚不明确。先前研究主要关注对话有用性、生产力或粗粒度的采纳指标，未能充分理解提示结构在协作集成行为中的作用。

Method: 收集265个开发者-ChatGPT交互案例（来自开源PR中自我承认的ChatGPT使用），基于上下文、具体性和验证三个维度对提示结构进行操作化编码；评估LLM辅助标注与人类判断的一致性，并分析提示结构对代码生成、采纳和集成深度的影响。

Result: LLM辅助标注在不同维度和工作流上下文中与人类判断的一致性存在显著差异：具体性一致性最稳定，上下文被LLM系统性低估，验证难以一致评估。具体性和上下文与可操作的代码生成关联最强，验证是代码采纳的主要预测因子，集成深度与上下文关联最强。

Conclusion: 提示特征在AI辅助软件工程工作流中发挥 distinct, stage-dependent 效应，通过上下文基础、任务具体性和可评估性线索影响下游采纳和集成。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM)-powered tools such as ChatGPT are increasingly used in collaborative software engineering workflows, yet little is known about how prompt structure influences downstream pull request (PR) outcomes. Prior studies primarily examine conversational helpfulness, productivity, or coarse-grained adoption metrics, leaving the role of prompt structure in collaborative integration behavior insufficiently understood. We analyze 265 manually validated developer-ChatGPT interactions derived from self-admitted ChatGPT usage in open-source pull requests. Building on prior research on developer-facing artifacts and prompt engineering, we operationalize prompt structure using three dimensions: Context, Specificity, and Verification. We first evaluate whether LLM-assisted annotation can reliably reproduce human judgments of prompt structure, finding substantial variation across dimensions and workflow contexts. Specificity shows the most stable agreement with human judgments; Context is systematically under-scored by the LLM; and Verification remains difficult to assess consistently, motivating a hybrid human-LLM annotation strategy. Using this validated framework, we then examine how prompt structure influences actionable code generation, code adoption, and integration depth across AI-assisted PR workflows. Specificity and Context are most strongly associated with actionable code generation; Verification emerges as the primary predictor of code adoption; and integration depth is most strongly associated with Context. Overall, our findings show that prompt characteristics exert distinct, stage-dependent effects across AI-assisted software engineering workflows, influencing downstream adoption and integration through contextual grounding, task specificity, and evaluability cues.

</details>


### [84] [Library-Aware Doubles and Iterative Repair for Large Language Model-Generated Unit Tests in OpenSIL Firmware](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19725)
*Ma Toan Bach,Yuchi Zheng,Haingo Razafindranto,Tanvir Alam,Aric Leather,Ranveer Sandhu,Jitesh Arora*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于LLM的多智能体自动化工作流，用于为AMD开源固件库（openSIL）生成单元测试。该方法结合了测试脚手架自动生成、桩/模拟/伪造代码的库感知创建，以及基于编译日志和行覆盖反馈的迭代修复循环。在76个函数上评估，73个生成了可编译的UT，行覆盖率从73.9%（无指导）提升至98.8%（有行覆盖指导）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低级C固件中的单元测试因严格的构建约束（如缺失头文件、未解析符号和依赖不匹配）导致频繁的编译和链接失败，手动编写UT成本高昂且脆弱。

Method: 采用LLM引导的多智能体流水线，包含三个核心组件：①自动生成测试脚手架（test scaffolds）；②库感知地创建或复用桩（stubs）、模拟（mocks）、伪造（fakes）；③基于构建日志和行覆盖反馈的迭代编译-分发修复循环。

Result: 在76个目标函数中，工作流成功为73个生成可编译UT。无行覆盖指导或检索增强时平均行覆盖率为73.9%；48个函数的子集评估中，仅行覆盖指导达到98.8%，结合向量数据库检索达到94.7%。

Conclusion: 自动化的生成-修复流水线能够显著提升受限固件环境下UT创建效率和覆盖率，同时减少手动调试工作量。

Abstract: Validating changes in low-level C firmware is expensive because unit tests (UTs) are fragile under strict build constraints, where missing headers, unresolved symbols, and dependency mismatches frequently prevent compilation and linking. This study introduces an automated UT authoring workflow for the Open-Source Silicon Initialization Library (openSIL) firmware codebase maintained by Advanced Micro Devices (AMD) that reduces manual effort through a large language model (LLM) guided multi-agent pipeline. The workflow combines automated generation of test scaffolds, library-aware creation or reuse of stubs, mocks, and fakes, and an iterative compile-dispatch repair loop driven by build logs and line-coverage feedback. We evaluate the approach using compilation success, repair iterations, dispatch success, and line coverage, with time, cost, and token usage as secondary measures. Across 76 functions under test, the workflow generated compilable UTs for 73 functions. In a configuration without line coverage guidance or retrieval augmentation, mean line coverage reached 73.9%. On a 48-function subset evaluated under both configurations, mean line coverage reached 98.8% with line-coverage guidance alone and reached 94.7% when combined with vector-database retrieval. Results show that automated generation-and-repair pipelines can substantially improve UT creation efficiency and coverage for constrained firmware environments while reducing manual debugging effort.

</details>


### [85] [Agentic Electronic Design Automation: A Handoff Perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19795)
*Jiawei Liu,Peiyi Han,Yuntao Lu,Su Zheng,Fengyu Yan,Bei Yu*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文以**交接有效性（handoff validity）**为核心组织原则，系统综述了82个LLM驱动的EDA智能体系统，将其分为阶段边界、流程边界和组织边界三类，并据此提出了五层EDA智能体通信协议（EACP），旨在为可信赖的智能体EDA建立通用词汇与研究议程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: EDA本质上是多阶段、多交接的流程，设计工件、脚本和工程决策在工具、会话和组织之间反复传递。传统阶段级检查无法完整捕获显式或隐式交接需求。随着LLM智能体直接调用EDA工具、嵌入知识生成脚本并在会话间传递状态，其输出将影响下游工程决策，因此必须确保交接对象满足消费者假设与验收条件。现有研究缺乏统一的交接有效性框架，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 本文系统综述了82个EDA智能体系统，以交接有效性为组织原则，将系统分为三类：（1）**Stage-Bound**——在单个EDA阶段或有界验证任务内建立有效性；（2）**Flow-Bound**——跨工具、调用和会话保持连贯的工作流状态；（3）**Organization-Bound**——跨知识和权限边界维护源端溯源、范围和可采纳性。针对每类分析了交接契约、交接对象、协调机制和开放问题，并由此推导出五层EDA智能体通信协议（EACP）：智能体发现、智能体消息、工具调用、工作流编排、安全与IP协议。

Result: 提出了交接有效性作为EDA智能体系统设计的核心组织原则；对82个系统进行了三边界分类（Stage-Bound、Flow-Bound、Organization-Bound）；为每类系统提炼了交接契约、交接对象和协调机制；提出了五层EDA智能体通信协议（EACP）作为标准化通信框架。

Conclusion: 交接有效性为分析EDA智能体系统提供了统一的视角，三边界分类揭示了不同粒度下的交接挑战。EACP协议为多智能体EDA协作提供了可扩展的通信范式。本文旨在为可信赖的智能体EDA研究提供通用词汇和系统化研究议程。

Abstract: Electronic design automation (EDA) is inherently multi-stage and handoff-heavy. Design artifacts, flow scripts, and engineering decisions cross tool, session, and organizational boundaries before final implementation, signoff, or release. Each transfer carries explicit and implicit requirements that may not be fully captured by stage-local checks. LLM-based agents now invoke EDA tools directly, embed retrieved knowledge in executable scripts, and hand off state across sessions and stages. Once their outputs condition downstream engineering decisions, the transferred object must satisfy a handoff contract and meet the assumptions of its next consumer. This survey introduces handoff validity as its organizing principle. A handoff is valid when the transferred object satisfies the consumer's acceptance conditions and carries sufficient context, evidence, and provenance for downstream use. We review 82 systems and classify them into three boundary classes. Stage-Bound systems establish validity within a single EDA stage or bounded verification task. Flow-Bound systems preserve coherent workflow state across tools, invocations, and sessions. Organization-Bound systems maintain source grounding, provenance, scope, and admissibility across knowledge and authority boundaries. For each class, we analyze handoff contracts, handoff objects, coordination mechanisms, and open questions. These analyses motivate a five-layer EDA agent communication protocol (EACP), covering the agent discovery, agent message, tool invocation, workflow orchestration, and security and IP protocols. We aim to provide a common vocabulary and research agenda for trustworthy agentic EDA.

</details>


### [86] [The Hidden Environmental Cost of Poor Coding Practices in TensorFlow and Keras Applications: A Study on Resource Leaks and Carbon Emissions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19799)
*Bashar Abdallah,Gustavo Santos,Rola Al Bataineh,Alain Abran,Mohammad Hamdaqa*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该论文通过实验证明，机器学习代码中的两种资源泄漏气味（IMR和UTR）会显著增加能耗（32%-46%）和CO₂排放，且差异具有统计学意义，表明资源泄漏对ML可持续性构成可测量的风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管资源泄漏在机器学习代码中可能导致隐藏的能源浪费和碳排放增加，但缺乏量化其环境影响的实证证据。

Method: 对TensorFlow和Keras工作负载中的两种资源泄漏气味（Improper Model Reuse和Unreleased Tensor References）进行对照实验，执行相同的训练任务并与无气味基线比较，测量电耗和碳排放量。

Result: IMR和UTR分别使电耗增加约32%和46%，CO₂排放同比增加，配对统计检验表明差异具有系统性和统计显著性。

Conclusion: 资源泄漏气味对软件质量和可持续性均构成可测量风险，应将资源生命周期管理和能效考虑纳入ML开发实践中。

Abstract: Efficiency and sustainability are critical considerations in the development and deployment of machine learning (ML) applications. Among the factors influencing sustainability, resource leaks in ML code can introduce hidden inefficiencies that elevate energy consumption and CO2 emissions. Despite this, empirical evidence quantifying their environmental impact remains limited. This emerging results paper presents an initial empirical investigation of two common resource-leak smells, namely Improper Model Reuse (IMR) and Unreleased Tensor References (UTR), and their impact on energy consumption and CO2 emissions in TensorFlow and Keras workloads. Controlled experiments were conducted for each smell by executing identical training tasks while comparing against a smell-free baseline. Our preliminary results show that both smells consistently increase estimated electricity usage and carbon emissions. IMR and UTR increased electricity consumption by approximately 32% and 46%, respectively, with proportional increases in CO2 emissions. Paired statistical tests indicate that these differences are systematic and statistically significant, providing initial empirical evidence that resource-leak smells may degrade ML energy efficiency and environmental sustainability. These findings suggest that resource-leak smells pose measurable risks to both software quality and sustainability, emphasizing the importance of integrating resource-lifecycle management and energy-efficiency considerations into ML development.

</details>


### [87] [CoRaCommit: A VS Code Extension for Commit Message Generation with Exemplar Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19814)
*Chaoran Cai,Bo Xiong,Chong Wang,Lulu He,Peng Liang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: CoRaCommit是一个VS Code扩展，通过检索相似提交示例作为提示上下文、并行调用多个大语言模型生成候选提交信息、并基于用户反馈动态推荐最佳LLM，显著提升了提交信息生成质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VS Code扩展在生成提交信息时仅直接调用大语言模型基于代码差异生成，未利用相似提交示例作为参考，也缺乏基于用户反馈的LLM推荐机制，导致生成质量不稳定且表达不统一。

Method: 1) 检索相似提交示例作为提示上下文；2) 并行调用多个大语言模型生成候选提交信息并进行对比；3) 基于用户反馈动态推荐最佳大语言模型。

Result: 在ApacheCM数据集的945个提交上，CoRaCommit在BLEU、CIDEr、METEOR和ROUGE-L指标上均优于现有VS Code扩展。

Conclusion: 检索增强的上下文能够有效提升提交信息生成质量，并行多模型调用与用户反馈驱动的动态推荐机制是可行的改进方向。

Abstract: Commit messages are essential textual artifacts that describe the intent behind code changes, and play a critical role in version control, code review, and historical tracking. However, in practice, commit messages are primarily authored manually, which is time-consuming and often results in inconsistent quality and non-uniform expression. Existing VS Code extensions for commit message generation typically directly invoke large language models based on the code diff, without leveraging similar commit exemplars as references, and rarely support user feedback-driven LLM recommendation. To address these limitations, this paper presents CoRaCommit, a VS Code extension that enhances commit message generation by retrieving similar commit exemplars as prompt context, invoking multiple LLMs in parallel for candidate commit message comparison, and dynamically recommending LLMs based on user feedback. Experimental results on 945 commits from the ApacheCM dataset show that CoRaCommit outperforms existing VS Code extensions across BLEU, CIDEr, METEOR, and ROUGE-L metrics, demonstrating the effectiveness of retrieval-augmented context for commit message generation.

</details>


### [88] [JAMER: Project-Level Code Framework Dataset and Benchmark on Professional Game Engines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19830)
*Jianwen Sun,Chuanhao Li,Zizhen Li,Yukang Feng,Fanrui Zhang,Yifei Huang,Yu Dai,Kaipeng Zhang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出了JamSet和JamBench，这是首个基于专业游戏引擎（Godot）的项目级游戏代码框架数据集和基准测试。通过分析超过24万个游戏开发竞赛仓库，筛选出8,133个经过验证的项目，其中300个经过人工验证形成JamBench基准。评估9个前沿模型发现，随着项目规模增大，模型能力出现断崖式下降，运行时通过率从80.4%（小项目）降至5.7%（大项目），而代码智能体虽能提升编译率却无法改善运行时行为质量，表明瓶颈在于架构设计而非语法正确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前AI驱动游戏开发在资产生成、玩法设计和网页游戏编码方面取得了进展，但在专业游戏引擎上的项目级代码工程仍缺乏大规模数据集和确定性评估方法。作者旨在填补这一空白，通过利用Game Jam竞赛中产生的大量开源项目来构建首个专业引擎上的项目级游戏代码数据集和基准。

Method: 基于Godot引擎的文本格式和无头执行模式，设计了一个从文件完整性到运行时行为收集的确定性验证管线，从超过24万个仓库中筛选出8,133个验证项目。其中300个人工验证项目构成JamBench基准，其余构成JamSet数据集。定义了主题驱动生成和代码补全任务，评估管线结合编译通过率、结构完整性评分和行為对齐评分。

Result: 评估9个前沿模型发现能力断崖：运行时通过率从80.4%（小项目）降至5.7%（大项目）。代码智能体提升编译率但未改善运行时行为质量，表明瓶颈在架构设计。实验验证了JamSet作为训练数据的有效性。

Conclusion: 项目级游戏代码工程对当前AI模型仍具极大挑战，尤其是在大规模项目上。当前瓶颈在于架构设计而非语法正确性，需要新的研究方向以突破这一局限。

Abstract: Current AI-driven game development has made substantial progress in asset generation, gameplay design, and web-based game coding, yet project-level code engineering on professional game engines remains largely unexplored due to the absence of large-scale datasets and deterministic evaluation methods. We present JamSet and JamBench, the first project-level game code framework dataset and benchmark built on a professional game engine. Our key insight is that Game Jam competitions, community events where developers build complete games under tight time constraints, yield thousands of open-source projects suitable for this purpose. Building on the Godot engine's text-based format and headless execution mode, we design a deterministic verification pipeline from file integrity to runtime behavior collection, distilling 8,133 verified projects from over 240,000 repositories. Of these, 300 manually verified projects form JamBench; the rest constitute JamSet. JamBench defines theme-driven generation and code completion tasks, evaluated through a pipeline combining compilation pass rates, Structural Completeness Score (SCS), and Behavioral Alignment Score (BAS). Evaluation of 9 frontier models reveals a capability cliff as project scale increases, with runtime pass rates dropping from 80.4% on small projects to 5.7% on large ones (Task2a). Code Agents improve compilation rates yet yield no gains in runtime behavioral quality, indicating that the bottleneck lies in architectural design rather than syntactic correctness. Experiments validate JamSet as effective training data. All data and code are publicly available.

</details>


### [89] [Repository-Level Solidity Code Generation with Large Language Models: From Prompting to Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19988)
*Shi Chen,Rongcun Wang,Yuan Tian,Xiaoyuan Xie,Wei Song,Rubing Huang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出了 SolidityBench（5,470 个仓库级 Solidity 智能合约）和 SolidityScore（面向 Solidity 语义的评估指标），系统评估了多种代码 LLM 在仓库级 Solidity 生成上的表现，发现监督微调是最有效的策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用代码生成 LLM 在专业软件领域（如 Solidity 智能合约）的效果尚未被充分探索，现有基准和指标不足以评估仓库级 Solidity 代码生成，而该领域对语言规范、安全性和软件工程约束要求极高。

Method: 构建了包含 5,470 个仓库级 Solidity 智能合约及其自然语言描述的基准 SolidityBench，并提出了 SolidityScore 这一面向 Solidity 领域关键结构（安全修饰符、合约声明、专有关键词）的语义评估指标。在此基础上评估了 Qwen2.5-Coder、DeepSeek-Coder、CodeLlama 等模型在零样本、思维链、上下文学习、检索增强生成和监督微调五种策略下的表现。

Result: 通用模型在仓库级 Solidity 生成中存在系统性结构缺陷；非参数方法中检索增强生成表现最佳，而上下文学习在超过两个示例后因上下文饱和导致性能下降；监督微调实现了最大幅度的改进。

Conclusion: 高质量的领域数据结合监督微调是提升 LLM 生成智能合约可靠性的最有效策略，本研究为仓库级 Solidity 代码生成提供了全面的基准评估。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have shown strong capabilities in general-purpose code generation, but their effectiveness in specialized software domains remains underexplored. Solidity smart contracts represent a high-stakes domain where generated code must satisfy strict language-level, security, and software-engineering constraints. Existing benchmarks and metrics remain insufficient for repository-level Solidity generation, where models must synthesize complete contracts from natural language requirements. To address this gap, we introduce SolidityBench, a benchmark of 5,470 repository-level Solidity smart contracts paired with natural language descriptions. We also propose SolidityScore, a Solidity-aware semantic metric that emphasizes domain-critical constructs such as security modifiers, contract declarations, and Solidity-specific keywords. Using this benchmark, we evaluate representative code LLMs, including Qwen2.5-Coder, DeepSeek-Coder, and CodeLlama, across zero-shot prompting, Chain-of-Thought reasoning, in-context learning, retrieval-augmented generation, and supervised fine-tuning. The results show that general-purpose models exhibit systematic structural deficiencies in repository-level Solidity generation. Among non-parametric methods, retrieval-augmented generation performs best, while in-context learning degrades beyond two examples due to context saturation. Supervised fine-tuning achieves the largest improvement by internalizing Solidity-specific constraints into model parameters. Overall, our study provides a comprehensive benchmark for repository-level Solidity code generation and shows that high-quality domain data combined with supervised fine-tuning is the most effective strategy for improving the reliability of LLM-generated smart contracts.

</details>


### [90] [Beyond Static Endpoints: Tool Programs as an Interface for Flexible Agentic Web Services](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19992)
*Mugeng Liu,Shuoqi Li,Yixuan Zhang,Yun Ma*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: ToolPro将LLM代理的多步工具调用意图表示为可执行工具程序，通过约束引导的程序构建、效应感知的重放和性能驱动的策略选择，在MCP风格服务上实现了高达53.4%的延迟降低和96.1%的流量减少。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM代理的Web服务接口多为静态端点，难以表达包含循环、条件分支、连接和重试等复杂逻辑的长周期工作流，导致多步服务交互效率低下。

Method: ToolPro包含三个核心组件：1) 约束引导的程序构建，将工具的意图表示为可执行工具程序；2) 效应感知的重放机制，确保状态修改调用仅执行一次；3) 基于profile驱动的策略，动态判断何时程序执行优于逐步调用。

Result: 在真实应用的不同工作流上，ToolPro将端到端延迟降低最高53.4%，客户端流量减少最高96.1%，在网络延迟更高和工作流更复杂时收益更大。

Conclusion: ToolPro通过将工具意图编码为可执行程序，有效解决了静态接口表达力不足的问题，显著提升了LLM代理在复杂工作流场景下的执行效率。

Abstract: In the agentic web era, LLM-based agents increasingly invoke web services as tools, yet most interfaces remain \emph{static endpoints} that poorly express long-horizon workflows with loops, conditionals, joins, and retries. We present ToolPro, which represents an agent's tool intent as an \emph{executable tool program} that compactly encodes multi-step service interactions with explicit effect types. ToolPro combines constraint-guided program construction, effect-aware replay for exactly-once state-modifying calls, and a profile-driven policy that decides when program execution outperforms stepwise calling. We instantiate ToolPro over MCP-style services with WebAssembly sandboxing and evaluate it on diverse workflows of real-world applications. ToolPro reduces end-to-end latency by up to 53.4\% and client-side traffic by up to 96.1\%, with larger gains under higher network latency and workflow complexity.

</details>


### [91] [When Lower Privileges Suffice: Investigating Over-Privileged Tool Selection in LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20023)
*Kaiyue Yang,Yuyan Bu,Jingwei Yi,Yuchi Wang,Biyu Zhou,Juntao Dai,Songlin Hu,Yaodong Yang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文研究LLM智能体在工具选择中的过度特权问题，提出ToolPrivBench评估框架，发现智能体普遍倾向于使用高权限工具（即使在低权限工具足够的情况下），并且这种倾向在瞬态故障下会被放大。作者提出了一种特权感知的后训练防御方法，能有效减少不必要的高权限工具使用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工具选择研究仅关注安全无关的元数据偏好，忽视了涉及不同权限级别的工具选择安全风险。智能体自主选择高权限工具可能带来安全隐患，而这一问题尚未被充分研究。

Method: 提出ToolPrivBench评估框架，涵盖8个领域和5种重复风险模式，测量智能体的初始工具选择以及在瞬态故障后的升级行为。同时提出一种特权感知的后训练防御方法。

Result: 主流LLM智能体中普遍存在过度特权工具选择现象，瞬态故障会进一步加剧这一问题。通用安全对齐无法可靠迁移到最小特权工具选择，提示级别的控制措施在瞬态故障下效果有限。所提出的后训练防御方法能大幅减少不必要的高权限工具使用，同时保持通用能力。

Conclusion: 过度特权工具选择是LLM智能体普遍存在的安全隐患，需要专门的特权感知防御策略来解决，而非依赖通用安全对齐或简单提示控制。

Abstract: As LLM agents increasingly select tools autonomously, their choices among tools with different privileges become safety-relevant. However, prior tool-selection studies focus on safety-agnostic metadata preferences, leaving privilege-sensitive choices underexplored. To address this gap, we study over-privileged tool selection, in which an agent selects or escalates to a higher-privilege tool despite a sufficient lower-privilege alternative. We introduce ToolPrivBench to evaluate whether agents choose higher-privilege tools despite sufficient lower-privilege alternatives, measuring both initial selection and escalation after transient tool failures. Across eight domains and five recurring risk patterns, we find that over-privileged tool selection is common among mainstream LLM agents and is further amplified by transient failures. We further find that general safety alignment does not reliably transfer to least-privilege tool choice, while prompt-level controls provide only limited mitigation under transient failures. We therefore introduce a privilege-aware post-training defense that teaches agents to prefer sufficient lower-privilege tools and escalate only when necessary. Our mitigation experiments show that this defense substantially reduces unnecessary high-privilege tool use while preserving general capabilities.

</details>


### [92] [The Correctness Illusion in LLM-Generated GPU Kernels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20128)
*Dipankar Sarkar*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该论文指出现有LLM生成GPU内核基准测试（如KernelBench、TritonBench、GEAK）通过固定形状、小样本的allclose-style检查来评估正确性，存在局限性。作者构建了24个Triton和CPU替代内核（15个正确控制、9个LLM风格错误变体），并采用基于操作模式感知的种子化模糊测试、高精度fp64 CPU参考和逐操作/逐数据类型的绝对容差进行重新评估。该种子化Oracle正确识别所有9个错误内核和15个正确控制，精度零损失。随后扩展到26个操作（含flash-attention对），在5种GPU（RTX 3060、A10、L40S、A100 SXM4、H100 NVL）上验证，结果一致：10/10幻觉捕获，16/16控制正常。所有失败均可从存储种子逐字节重放。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM生成GPU内核的基准测试依赖固定形状、小样本的allclose-style检查来判定正确性，这种评估方法可能遗漏LLM常见的转录错误（如幻觉错误），无法可靠检测出内核中的bug。

Method: 构建24个受控内核语料库（15正确+9错误），采用操作模式感知的种子化模糊测试，使用高精度fp64 CPU参考作为基准，并设置逐操作/逐数据类型的绝对容差。之后扩展至26个操作，在5种不同GPU上重复验证。

Result: 种子化Oracle在初始24个内核上准确识别9/9错误和15/15正确，零精度损失。在5种GPU上扩展至26个操作后，10/10幻觉被捕获，16/16控制通过，所有GPU判定结果完全一致。

Conclusion: 现有固定形状的allclose-style检查可能被LLM生成的buggy内核欺骗。基于操作模式感知的种子化模糊测试结合高精度参考和动态容差，能更可靠地检测LLM转录错误，且结果在不同GPU间具有可复制性。

Abstract: Benchmarks for LLM-generated GPU kernels (KernelBench, TritonBench, GEAK) score correctness through fixed-shape, small-sample allclose-style checks. The number of inputs varies between benchmarks. The shape, dtype, and tolerance are fixed for each kernel. We test that oracle empirically. We construct a controlled corpus of 24 Triton and CPU stand-in kernels (15 correct controls and 9 LLM-style buggy variants seeded with documented transcription errors) and re-evaluate it under op-schema-aware seeded fuzzing with a high-precision (fp64) CPU reference and per-(op, dtype) absolute tolerances. The seeded oracle flags 9 of 9 buggy kernels and passes 15 of 15 correct controls, at zero precision cost on controls. We extend the corpus to 26 ops (adding a flash-attention pair) and re-run the same protocol on five GPU classes (RTX 3060, A10, L40S, A100 SXM4, H100 NVL). The verdicts are identical across all five GPUs: 10 of 10 illusions caught and 16 of 16 controls clean. The corpus result is about LLM-style transcription bugs that the allclose-on-one-shape oracle certifies as correct, not about the bug rate of any specific deployed LLM. Every flagged failure replays byte-for-byte from a stored seed.

</details>


### [93] [Learning Critical Testing Literacy Through Puzzles: an Experience Report](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20129)
*Niels Doorn,Bart Th. Knaack,Tanja E. J. Vos,Beatriz Marín*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文报告了使用谜题学习关键测试素养（CTL）的13场工作坊经验，提出了P4TEST教学框架，开发了开源网络应用，并发现谜题本身并非干预手段，完整的“解决-复盘-反思”序列才是关键。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软件测试重要但难教，现有教学方法缺乏针对批判性测试思维的系统性培养手段。

Method: 基于关键测试者认知模型构建了P4TEST教学框架和谜题知识库，在13场工作坊（11场半结构化+2场引入工作簿和出声思考）中对学生、测试人员、教师和小学生进行评估。

Result: 参与者普遍在解谜中感知到实验性行为；学生趋向收敛于答案，专业人员持续探索；情绪可见但难以书面化；出声思考揭示即时推理，书面反思引发元认知；Sense-making/反思性行动是核心主题。

Conclusion: 谜题本身不是教学干预的全部，完整的“解决-复盘-反思”序列才是关键；未来需更刻意地设计该序列；已开发开源网络应用以支持工作坊定制。

Abstract: In this paper, we report our experiences and takeaways from workshops using puzzles to learn CTL.
  Background: Software testing is important yet difficult to teach. We introduced a BoK of puzzle-based learning activities to teach CTL, based on a model of critical tester's cognition, leading to the pedagogical framework P4TEST. We conducted thirteen workshops with students, testers, teachers, and primary school pupils to assess puzzle-based teaching of critical testing literacy.
  Experience: Across eleven workshops, we used a semi-structured approach, varying puzzles, materials, and timing. In two additional workshops, we introduced workbooks and think-aloud sessions to gather more data on the learning experience.
  Observations: Participants consistently perceived themselves as experimenting while solving puzzles. Students tended to converge on solutions, while professionals continued exploring. Emotions were visible in behaviour but hard to surface through written reflection alone. Think-aloud sessions revealed immediate reasoning; written reflections elicited more meta-cognitive reflection. The theme Sensemaking / reflection-in-action captured how participants framed problems, navigated dead ends, and shifted strategies.
  Reflections: Puzzles are not the intervention: the entire sequence of solving, debriefing, and reflecting is. Designing that sequence more deliberately is the work ahead. We also developed an open-source web application with built-in analytics to customise workshops.

</details>


### [94] [N-Version Programming with Coding Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20158)
*Javier Ron,Benoit Baudry,Martin Monperrus*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文在当代AI编码代理的背景下重新审视了N版本编程的经典概念。通过Knight-Leveson实验的复现，研究评估了48个由代理生成的导弹拦截程序实现，发现存在显著的共模故障，但多数据胜策略（三版本单元）使平均故障数从387.44降至130.99，且11,844个N版本单元零故障，证明N版本编程结合编码代理是一种有效工程策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 验证在当代AI编码代理（如LLM驱动的代码生成）的背景下，N版本编程的经典假设（通过多样性实现容错）是否仍然成立，特别是评估不同代理系统、模型和编程语言是否能产生多样化的故障模式。

Method: 基于Knight-Leveson实验的Launch Interceptor Program Specification，使用多种AI编码代理生成48个实现，在共享oracle和100万个随机测试输入上进行评估，分析共模故障并通过多数投票构建三版本单元进行多数据胜评估。

Result: 发现了显著的共模故障（与Knight-Leveson原始发现一致），多数投票三版本单元平均故障数从单版本的387.44降至130.99，11,844个N版本单元实现零故障。许多共现故障可追溯到规范中特别困难或模糊的部分。

Conclusion: 提供了迄今为止最强有力的证据，证明在AI编码代理时代，N版本编程仍然是一种有用的工程策略，能够通过多样性有效降低故障率。

Abstract: This paper revisits the classical concept on N-version programming in the setting of contemporary AI coding agents. Revisiting the seminal Knight-Leveson experiment, we study whether diversity across agent systems, models, and implementation languages creates diverse failure modes. Using the Knight-Leveson's, Launch Interceptor Program Specification, we evaluate 48 agent-generated implementations on a shared oracle and a campaign of 1,000,000 randomized test inputs. The results show substantial common-mode failure, along the findings of Knight-Leveson. Further analysis that many of those co-occuring failures can be traced to where is specification is particularly hard or ambiguous. We also demonstrate that diversity from coding agents provides practical benefit: across majority voting three-version units, the mean failure count drops from 387.44 for single versions to 130.99 for triples, and 11,844 N-version units exhibit zero observed failures. Our original results is the strongest evidence to date that N-Version Programming with coding agents is a useful engineering strategy.

</details>


### [95] [Qiskit Code Migration with LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20173)
*Jose Manuel Suarez,Luis Mariano Bibbo,Joaquin Bogado,Alenandro Fernandez*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结合LLM与检索增强生成（RAG）的混合方法，利用自动生成的迁移场景分类体系作为结构化知识源，实现Qiskit代码跨版本自动迁移。实验表明，基于分类体系的RAG架构（尤其是限制性检索方案）能显著减少幻觉、提升描述质量，其中Google Gemini Flash-2.5在检测复杂重构场景中表现最优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子开发套件（QDK）快速演进带来的技术债务严重影响了代码可维护性和软件复用；量子软件工程（QSE）领域高质量训练数据稀缺、框架高度易变，导致通用大语言模型（LLM）产生不可靠或幻觉结果。

Method: 提出LLM与RAG结合的混合方法；自动生成迁移场景分类体系（taxonomy）作为结构化、版本特定的知识源；通过自动化可扩展工作流评估不同检索方案（无约束vs限制性）下的LLM（Google Gemini Flash-2.5和OpenAI Gpt-oss-20b）。

Result: 基于分类体系的RAG架构（尤其限制性方案）显著减少幻觉、提升描述质量；Google Gemini Flash-2.5在检测复杂重构场景中表现优于对比模型。

Conclusion: 该数据驱动的方法能够促进技术独立性，提供缓解API过时问题的智能助手，确保量子算法在快速演进的生态系统中的长期可用性，并降低量子软件工程的学习曲线。

Abstract: The rapid evolution of Quantum Development Kits (QDKs) introduces a specific form of technical debt that compromises code maintainability and hinders software reuse. In the specialized domain of Quantum Software Engineering (QSE), this challenge is intensified by the scarcity of high-quality training data and the high volatility of emerging frameworks, which often lead general-purpose Large Language Models (LLMs) to produce unreliable or hallucinated results. This paper proposes a hybrid approach integrating LLMs with Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) to automate the migration of Qiskit code across versions. The proposed methodology enhances the precision and reliability of migration suggestions by leveraging an automatically generated taxonomy of migration scenarios as the structured, version-specific knowledge source to guide the models. The approach is implemented through an automated, extensible workflow evaluating LLMs (Google Gemini Flash-2.5 and OpenAI Gpt-oss-20b) under different retrieval schemes (unconstrained and restrictive). Results demonstrate that the taxonomy-based RAG architecture, particularly under the restrictive scheme, significantly reduces hallucinations and improves descriptive quality, with Google Gemini Flash-2.5 showing superior performance in detecting complex refactoring scenarios. These findings confirm the potential of this data-centric methodology to foster technological independence and provide robust, intelligent assistants that mitigate API obsolescence, ensuring the long-term availability of quantum algorithms within a rapidly shifting ecosystem and flattening the learning curve within Quantum Software Engineering (QSE).

</details>


### [96] [SysML Modeling of Digital Twins for Renewable Energy Communities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20230)
*Mohammad Samadi,Luís Miguel Pinho,Andrey Sadovykh,Gabriela Lucas*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于模型系统工程（MBSE）的工作流，用于构建可再生能源社区（REC）的数字孪生系统，通过SysML建模并引入SAREF4ENER本体来弥合语义鸿沟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: REC数字孪生的构建因设备、合约和运行时数据的异构性而面临困难，现有方法缺乏系统性的工程化工作流。

Method: 从工业验证的REC领域模型出发，使用开源工具Modelio在SysML中重新表达代表性房屋子集，生成设备分类和社区组织视图的两个模块定义图，并讨论SysML存在的四个语义鸿沟，提出引入SAREF4ENER本体作为参考包来弥补。

Result: 生成了SysML表示的设备分类图和社区组织视图，识别了SysML在REC数字孪生建模中的四个语义鸿沟。

Conclusion: SysML与基于SAREF的智能能源数字孪生语义相结合仍是未充分探索的领域，本文作为该方向的第一步工作。

Abstract: Renewable Energy Communities (RECs) are emerging as a key organizational model for local and global sharing of renewable generation, storage, and flexible loads. Engineering Digital Twins of RECs is made difficult by the heterogeneity of devices, contracts, and runtime data involved. In this paper, we take a first step toward a Model-Based Systems Engineering (MBSE) workflow for REC's Digital Twins. Starting from an industrially-validated REC domain model, we re-express a representative house subset in SysML using the open-source Modelio tool, yielding two Block Definition Diagrams - a device taxonomy and a community organizational view. We then discuss four semantic gaps that plain SysML leaves open and sketch how the SAREF4ENER ontology could be imported as a reference package to close them. Combining SysML with SAREF-based semantics for smart-energy Digital Twins remains largely unexplored, and we position this paper as a first step along that line.

</details>


### [97] [Phoenix: Safe GitHub Issue Resolution via Multi-Agent LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20243)
*Kipngeno Koech,Muhammad Adam,Baimam Boukar Jean Jacques,Joao Barros*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: Phoenix 是一个多智能体LLM系统，通过七个安全层和基线测试策略，在6个专业代理协作下自动化GitHub问题处理（从分类到PR创建）。在24例SWE-bench Lite子集上达到75% oracle解决率，42个真实问题中实现100%正确性保持，但约半数PR存在代码路径定位错误。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动化解决GitHub issue的完整流程（从分类到PR创建）需要处理代码定位准确性、安全性及回归问题，现有系统缺乏系统化的安全机制和可靠的评估策略。

Method: 构建包含6个专业代理（Planner、Reproducer、Coder、Tester、Failure Analyst、PR Agent）的多智能体系统，由基于标签的GitHub webhook状态机协调，结合7层安全控制和基线测试评估策略。

Result: 在SWE-bench Lite 24例子集上达到75% oracle解决率且无回归；42个真实问题中100%正确性保持（硬层级平均122秒）；约半数PR修复定位正确，其余存在代码路径错误。

Conclusion: 多智能体LLM系统结合安全机制可有效自动化issue处理，但Planner的代码定位能力是主要瓶颈，需通过检索增强来改进。

Abstract: We present Phoenix, a multi-agent LLM system that resolves GitHub issues from triage through pull-request creation, combining seven layered safety controls with a baseline-aware test evaluation strategy. Phoenix decomposes the work across six specialized agents. Planner, reproducer, coder, tester, failure analyst and Pull Request (PR) agent, all coordinated by a label-based GitHub webhook state machine. Every change is checked against a baseline test run before a pull request is opened. On a 24-instance slice of SWE-bench Lite. run on the production webhook path, Phoenix oracle-resolves 75% of instances with no pass-to-pass regressions on successful runs; this curated slice is not directly comparable to full-split leaderboard results, and we discuss the limits of the comparison. A complementary pilot on 42 real issues across 14 repositories yields 100% correctness preservation (CP; mean 122s on the hard tier). Manual inspection shows that about half of the resulting pull requests are well-targeted fixes. The other half place code at incorrect paths, a planner localization limitation we are addressing with retrieval. We also report the deployment failure modes (WAF filtering, token expiry, permission boundaries, flaky CI) that motivated each safety mechanism.

</details>


### [98] [Token-Operations-Oriented Inference Optimization Techniques for Large Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20295)
*Shiguo Lian,Kai Wang,Zhaoxiang Liu,Wen Liu,Minjie Hua,Yutong Liu,Jiangze Yan,Xin Wang,Cong Wang,Yilin Zhang,Yi Shen,Jieyun Huang,Fang Zhao,Huanlin Gao,Ping Chen,Xinyu Yang,Kaikai Zhao,Yao Zhao,Xinggang Wang,Huishuai Zhang,Dongyan Zhao,Junping Du,Tao Chen,Xiang Gao,Qinghuai Ma*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文围绕以Token为导向的大模型推理优化技术，首次提出四层技术架构（多模型融合、模型优化、算-模融合、算-网-模融合），系统综述了各层关键技术及产业现状，分析了实际业务场景中的应用价值，为降低Token生产成本、提升服务效率、保障供应稳定、推动大模型服务从"可用"到"好运营"提供了技术路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大模型推理优化是支撑大模型服务规模化、低成本、高稳定运行的关键基础，当前亟需系统性的技术架构来指导Token生产成本的降低和Token服务效率的提升。

Method: 首次提出了四层技术架构：多模型融合、模型优化、算-模融合、算-网-模融合，并基于此架构对各层关键技术及产业现状进行系统综述与分析。

Result: 系统梳理了四层技术架构下的关键技术和行业应用现状，分析了相关技术在真实业务场景中的应用价值。

Conclusion: 本文为降低Token生产成本、提高Token服务效率、保障Token供应稳定性、推动大模型服务从"可调用"向"可运营"转变提供了切实可行的技术路径。

Abstract: Large model inference optimization serves as a key foundation for supporting the scalable, low-cost, and highly stable operation of large model services. Centered on token-oriented inference optimization technology, this paper proposes for the first time a four-layer technical architecture consisting of Multi-model Fusion, Model Optimization, Compute-Model Fusion, and Compute-Network-Model Fusion. It systematically reviews the key technologies and current industry status across these four levels and analyzes the application value of related technologies in real-world business scenarios. This paper provides a practical technical path for reducing token production costs, improving token service efficiency, ensuring the stability of token supply, and driving the transition of large model services from being merely callable to being operable.

</details>


### [99] [A Model-Driven Approach for Developing Families of Reinforcement Learning Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20324)
*Xiaoran Liu,Istvan David*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种模型驱动的方法，通过混合遗传算法和模型变换技术，自动生成强化学习训练所需的环境变体家族，以替代传统手动构建的繁琐过程，并在野火缓解场景和课程学习范式中验证了方法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前大多数强化学习问题需要在一组相似但略有不同的环境变体家族中进行训练才能收敛，但环境家族的开发过程是劳动密集、易出错且难以扩展的手工工作，亟需一种自动化、可扩展的方法来缓解这些问题。

Method: 提出一种模型驱动的方法：采用混合遗传算法（结合基于种群的全局搜索与启发式局部搜索）生成环境家族；将变异和约束表达为模型变换，并由先进的模型变换引擎将其操作化纳入搜索过程；同时开发了原型工具支持该方法。

Result: 在野火缓解场景和课程学习范式下进行了实验验证，证明了方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 所提出的模型驱动方法能够自动化生成RL训练所需的环境变体家族，减少了手动工作量，提升了可扩展性，且在特定场景中表现良好，可用于支持课程学习等依赖环境家族的学习范式。

Abstract: Virtual training environments are software-intensive systems in which reinforcement learning (RL) agents learn, adapt, and demonstrate meaningful behavior. Virtual training environments offer a safe and cost-efficient alternative to training agents in real-world settings. However, to converge, most realistic RL problems require training in multiple, mostly similar but slightly different environments - i.e., families of environment variants. The typical development process of environment families is a labor-intensive and error-prone manual endeavor that does not scale well. To alleviate these issues, in this paper, we propose a model-driven approach for developing families of RL training environments. To obtain the family of environments, we develop an approach and prototype tool. In our approach, a hybrid genetic algorithm - a combination of population-based global search and heuristic local search - generates environment families. Mutations and constraints are expressed as model transformations and are operationalized into a search process by a state-of-the-art model transformation engine. We demonstrate the soundness of our approach in a wildfire mitigation scenario and curriculum learning - a particular learning paradigm that relies on environment families.

</details>


### [100] [AutoPass: Evidence-Guided LLM Agents for Compiler Performance Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20373)
*Zepeng Li,Jie Ren,Zhanyong Tang,Jie Zheng,Zheng Wang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: AutoPass是一个多智能体框架，通过向LLM开放编译器内部状态（如中间表示和优化状态），并结合运行时反馈迭代优化编译配置，在无需训练/微调的情况下，在x86-64和ARM64平台上分别实现了比LLVM -O3高1.043倍和1.117倍的几何平均加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在代码编译任务中展现出潜力，但将其应用于运行时性能调优面临两大挑战：复杂的微架构效应和噪声运行时测量。现有的自动调优方案将编译器视为黑盒，无法利用编译器内部的优化信息，导致搜索效率低下。

Method: AutoPass是一个多智能体框架：(1) 向LLM开放编译器内部状态，允许查询编译器优化状态和分析中间表示；(2) 使用编译器和运行时证据联合指导LLM生成优化决策；(3) 通过迭代搜索过程，利用实测运行时反馈诊断性能回归并引导延迟改进编辑；(4) 纯推理模式，无需离线训练或任务特定微调。

Result: 在LLVM编译器上实现，并在服务器级x86-64和嵌入式ARM64系统上评估。AutoPass优于专家调整的启发式方法和经典自动调优方法，在x86-64上实现1.043x几何平均加速，在ARM64上实现1.117x几何平均加速（相对于LLVM -O3）。

Conclusion: AutoPass通过将编译器内部信息开放给LLM，结合运行时反馈迭代优化，在无需额外训练的条件下显著提升了编译器性能调优效果，且易于迁移到新的基准测试和平台。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) show promise for code compilation tasks, but applying them to runtime performance tuning is difficult due to complex microarchitectural effects and noisy runtime measurements. We present AutoPass, a multi-agent framework for compiler performance tuning that uses compiler and runtime evidence to guide LLM-generated optimization decisions. Rather than treating the compiler as a black box like prior auto-tuning schemes, AutoPass opens up the compiler to the LLM, enabling it to query compiler-internal optimization states and analyze the intermediate representation to orchestrate compiler options. The search process iteratively refines optimization configurations using measured runtime feedback to diagnose regressions and guide latency-improving edits. AutoPass operates in an inference-only, training-free setting and requires no offline training or task-specific fine-tuning, making it readily applicable to new benchmarks and platforms. We implement AutoPass on the LLVM compiler and evaluate it on server-grade x86-64 and embedded ARM64 systems. AutoPass outperforms expert-tuned heuristics and classical autotuning methods, achieving geometric-mean speedups of 1.043x and 1.117x over LLVM -O3 on x86-64 and ARM64, respectively.

</details>


### [101] [Probe-and-Refine Tuning of Repository Guidance for Coding Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20512)
*Asa Shepard,Jeannie Albrecht*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出"探测-精调"（probe-and-refine tuning）方法，通过合成bug修复探针来迭代诊断和修补仓库的指导文件（AGENTS.md），无需代理循环或工具调用。在SWE-bench Verified上使用Qwen3.5-35B-A3B在200步下，该方法达到33.0%的平均解决率，优于静态知识库的28.3%和无基线的25.5%（p<0.001），提升主要来自覆盖率而非精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM编码代理需要仓库级别的高阶操作知识（如文件子系统划分、测试套件运行方法、历史错误修复工作流），这些知识不存在于代码本身。工程师通常维护AGENTS.md文件来提供上下文指导，但关于LLM生成的指导是否有效，现有研究结论相互矛盾，因此需要明确指导生成方式如何影响代理性能。

Method: 提出probe-and-refine tuning流程：使用合成bug修复探针，通过单次LLM调用迭代诊断和修补仓库的指导文件，在调优过程中不涉及代理循环或工具使用。具体通过让LLM生成对合成bug的修复策略，分析其输出质量来诊断指导文件的不足，然后自动更新指导文件。

Result: 在SWE-bench Verified上，使用Qwen3.5-35B-A3B在200步下，probe-and-refine达到33.0%的平均解决率，对比静态知识库28.3%和无基线25.5%（p<0.001）。提升来自覆盖率：精调后的指导使14.5个百分点更多的实例产生可评估补丁，而每补丁精度统计上恒定（~59%，p=0.119）。步数预算实验表明指导让代理能有效利用更大步数预算；跨模型实验（NVIDIA-Nemotron-3-Nano-30B-A3B）显示当模型无法生成足够诊断性输出时，调优循环性能下降，但每补丁精度仍保持恒定。

Conclusion: 指导文件的生成方式是影响LLM编码代理性能的决定性变量。Probe-and-refine tuning能有效提升代理性能，其机制是通过扩大覆盖率（帮助代理找到正确文件）而非提升补丁质量。该方法在模型具备足够的诊断性输出能力时效果最佳。

Abstract: LLM-based coding agents need higher-level operational knowledge about a repository (which files house which subsystems, how to run the test suite, which workflows have historically led to wrong fixes) that does not exist in the code itself. Engineers typically maintain \texttt{AGENTS.md} files to supply this context as instructions for coding agents, but whether they help is contested: recent studies disagree on whether LLM-generated guidance improves or harms agent performance. In this paper we show that how the guidance is produced is the decisive variable, and introduce \emph{probe-and-refine tuning}: a procedure that uses synthetic bug-fix probes to iteratively diagnose and patch a repository's guidance file through single-shot LLM calls, with no agent loop or tool use during tuning. On SWE-bench Verified across four independent trials with Qwen3.5-35B-A3B at 200 steps, probe-and-refine achieves 33.0\,\% mean resolve rate vs.\ 28.3\,\% for the static knowledge base used to initialize it and 25.5\,\% for an unguided baseline ($p < 0.001$ for both probe-and-refine contrasts). The improvement comes from coverage rather than precision: refined guidance produces evaluable patches for 14.5 percentage points (pp) more instances while per-patch precision remains statistically constant ($\sim$59\,\%, $p = 0.119$), showing that improved guidance helps agents reach the correct file rather than improving the quality of the changes they make. Further, a step-budget experiment shows that guidance is what lets the agent use a larger step budget productively, and a cross-model experiment with NVIDIA-Nemotron-3-Nano-30B-A3B finds that the tuning loop degrades when the model cannot generate sufficiently diagnostic output, though per-patch precision remains constant even then.

</details>


<div id='cs.HC'></div>

# cs.HC [[Back]](#toc)

### [102] [LLM-Mediated Human-AI Interaction in Search and Rescue: Impact of Expertise on Attentional Allocation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19514)
*Elahe Oveisi,Hemanth Manjunatha*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本研究在模拟搜索救援环境中，对比了有无LLM（大语言模型）辅助条件下的人机协作表现。结果显示，LLM辅助提高了任务效率（每步收益和受害者数量），但并未增加总拯救人数。眼动追踪揭示了注意力-引导权衡：视觉资源向聊天界面转移，瞳孔大小变异性增加。专业技能调节了这种效应：新手表现出被动依赖，而专家则维持"验证循环"，持续扫描环境。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究AI辅助系统（特别是LLM）在复杂任务中如何影响人类认知和行为，以及这种影响如何在不同专业水平的人之间变化，旨在理解人机协作中AI引导的有效性机制。

Method: 在模拟搜索救援环境中，设置两种LLM引导条件（实验组）和无LLM基线条件（对照组），通过多层面分析（任务绩效、眼动追踪、规划行为）比较人类表现。眼动追踪提供注意力分配和与AI引导交互的细粒度洞察，行为指标捕捉用户决策结构随时间的变化。

Result: LLM引导提高了任务效率（每步收益和每步拯救受害者数），但未增加总拯救人数。眼动数据揭示了注意力-引导权衡，视觉资源转移到聊天界面，瞳孔大小变异性增加。新手表现出被动AI依赖，专家则维持"验证循环"，持续扫描环境以验证AI建议。

Conclusion: LLM介导的人机团队效能取决于操作者交叉验证AI建议与地面实况的能力，以维持情境意识。不同专业水平的用户对AI辅助的反应模式存在本质差异，专家更能保持主动监控和验证。

Abstract: Human-AI teaming (HAT) increasingly involves AI systems that provide real-time, context-aware guidance in complex tasks. While such systems can improve performance, their effectiveness depends on how they shape human cognition and behavior. In particular, AI assistance can introduce cognitive demands and influence attention, planning, and interaction with the task environment, with effects that can vary across levels of expertise. This work investigates these mechanisms in a simulated search and rescue (SAR) environment. We compare human performance under two LLM (Large Language Model)-guided conditions and a no-LLM baseline, and analyze interaction at multiple levels, including task performance, eye-tracking measures, and planning behavior. Eye tracking provides fine-grained insight into attention allocation and interaction with AI guidance, while behavioral measures capture how users structure and adapt their decisions over time. Results indicate that LLM guidance enhanced task efficiency (higher rewards and victims-per-step) but did not increase total victims saved. Eye-tracking data revealed an attention-guidance trade-off, with visual resources shifting to the chat interface alongside increased pupil size variability. Expertise moderated this effect: novices exhibited passive AI reliance, whereas experts maintained a "verification loop" through persistent environmental scanning. These findings suggest that LLM-mediated teaming efficacy depends on the operator's ability to cross-reference AI guidance with ground truth to maintain situational awareness.

</details>


### [103] [Code as Anchor, Memory and Metaphor as Support: Learner Experiences with Multi-View Visualizations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19570)
*Naaz Sibia,Jessica Wen,Amber Richardson,Yashika Jain,Khushi Malik,Bogdan Simion,Carolina Nobre,Angela Zavaleta Bernuy,Andrew Petersen,Michael Liut*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 尽管程序可视化工具在认知设计上合理，但新手程序员常忽略它们。本研究发现学生近一半时间聚焦代码，缺乏可视化经验者更甚，选择性参与受制于**能动性**、**表征适配**和**正当性**三大因素。建议将可视化定位为验证工具、提供可切换的抽象层级、并赋予工具学科正当性信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究从认知角度提出了多外部表征（MERs）的设计原则，但关于学习者为何会忽略或拒绝精心设计的可视化支架的深层原因知之甚少。本研究旨在探究塑造学习者与可用表征之间选择性参与的因素。

Method: 对19名完成CS1和CS2的本科生进行**被试内设计**，结合**出声思维任务**、**反思性访谈**和**基于网络摄像头的眼动追踪**，使用包含同步代码、内存和隐喻视图的多表征探测工具以及Python Tutor，涵盖作用域、while循环和链表主题。

Result: 眼动分析显示学生近一半时间聚焦代码，缺乏经验者更锚定代码、极少参与隐喻视图。访谈识别出三个因素：**能动性**（学生寻求对认知努力的控制而非简单降低）、**表征适配**（相同设计在不同情境下可能感觉有帮助或过度负荷）、**正当性**（部分学生认为隐喻支架过于幼稚，缺乏大学水平的学科严肃性）。

Conclusion: 多表征编程教育工具需在认知设计之外关注**情感和社会因素**。实践建议包括：将可视化定位为验证工具、提供可切换抽象层级、并通过工具设计传递学科正当性信号。这解释了为何认知上合理的可视化工具仍可能无法吸引目标学生群体。

Abstract: Program visualizations are widely used to support novice programmers, yet students often ignore or resist well-designed visual scaffolds. Research on multiple external representations (MERs) offers cognitive design principles for coordinating views, but less is known about what shapes learners' engagement with available representations.
  We conducted a within-subjects study with 19 undergraduates who had completed CS1 and CS2. Students completed think-aloud tasks, reflective interviews, and webcam-based gaze tracking while using a multi-representational probe with synchronized code, memory, and metaphor views, and Python Tutor, across scope, while loops, and linked lists.
  Gaze analysis showed that students spent nearly half their time focused on code despite available visual scaffolds. Students without prior experience anchored even more heavily in code and engaged minimally with metaphor views. Interviews identified three factors shaping selective engagement: agency, as students sought control over cognitive effort rather than simply having it reduced; representational fit, as identical designs differed in whether they felt helpful or overwhelming; and legitimacy, as some students avoided metaphorical scaffolds they perceived as childish or insufficiently rigorous for university-level work.
  These findings suggest that multi-representational tools in computing education require attention to affective and social factors alongside cognitive design. Practical considerations include positioning visualizations as verification instruments, offering toggleable abstraction levels, and framing tools to signal disciplinary legitimacy. More broadly, the themes help explain why cognitively sound visualization tools may fail to engage the students they are designed to help.

</details>


### [104] [Building Drift: Documenting On-Site Construction Adaptations Across Material Lifecycles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19609)
*Ritik Batra,Martin Tamke,Tom Svilans,Jan Hüls,Amritansh Kwatra,Steven J. Jackson,Thijs Roumen,Mette Ramsgaard Thomsen*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文提出"建筑漂移"（Building Drift）概念，用于描述使用回收材料的建筑在其生命周期中物理状态与数字模型的集体偏差。通过ReShelter案例研究，建立了包含五个类别的漂移分类法，并开发了Pentimento文档工具，利用视频和3D高斯泼溅技术时空语义化记录现场适应过程，以支持可持续的建筑材料循环利用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在循环经济背景下，回收材料的使用带来不可预测性，需要现场即兴调整，但缺乏有效的文档记录方式，导致后续合作者、评估者和继承者无法获取关键信息来继续、评估和再利用这些材料。现有数字工具难以捕捉跨生命周期中的现场适应与物理-数字偏差。

Method: 通过ReShelter（一个在森林中建造的回收木材亭）的案例研究，归纳出建筑漂移的分类法（五个类别：照料场地、寻找适配、解读材料、标记测量、跨社区协调）。在此基础上开发Pentimento工具，融合视频文档与3D高斯泼溅技术，在空间、时间和语义三个维度上将现场适应与设计模型相关联。

Result: 建立了涵盖建筑漂移五个维度的分类体系，并实现了Pentimento工具，使各利益相关方能够以降低材料再利用障碍的方式导航材料历史。

Conclusion: 该研究为支持回收材料建筑中不可或缺的现场即兴操作提供了计算工具路径，从而推动更可持续的回收、修复和再利用循环。

Abstract: In a circular economy for construction, reclaimed materials carry prior lives of use and go on to have post-lives in future buildings. Yet working with such materials introduces unpredictability that requires on-site improvisation, making their reuse challenging to document and scale across building lifetimes. Without documentation, the on-site adaptations that make construction with reclaimed materials possible leave collaborators, evaluators, and inheritors without the information they need to continue, assess, and reuse materials. We call the collective deviation of the physical state from the digital model through these adaptations "building drift." Through a case study, ReShelter, a reclaimed timber pavilion constructed in the forest, we develop a taxonomy for building drift that characterizes the collective deviation across building lifetimes: Tending the Site, Foraging for Fit, Interpreting the Material, Marking Measurements, and Coordinating Across Communities.
  To put our taxonomy for building drift into practice, we present Pentimento, a documentation tool that leverages video documentation and 3D Gaussian Splatting to spatially, temporally, and semantically represent on-site adaptations in relation to the designed model. Pentimento enables each stakeholder to navigate material histories in ways that reduce barriers to material reuse. Together, these contributions open pathways towards computational tools that support the on-site improvisation essential to construction with reclaimed materials, enabling more sustainable cycles of recovery, repair, and reuse.

</details>


### [105] [Syndesmoscope: The Power of Invariant Plots\\Linked to Traditional Network Views](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19689)
*Matt Oddo,Indira Sowy,Stephen Kobourov,Tamara Munzner*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文提出Syndesmoscope系统，通过并置力导向图、密集-稀疏梯度、测地离心率和谱二分等不变性可视化视图，并引入基于密度分解的kSnakes不变图，结合跳跃式高亮和跳房子式遍历交互，实现单一视图无法揭示的网络模式探索。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统网络表示（节点-链接图、邻接矩阵）的视觉模式严重依赖布局或排序算法，导致同一拓扑可能呈现不同模式；而不变性图（invariant plots）能为同一拓扑一致地呈现相同模式，但研究者对其探索不足，也未将其集成到可视化系统中。

Method: 构建Syndesmoscope交互系统，并置四个视图：力导向布局、密集-稀疏梯度（基于图论属性）、测地离心率、谱二分；并引入新不变图kSnakes（基于密度分解）。支持两种交互：leapfrogging（跨视图联动高亮）和hopscotching（基于跳数的拓扑遍历扩展选择）。

Result: 通过对72个多样网络的用例验证，展示了这些交互如何揭示单一视图无法访问的网络模式。系统已部署为在线Demo。

Conclusion: 不变性图与多视图联动交互能有效揭示深层网络结构模式，为网络可视化提供新范式。

Abstract: Traditional network representations, such as node-link views and adjacency matrices, can show dramatically different visual patterns, depending on the underlying layout or seriation algorithm. In contrast, invariant plots consistently surface the same visual pattern for the same input topology; yet researchers have underexplored them and have not integrated them into visualization systems. We present Syndesmoscope, an interactive system for network exploration that juxtaposes multiple views of the same network. Panes show a familiar a force-directed view alongside three panes with interpretable geometric layouts based on graph-theoretic properties: dense-sparse gradient, geodesic eccentricity, and spectral bisection. As a secondary contribution, we introduce kSnakes, a new invariant plot based on density decomposition. Syndesmoscope supports two key interactions: leapfrogging, or linked highlighting between different and interpretable visual patterns; and hopscotching, or hop-based traversal that extends data selections through the underlying topology. Through usage scenarios across a corpus of 72 diverse networks, we demonstrate how these interactions reveal network patterns inaccessible through any single view alone. Live demo available at https://syndesmoscope.vercel.app/.

</details>


### [106] [Vibe Coding for Visualization Implementation: An Empirical Study of Practices and Challenges](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19703)
*Zhengyu Sun,Xiaolin Wen,Fengjie Wang,Can Liu,Yi Lai,Christophe Hurter,Yong Wang*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文通过16名参与者的实证研究，探究用户如何使用AI驱动的"vibe coding"工具进行数据可视化实现，揭示了用户在提示、评估和迭代过程中的多样化实践与挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数据可视化虽重要但创建过程劳动密集，现有AI工具虽降低了门槛，但可视化实现需要用户意图与视觉表征的精确对齐，这与通用软件开发有所不同，因此需要专门研究用户如何使用这些工具进行可视化实现。

Method: 招募16名不同专业水平的参与者，完成两项可视化任务并进行半结构化访谈，采用实证研究方法。

Result: 揭示了用户在提示（prompting）、评估（evaluation）和迭代（iteration）三个阶段采用的多样化实践模式，以及在可视化实现过程中遇到的各类挑战。

Conclusion: AI驱动的vibe coding工具在可视化实现中既有潜力也面临独特挑战，用户需要在自然语言交互中实现意图与视觉表征的精确对齐。

Abstract: Data visualization is essential for data analysis and communication, yet creating expressive visualizations remains labor-intensive. Recent AI-driven ``vibe coding'' tools enable users to generate visualizations through natural language interaction, lowering the barrier to entry. However, visualization implementation requires precise alignment between user intent and visual representation, which may differ from general software development practices. We present an empirical study with 16 participants of varying expertise to examine how users employ vibe coding tools for visualization implementation. Participants completed two visualization tasks and a semi-structured interview. Our findings characterize the diverse practices users adopt across prompting, evaluation, and iteration, and surface the challenges they encounter throughout the process.

</details>


### [107] [Designing for Interconnected Islamic Learning: A Qualitative Study of Muslim Women's Experiences with Qur'an, Hadith, and Seerah Apps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19745)
*Ishrat Jahan Easha,Nabil Mosharraf Hossain,Araf Mohammad Mahbub,Fairoze Bint Abu Hassan,Zunaid Aslam,Yemin Sajid,Riasat Islam*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 通过对五名穆斯林女性的半结构化访谈，研究揭示了伊斯兰数字学习工具中古兰经、圣训和先知传记的分离问题，提出了“分层语境性”概念，强调语境扩展需平衡解释可信度、 devotional flow 和设备间的连续性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 伊斯兰学习需要同时阅读古兰经、圣训和先知传记，但现有数字工具将这三种资源分散在不同的应用和搜索路径中，破坏了学习体验的连贯性。

Method: 从在线伊斯兰学习社区招募五名穆斯林女性，进行半结构化访谈，运用性别化数字宗教、认识论信任和无缝学习理论进行分析。

Result: 提炼出五个主题：语境理解、真实性、界面杂乱、学习模式和引导功能；揭示了参与者期望在阅读时获得跨文本语境，但只有当这种扩展可信、可选且不打断阅读时才被接受。

Conclusion: 提出“分层语境性”作为HCI概念，指出数字宗教学习中语境扩展必须与解释可信度、虔诚专注以及跨设备、跨强度的学习连续性相平衡。

Abstract: Islamic learning often depends on reading the Qur'an, Hadith, and Seerah together, yet digital tools typically separate these sources across apps, screens, and search pathways. We examine this as a human-computer interaction problem through five semi-structured interviews with Muslim women recruited from an online Islamic learning community. Participants described a recurring tension: they wanted Qur'an-Hadith-Seerah context at the point of reading, but only when contextual expansion remained trustworthy, optional, and did not interrupt reading. Interpreting the interviews through gendered digital religion, epistemic trust, and seamless learning, we identify five themes concerning contextual understanding, authenticity, interface clutter, study modes, and guidance features. We introduce layered contextuality as an HCI account of this domain: contextual expansion must be balanced with interpretive accountability, devotional flow, and continuity across devices and study intensities.

</details>


### [108] [MemGUI-Agent: An End-to-End Long-Horizon Mobile GUI Agent with Proactive Context Management](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19926)
*Guangyi Liu,Gao Wu,Congxiao Liu,Pengxiang Zhao,Liang Liu,Mading Li,Qi Zhang,Mengyan Wang,Liang Guo,Yong Liu*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文提出MemGUI-Agent，一种基于"上下文即动作"(ConAct)范式的长视界移动GUI智能体，通过将上下文管理建模为可学习的动作来替代传统的ReAct式提示累积，解决了长任务中关键信息稀释和提示爆炸问题。在8B参数规模下，MemGUI-8B-SFT在MemGUI-Bench上达到最佳性能，并泛化到分布外数据集MobileWorld。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MLLM-based移动GUI智能体在短视界任务上表现良好，但在长视界任务中，ReAct-style提示方法会被动累积每步记录，导致提示爆炸和跨应用关键事实被稀释，智能体无法可靠地保留中间信息。

Method: 提出Context-as-Action (ConAct)范式，将上下文管理建模为与UI动作由同一策略发出的第一类动作；维护三个结构化上下文字段（折叠动作历史、折叠UI状态、最近步骤记录）；构建包含2,956条轨迹的MemGUI-3K数据集用于监督训练和离线分析。

Result: 在8B参数规模下，MemGUI-8B-SFT在MemGUI-Bench上达到最佳开放数据8B性能，并成功泛化到分布外的MobileWorld基准测试。

Conclusion: 将上下文管理作为可学习的主动动作而非被动累积，是提升移动GUI智能体长视界任务可靠性的有效途径，且该方法在不同模型规模下均可学习。

Abstract: MLLM-based mobile GUI agents have made substantial progress on short-horizon tasks, yet remain unreliable on long-horizon tasks that require retaining intermediate facts across many steps and app transitions. We attribute this limitation to ReAct-style prompting, which passively accumulates per-step records, leading to prompt explosion and dilution of critical cross-app facts. To address this, we introduce MemGUI-Agent, an end-to-end long-horizon mobile GUI agent with proactive context management. MemGUI-Agent is built on Context-as-Action (ConAct), which casts context management as first-class actions emitted by the same policy that selects UI actions. Instead of passively appending history, ConAct maintains three structured context fields: folded action history, folded UI state, and recent step record, preserving critical UI facts while keeping context compact. To make proactive context management learnable across model scales, we construct MemGUI-3K, a 2,956-trajectory dataset with full ConAct annotations for supervised training and offline analysis. Training an 8B model on MemGUI-3K produces MemGUI-8B-SFT, an 8B MemGUI-Agent that achieves the best open-data 8B performance on MemGUI-Bench and generalizes to the out-of-distribution MobileWorld benchmark. Code, data, and trained models will be released at https://memgui-agent.github.io/.

</details>


### [109] [MobileForge: Annotation-Free Adaptation for Mobile GUI Agents with Hierarchical Feedback-Guided Policy Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19930)
*Guangyi Liu,Pengxiang Zhao,Gao Wu,Yiwen Yin,Mading Li,Liang Liu,Congxiao Liu,Zhang Qi,Mengyan Wang,Liang Guo,Yong Liu*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: MobileForge是一个无需标注的移动GUI智能体适配系统，通过MobileGym环境与HiFPO分层策略优化，将Qwen3-VL-8B在AndroidWorld上提升至67.2% Pass@3，接近闭源模型；适配后的ForgeOwl-8B达到77.6% Pass@3和41.0%跨域成功率，成为最强开源移动GUI智能体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MLLM-based移动GUI智能体在适配真实目标应用时成本高昂，因为移动应用数量庞大、更新频繁，难以通过人工编写任务、演示或奖励标签来覆盖。无标注的GUI学习方法虽减少了人工监督，但缺乏统一框架来衔接目标应用探索、课程挖掘、轨迹执行与反馈，且策略优化常依赖孤立的轨迹和粗粒度奖励，难以转化为可靠的改进信号。

Method: 提出MobileForge系统，包含两个核心组件：(1) MobileGym——将任务生成和轨迹评估锚定在真实移动应用交互中；(2) 分层反馈引导策略优化（HiFPO）——将轨迹结果、步骤级过程反馈和修正提示转化为提示上下文化的步骤级GRPO更新。

Result: 在完全无标注的自动生成适配数据上，MobileForge将Qwen3-VL-8B在AndroidWorld上达到67.2% Pass@3，接近闭源GUI专用模型GUI-Owl-1.5-8B的69.0%。适配后的ForgeOwl-8B进一步达到77.6% Pass@3，并在域外MobileWorld GUI-only分割上取得41.0%成功率，成为评估中最强的开源移动GUI智能体。

Conclusion: MobileForge证明了无需人工标注即可有效适配移动GUI智能体，通过统一的环境框架和分层反馈策略优化，达到了与闭源专业模型相当甚至更优的性能，为移动GUI智能体的低成本大规模部署提供了可行方案。

Abstract: MLLM-based mobile GUI agents have made substantial progress in UI understanding and action execution, but adapting them to real target apps remains costly because mobile apps are numerous, frequently updated, and hard to cover with human-written tasks, demonstrations, or reward labels. Existing annotation-free GUI learning reduces manual supervision, yet lacks a unified substrate connecting target-app exploration, curriculum mining, rollout execution, and feedback, while policy optimization often relies on isolated rollouts and coarse rewards that are hard to convert into reliable improvement signals. We present MobileForge, an annotation-free adaptation system for mobile GUI agents. MobileForge consists of MobileGym, which grounds task generation and rollout evaluation in real mobile app interaction, and Hierarchical Feedback-Guided Policy Optimization (HiFPO), which turns trajectory outcomes, step-level process feedback, and corrective hints into hint-contextualized step-level GRPO updates. Using only automatically generated annotation-free adaptation data, MobileForge adapts Qwen3-VL-8B to 67.2% Pass@3 on AndroidWorld, close to the closed-data GUI-specialized GUI-Owl-1.5-8B base model at 69.0%. The MobileForge-adapted ForgeOwl-8B further reaches 77.6% Pass@3 on AndroidWorld and 41.0% success on the out-of-domain MobileWorld GUI-only split, establishing the strongest open-data mobile GUI agent in our evaluation. Code, data, and trained models will be released at https://mobile-forge.github.io/.

</details>


### [110] [DataMagic: Transforming Tabular Data into Data Insight Video](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20388)
*Yupeng Xie,Chen Ma,Zhenyang Wang,Liangwei Wang,Jiayi Zhu,Chuxuan Zeng,Zhouan Shen,Boyan Li,Yuyu Luo*

Main category: cs.HC
