While benchmarks have been central to progress in RL, existing safety benchmarks with high-fidelity 3D physics remain computationally slow, limiting large-scale experimentation and rapid prototyping. To address this gap, we propose CRAX (Constrained RL Accelerated with JAX). Built on top of the MuJoCo XLA (MJX) physics engine with realistic 3D dynamics, CRAX leverages vectorized operations and hardware acceleration, yielding up to ~100x speedups over comparable CPU-based safety benchmarks. The benchmark features six environment suites and three agent-specific tasks, each spanning three difficulty levels. Evaluating six popular safe RL methods shows that no single approach dominates across all tasks, and reveals the trade-offs between performance and safety. We find that curriculum learning across difficulty levels and safety transfer can improve performance over direct training in harder settings.

</details>


### [219] [Towards Modality-imbalanced Federated Graph Learning: A Data Synthesis-based Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20382)
*Zhengyu Wu,Hongchao Qin,Xunkai Li,Zekai Chen,Rong-Hua Li,Guoren Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出FedMGS框架，通过隐式图感知潜在语义表示合成解决多模态联邦图学习中粗细粒度的模态不平衡问题，在四个任务上实现最高17.41%的性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态联邦图学习（MM-FGL）面临客户端级和节点级两种粒度的模态不平衡挑战——某些客户端缺少完整模态或节点缺失视觉/文本属性。现有方法多针对非图或集中式场景，难以直接适配。

Method: 将模态不平衡MM-FGL形式化为隐式图感知潜在语义表示合成问题，提出FedMGS框架，包含三个核心组件：可用性感知图编码器（防止缺失模态污染局部结构传播）、原型引导的潜在语义合成器（建立跨客户端语义锚点）、可靠性校准的语义融合机制（调节恢复表示的影响）。

Result: 在四个任务上的实验表明，FedMGS在效率-性能权衡下始终优于竞争基线，最高提升17.41%。

Conclusion: FedMGS通过在表示空间中直接恢复缺失模态语义，最大化与原数据语义分布的对齐，有效缓解了缺失模态导致的高方差问题，为模态不平衡的MM-FGL提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: MultiModal Federated Graph Learning (MM-FGL) offers a natural collaborative training paradigm, but its practical deployment is challenged by two granularities of modality imbalance. Client-level imbalance occurs when certain clients lack entire modalities, while node-level imbalance occurs when individual nodes exhibit missing visual or textual attributes. While several relevant studies exist, our investigation reveals that they predominantly target graph-agnostic or centralized scenarios, rendering them difficult to adapt directly. To address these challenges, we formalize modality-imbalanced MM-FGL as an implicit graph-aware latent semantic representation synthesis problem. This paradigm recovers missing modal semantics directly within the representation space, thereby maximizing alignment with the original data's semantic distribution and mitigating the high variance induced by missing modalities. To this end, we propose FedMGS (Federated Modality-aware Graph Synthesis), which integrates three core components. The availability-aware graph encoder prevents missing modalities from contaminating local structural propagation. The prototype-guided latent semantic synthesizer establishes cross-client semantic anchors for unavailable modalities. The reliability-calibrated semantic fusion mechanism regulates the impact of recovered latent representations prior to predictive readout. Extensive experiments on four tasks show that FedMGS consistently outperforms competitive baselines with gains up to 17.41% with best efficiency-performance tradeoff.

</details>


### [220] [The Significance of Style Diversity in Annotation-Free Synthetic Data Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20400)
*Zahra Abbasiantaeb,Zeno Belligoli,Omar Essam,Mohammad Aliannejadi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种完全无需人工标注数据的意图分类合成对话生成框架，利用主题和风格属性提升数据多样性，通过LLM-as-a-judge过滤保证质量，在工业和公开数据集上达到人工标注数据93.3%的性能，并发现风格多样性比主题多样性对合成数据效用更关键。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在快节奏的工业场景中，获取人工标注的种子数据用于意图分类的高质量合成数据生成通常不可行，现有方法依赖人工标注数据，限制了其在工业界的实用性和扩展性。

Method: 提出无需人工标注的对话生成框架：(1) 利用两种不同的话题和风格属性提升数据多样性；(2) 提出Univ和Exam两种事后风格化模型，将LLM生成的合成话语转换为更多样化、类人的语言风格；(3) 采用LLM-as-a-judge过滤流程提升数据质量。

Result: 在工业和公开数据集上，所提方法达到使用人工标注训练数据性能的93.3%；风格多样性比主题多样性对合成数据效用更关键；在生成过程中融入风格属性比事后风格适配更有效。

Conclusion: 完全无需人工标注即可生成高质量的意图分类合成数据，风格多样性是影响合成数据效用的关键因素，生成过程中融入风格属性优于事后适配。

Abstract: Generating high-utility synthetic data for intent classification typically requires human-annotated seed data, which is often unavailable in fast-paced industrial settings. In this paper, we propose a framework for synthetic dialogue generation that works entirely without human-annotated data, relying solely on intent definitions. Our proposed dialogue generation framework utilizes two different types of topic and style attributes to improve data diversity. Also, we propose two novel post-hoc stylization models called Univ and Exam to transform synthetic LLM-generated utterances into more varied, human-like linguistic styles. To enhance data quality, we utilize an LLM-as-a-judge filtering process. Experimental results on both industrial and public datasets demonstrate that the proposed approach achieves up to 93.3% of the performance obtained using human-annotated training data. Crucially, the findings reveal that style diversity is more critical than topic diversity for synthetic data utility, as it prevents models from learning spurious stylistic correlations. Furthermore, the study shows that incorporating style attributes during the generation process is more effective than post-hoc style adaptation.

</details>


### [221] [Direct Advantage Estimation for Scalable and Sample-efficient Deep Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20411)
*Hsiao-Ru Pan,Bernhard Schölkopf*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文扩展了直接优势估计（DAE）到部分可观测领域，并通过引入离散潜动态模型降低计算复杂度，在Arcade Learning Environment上验证了其有效性和样本效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DAE在深度强化学习中能提升样本效率，但存在两个局限：依赖完全可观测环境（限制现实应用）以及需要建模转移概率导致高维观测下计算开销大。本文旨在同时解决这两个问题。

Method: 1) 将DAE的理论框架扩展到部分可观测领域，仅需最小修改；2) 引入离散潜动态模型来高效近似转移概率，降低计算复杂度。

Result: 在Arcade Learning Environment上，DAE随着函数逼近器容量的增加而有效扩展，同时保持高样本效率。

Conclusion: 所提方法成功将DAE的应用范围扩展至部分可观测环境，并显著降低了计算开销，在保持样本效率的同时具有良好的可扩展性。

Abstract: Direct Advantage Estimation (DAE) has been shown to improve the sample efficiency of deep reinforcement learning algorithms. However, its reliance on full environment observability limits its applicability in realistic settings, and its requirement to model transition probabilities incurs substantial computational overhead for high-dimensional observations. In the present work, we address both limitations. First, we extend the theoretical framework of DAE to partially observable domains with minimal modifications. Second, we reduce its computational complexity by introducing discrete latent dynamics models that efficiently approximate transition probabilities. We evaluate our approach on the Arcade Learning Environment and find that DAE scales effectively with function approximator capacity while retaining high sample efficiency.

</details>


### [222] [Pseudo-Feature Padding: A Lightweight Defense Against False Data Injection in Power Grids](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20415)
*Farhin Farhad Riya,Shahinul Hoque,Yingyuan Yang,Jinyuan Sun,Kevin Tomsovic*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种面向网络物理系统（CPS）中深度神经网络（DNN）的轻量级防御框架，通过在输入样本中添加基于统计分布的伪特征填充（padding），以随机化和数据感知方式增加输入维度，使对抗性攻击的计算变得不可行，从而有效抵御虚假数据注入攻击（FDIA），在IEEE多总线电力系统上验证了其鲁棒性提升和低性能影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CPS中DNN在检测FDIA方面表现优异，但CPS的独特基础设施使DNN容易被攻击者利用以逃避检测，且传统防御机制难以应对此类攻击。

Method: 提出一种额外的输入层，利用输入样本统计分布生成伪特征值进行填充（padding），以随机化和数据感知方式增加输入维度，使对抗扰动不可迁移且填充结构不可预测，框架轻量级、模型无关且无需修改核心架构。

Result: 在IEEE 14、30、118和300总线系统的电力系统状态估计任务上，该填充策略在对抗环境下显著提升了模型鲁棒性，性能影响极小，有效缓解了传统防御无法抵御的攻击。

Conclusion: 所提方法是一种高效、可部署于真实CPS环境的对抗防御方案，通过简单的输入填充即可实现显著的鲁棒性提升。

Abstract: Deep Neural Networks DNNs have achieved remarkable accuracy in various tasks including their application in CyberPhysical Systems CPS for detecting False Data Injection Attacks FDIA during critical operations However the unique infrastructure of CPS makes DNNs vulnerable to exploitation by attackers aiming to evade detection Additionally the distinct nature of CPS presents challenges for conventional defense mechanisms against FDIA This paper proposes an innovative defense framework that strengthens DNNs against such attacks by introducing an additional input layer that performs padding in the input samples using pseudofeature values derived from the inputs statistical distribution This padding increases the input dimensionality in a randomized and dataaware manner making adversarial attacks computationally infeasible due to the nontransferable nature of crafted perturbations and the unpredictability of the padded structure Our method is lightweight modelagnostic and requires no modifications to the core architecture making it highly deployable in realworld CPS settings We evaluated our framework on critical power grid applications such as state estimation using the IEEE 14bus 30bus 118bus and 300bus systems Experiments under adversarial settings demonstrate that our padding strategy significantly improves model robustness with negligible impact on performance and effectively mitigates attacks that would otherwise bypass conventional defenses

</details>


### [223] [On the Redundancy of Timestep Embeddings in Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20416)
*José A. Chávez*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文挑战了扩散模型中显式时间步嵌入的必要性，通过理论和实验证明，在特定条件下，无时间条件的U-Net和DiT模型能隐式推断噪声尺度，甚至在某些指标上超越有条件的模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 质疑扩散模型中显式时间步嵌入的必要性，探究无时间条件建模的可行性，以简化架构、提升效率。

Method: 理论分析证明扩散训练目标全局最优解可在无显式时间条件时达到；在U-Net和DiT架构上移除时间嵌入，在CelebA和CIFAR-10数据集上进行消融实验。

Result: 无时间条件的模型能保持高结构保真度，在FID、precision、recall等指标上甚至超越有条件的对应模型。

Conclusion: 扩散架构可通过受损输入隐式推断噪声尺度，显式时间条件并非必要，这为更高效、结构聚焦的生成架构开辟了新路径。

Abstract: Diffusion models rely heavily on explicit timestep embeddings to modulate the denoising process across various noise scales. In this work, we challenge the necessity of these temporal signals by analyzing their impact on U-Net and Diffusion Transformer architectures. Beyond empirical evidence, we provide a theoretical framework demonstrating that, under certain conditions, the global minimizer of the diffusion training objective can be achieved without explicit timestep conditioning. Our findings reveal a surprising robustness when timestep embeddings are completely removed. Extensive ablation studies on the CelebA and CIFAR-10 datasets show that these time-agnostic models can maintain high structural fidelity and even surpass their conditioned counterparts in competitive metrics, including FID, precision, and recall. Our analysis suggests these architectures can implicitly infer noise scales from the corrupted input under specific assumptions, rendering explicit temporal conditioning redundant. This study challenges long-standing temporal conditioning paradigms and paves the way for more efficient and structurally focused generative architectures.

</details>


### [224] [Neural network surrogates with uncertainty quantification for inverse problems in partial differential equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20417)
*Christian Jimenez-Beltran,Aretha L. Teckentrup,Antonio Vergari,Konstantinos C. Zygalakis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出DeepGaLA，一种为微分方程求解器提供不确定性感知预测的神经网络替代模型，通过延迟接受MCMC进行诊断，在保持精度的同时提升高维参数空间的计算效率，实现可扩展且可靠的贝叶斯逆问题推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统数值方法求解微分方程逆问题时计算成本高昂，尤其在贝叶斯框架下，复杂前向模型和高维参数空间中的似然评估极为耗时，亟需高效的替代模型。

Method: 提出DeepGaLA神经网络替代模型，提供不确定性感知的预测；使用延迟接受MCMC的短链运行作为诊断工具，评估替代模型诱导的后验近似保真度。

Result: DeepGaLA在多个数值实验中，其前向模型近似精度与高斯过程替代模型相当，且在高维参数空间中效率更优；能融入微分方程约束（包括非线性场景），减少训练数据有限时的过置信推理。

Conclusion: 不确定性量化的神经替代模型能够实现复杂系统中逆问题的可扩展且可靠的贝叶斯推理。

Abstract: Inverse problems for differential equations arise throughout science and engineering, where one seeks to infer unknown model parameters from noisy or incomplete observations. Traditional numerical methods for these problems are often computationally expensive, particularly in Bayesian settings where evaluating the likelihood becomes costly for complex forward models and high-dimensional parameter spaces. To address this challenge, we introduce DeepGaLA, a neural-network surrogate for differential equation solvers that provides uncertainty-aware predictions, reducing overconfident inference when training data are limited. To evaluate the fidelity of the surrogate-induced posterior approximations in practice, we show that a short run of delayed-acceptance Markov chain Monte Carlo can serve as an effective diagnostic. Across a range of numerical experiments, DeepGaLA delivers forward-model approximations with accuracy comparable to established Gaussian-process surrogates, while better maintaining efficiency as parameter dimension grows. Moreover, it can incorporate differential-equation constraints, including in nonlinear settings. Overall, these results indicate that uncertainty-quantified neural surrogates can enable scalable and reliable Bayesian inference for inverse problems in complex systems.

</details>


### [225] [Sparsity, Superposition, and Forgetting: A Mechanistic Study of Representation Retention in Continual Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20431)
*Jan Wasilewski,Jędrzej Kozal,Michał Woźniak,Bartosz Krawczyk*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一个可控的玩具框架来隔离和观察持续学习中的遗忘机制，通过合成数据管道定义真实潜在特征、构建可调稀疏性和重叠度的任务，并引入表示强度与叠加的量化指标。利用SINDy拟合保留动态关系，结合有效秩分析任务级表示能力分配。实验发现：(1)叠加在任务边界出现瞬时下降，提示边界特异性干扰；(2)高特征稀疏性增加叠加但不必然导致遗忘；(3)稀疏条件下有效秩增长，表明容量利用更广泛。这些结果细化了"叠加导致遗忘"的直觉认知。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 持续学习系统经常遗忘先前获取的知识，但在实际数据集中由于多种因素纠缠，难以隔离和观察遗忘的机制。作者希望构建一个可控环境，使这些机制变得可观测和可检验。

Method: 采用生成器-分离器合成数据管道，定义ground-truth潜在特征，构建具有可调稀疏性和重叠度的任务。引入表示强度和叠加的量化指标，通过SINDy拟合保留动态（保留、叠加、暴露历史之间的稀疏动态关系），并利用有效秩进行任务级分析。

Result: (1)叠加随时间增加，但在任务边界出现瞬时下降，表明存在边界特异性干扰而非稳定漂移；(2)高特征稀疏性导致更多叠加，但不必然引起遗忘——当表示强度足够强时，即使存在重叠也可减少遗忘；(3)任务级有效秩随稀疏性增长，表明稀疏条件下容量利用更广泛。

Conclusion: 叠加与遗忘的关系并非简单正相关，而是与表示强度和容量分配共同交互作用。该玩具分析为持续学习提供了可证伪的假设和诊断工具，细化了对叠加导致遗忘的常识性理解。

Abstract: Continual learning (CL) systems often forget previously acquired knowledge, yet the mechanisms driving forgetting remain hard to isolate in practice because real datasets entangle many factors. We present a controlled, toy-world framework that makes these mechanisms observable and testable. Using a synthetic generator-separator pipeline, we define ground-truth latent features, build tasks with tunable sparsity and overlap, and introduce measurable quantities for representation strength and superposition (directional overlap among features). We then study retention dynamics-the temporal change of representation strength by fitting sparse dynamical relations (via SINDy) between retention, superposition, and exposure history. A complementary task-level analysis based on effective rank characterizes how representational capacity is allocated across tasks. Our controlled experiments yield three takeaways. (1) Superposition tends to increase over time with transient dips at task boundaries, suggesting boundary-specific interference rather than steady drift. (2) Higher feature sparsity induces more superposition yet does not inevitably cause forgetting; when representations remain strong, forgetting can be reduced despite overlap. (3) Task-level effective rank grows with sparsity, indicating broader capacity usage under sparse regimes. Together, these results nuance the common intuition that more superposition leads to more forgetting by showing that overlap interacts with representation strength and capacity allocation. Our toy analysis provides falsifiable hypotheses and diagnostic tools for CL.

</details>


### [226] [Agentic Symbolic Search: Characterizing PDEs Beyond Hand-crafted Expressions, Meshes, and Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20467)
*Zongmin Yu,Liu Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为Agentic Symbolic Search (ASYS)的框架，通过将PDE理论、问题约束和搜索经验转化为可测试的符号程序，自动发现偏微分方程的可解释解析解或近似解，超越了传统数值模拟和神经网络方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数学家通过数学结构而非数值表格理解PDE解，但传统分析方法需针对每个问题手工推导，数值模拟和神经网络无法直接产生这些结构，因此需要自动化发现可解释符号表示的方法。

Method: ASYS是一个先验引导的框架，其中智能体将PDE理论、公共问题约束和累积搜索经验转化为可测试的可微分符号程序。数学形式通过进化搜索精炼，连续参数通过梯度优化拟合，实现自动化的归纳偏置注入而非盲目的符号回归。

Result: 在五个问题（有界动力学、有限时间爆破、自由边界聚焦）上，ASYS产生了可解释表示，包括Allen-Cahn 2D动力学的几何界面公式和Keller-Segel趋化爆破的九参数收缩律，而这些此前没有封闭形式描述。

Conclusion: ASYS展示了一种表征PDE解的新范式，超越了手工解析解、基于网格的数值解和神经网络近似，能够自动发现可解释的符号结构以指导数学家进一步分析。

Abstract: Mathematicians understand a PDE solution through mathematical structures rather than tables of computed values. Historically, this has been the product of mathematical analysis, carried out by hand for each problem individually. Neither numerical simulation nor neural networks produce those structures directly. We propose Agentic Symbolic Search (ASYS), a prior-guided framework in which an agent translates PDE theory, public problem constraints, and accumulated search experience into testable differentiable symbolic programs. The mathematical forms are refined under evolutionary search, while their continuous parameters are fit by gradient-based optimization. This makes the search an automated form of inductive-bias injection rather than blind symbolic regression. For problems with known analytical forms, ASYS recovers these forms naturally; for other problems, ASYS constructs analytical approximations which can guide mathematicians toward further analysis. In our experiments, across five problems spanning bounded dynamics, finite-time blow-up, and free-boundary focusing, ASYS produces interpretable representations, including a geometric interface formula for Allen-Cahn 2D dynamics and a nine-parameter contraction law for Keller-Segel chemotactic blow-up, in settings where no closed-form description was previously available. ASYS shows the possibility of a new paradigm for characterizing PDE solutions, beyond handcrafted analytical solutions, mesh-based numerical solutions, and neural network approximations.

</details>


### [227] [Fisher-Geometric Sharpness and the Implicit Bias of SGD toward Flat Minima](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20469)
*Md Sakir Ahmed,Kumaresh Sarmah,Hemen Dutta*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了基于Fisher信息矩阵的黎曼几何尖锐度(Riemannian sharpness)，解决了SGD偏好平坦极小值这一直觉在重参数化下不变性的理论问题，并通过理论推导和实验验证了该度量与泛化性能的可靠关联。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习中存在一个广泛接受的直觉：SGD隐式偏好平坦极小值，且平坦极小值泛化更好。但标准的欧氏平坦度度量（如Hessian矩阵的迹或最大特征值）在保持网络函数的重参数化下不具有不变性，这削弱了该叙事的理论基础。本文旨在解决这一理论缺陷。

Method: 将平坦性 grounding 在由Fisher信息矩阵(FIM)诱导的统计流形的黎曼几何中，定义黎曼尖锐度(Riemannian sharpness)并证明其在光滑、函数保持的重参数化下具有不变性。将小批量SGD的梯度噪声形式化为协方差结构与FIM成比例，推导随机微分方程的稳态分布，证明概率质量指数级集中在黎曼平坦极小值处。

Result: 黎曼尖锐度在真实FIM下具有精确的重参数化不变性；概率质量指数级集中在黎曼平坦极小值处；通过PAC-Bayes泛化界限建立了几何偏差与测试性能的联系；在MNIST和CIFAR-10上的实验表明SR能可靠追踪泛化，且其与η/B的缩放符合理论预测。

Conclusion: 本文为"平坦极小值泛化更好"提供了严格、重参数化不变的理论解释，解决了该领域长期存在的理论矛盾。

Abstract: A widely held intuition in deep learning is that stochastic gradient descent (SGD) implicitly favors flat minima and that flat minima generalize better, but standard Euclidean measures of flatness such as the trace or maximum eigenvalue of the loss Hessian are not invariant under reparametrizations that preserve the network function, which undermines the theoretical foundations of this narrative. In this study we resolve this issue by grounding flatness in the Riemannian geometry of the statistical manifold induced by the Fisher Information Matrix (FIM). We define Riemannian sharpness mathematically and prove that it is invariant under smooth, function-preserving reparametrizations, which directly addresses the critique of Dinh et al. in the paper ``Sharp minima can generalize for deep nets''.We note that this invariance is a property of the true FIM; the diagonal empirical estimator used in practice (and in all experiments below) inherits invariance only approximately, and exact invariance under arbitrary reparametrizations would require structured estimators such as K-FAC. We formalize the gradient noise of mini-batch SGD as having a covariance structure proportional to the FIM, derive the stationary distribution of the resulting stochastic differential equation, and then show that the probability mass is exponentially concentrated at Riemannian-flat minima. A PAC-Bayes generalization bound controlled explicitly by SR formally links this geometric bias to test performance. Our experiments on MNIST and CIFAR-10 confirm that SR reliably tracks generalization in ways that Euclidean sharpness does not, and that its scaling with $η/B$ matches the theoretical predictions. Together these results provide a rigorous, reparametrization-invariant account of why flat minima generalize.

</details>


### [228] [UltraQuant: 4-bit KV Caching for Context-Heavy Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20474)
*Inesh Chakrabarti,David Limpus,Aditi Ghai Rana,Bowen Bao,Spandan Tiwari,Thiago Crepaldi,Ashish Sirasao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对上下文密集型代理工作负载，研究了4位KV缓存压缩技术。通过TurboQuant风格的旋转和码本量化保证质量，以vLLM FP8 KV缓存为部署基准。提出了三个贡献：1) 建立多轮代理任务中任务质量、缓存驻留和服务吞吐量的联合评估框架；2) 描述使4位路径鲁棒的实用设计选择（非对称K/V处理、Walsh-Hadamard旋转、QJL移除、块尺度变体）；3) 在AMD GPU上的服务优化（优化解码注意力核、UltraQuant FP4近似路径）。在长上下文多轮代理工作负载上，UltraQuant在缓存压力大的后期轮次将P50首词延迟降低3.47倍（全轮次2.3倍），输出吞吐量相比FP8 KV基线提升1.63倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 上下文密集型代理（Context-heavy agents）对KV缓存造成巨大压力：长前缀在多轮短对话中被重复使用，并发性决定了服务系统能否保持GPU利用率。现有FP8 KV缓存方案在代理场景下内存和带宽效率不足，需要更激进的量化方案来缓解缓存压力。

Method: 采用4位KV缓存压缩，基于TurboQuant风格的旋转和码本量化；设计方面包括非对称K/V处理、Walsh-Hadamard旋转、移除QJL、引入块尺度变体；在AMD GPU上实现优化解码注意力核以及UltraQuant FP4近似路径（FP8查询、FP4 KV张量、UE8M0组尺度、CDNA4原生scaled-MFMA支持）。

Result: 在长上下文多轮代理工作负载上，UltraQuant在缓存压力大的后期轮次将P50首词延迟降低3.47倍（全轮次2.3倍），输出吞吐量相比FP8 KV基线提升1.63倍。

Conclusion: 4位KV缓存压缩结合适当的系统优化（如UltraQuant）能够在代理工作负载中显著降低延迟并提升吞吐量，同时保持任务质量，验证了在缓存压力场景下激进量化的有效性。

Abstract: Context-heavy agents place unusual pressure on the key-value (KV) cache: long prefixes are reused across many short turns, while concurrency determines whether the serving system can keep GPUs utilized. We study 4-bit KV-cache compression for this setting, using TurboQuant-style rotation and codebook quantization as a quality anchor and vLLM FP8 KV caching as the deployment anchor. We report three contributions. First, we frame 4-bit KV caching around multi-round agent workloads where task quality, cache residency, and serving throughput must be measured jointly. Second, we describe the practical design choices needed to make the 4-bit path robust, including asymmetric K/V treatment, Walsh-Hadamard rotation, QJL removal, and block-scale variants. Third, we present serving optimizations on AMD GPUs, including optimized decode-attention kernels and UltraQuant, an FP4 approximation path that uses FP8 queries, FP4 KV tensors, UE8M0 group scales, and native scaled-MFMA support on CDNA4. On a long-context, multi-turn agentic workload, UltraQuant cuts P50 time-to-first-token by 3.47x in the cache-pressured late rounds (2.3x across all rounds) and raises output throughput by 1.63x over the FP8 KV baseline.

</details>


### [229] [Marginal Advantage Accumulation for Memory-Driven Agent Self-Evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20475)
*Mingyu Yang,Keye Zheng,Congchao Cheng,Yujie Liu,Xingkang Lu,Fan Jiang,Yefei Zheng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出边际优势累积（MAA）机制，通过跨批次、操作级别的证据累积来区分稳定有效操作与偶然命中，在14/16的实验设置中取得最佳结果，并减少约75%的优化阶段token消耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有批量式迹蒸馏方法中，同一内存操作在不同批次可能收到矛盾反馈，缺乏跨批次的操作级证据累积机制，无法区分稳定有效操作与偶然命中。

Method: 形式化定义了可对齐性（alignability）和可比性（comparability）两个结构条件；提出边际优势累积（MAA），通过构建差分信号使操作跨批次可比，利用指数移动平均（EMA）累积每操作的有符号证据，并通过语义身份合并确保跨批次可追溯性。

Result: 在4个基准和4个目标模型的16个设置中，14个达到最佳结果；持续优于现有批量级蒸馏基线，在多数设置中匹配或超越在线替代方法；优化阶段token消耗减少约75%。

Conclusion: MAA作为一种后处理架构，有效解决了跨批次证据累积问题，显著提升了蒸馏性能并大幅降低计算开销。

Abstract: In batch-style trace distillation, the same memory operation may receive contradictory feedback across different batches. Existing methods lack a cross-batch, operation-level evidence accumulation mechanism, making it impossible to distinguish stably effective operations from accidental hits. This paper formalizes the requirement as two structural conditions, alignability and comparability, and proposes Marginal Advantage Accumulation (MAA). MAA constructs differential signals to make them comparable across batches, accumulates signed evidence per operation via EMA, and ensures cross-batch traceability through semantic identity merging. As a post-processing architecture, MAA achieves the best results in 14 out of 16 settings across 4 benchmarks and 4 target models, consistently outperforming existing batch-level distillation baselines and matching or surpassing online alternatives in most settings, while reducing optimization-phase token consumption by approximately 75%.

</details>


### [230] [Contagion Networks: Evaluator Bias Propagation in Multi-Agent LLM Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20493)
*Zewen Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出"传染网络"(Contagion Networks)形式化框架，用于度量LLM评估器偏差在智能体间的传播。通过3-agent实验（DeepSeek-chat，三种偏差类型），发现偏差持续传播（gamma∈[0.157,0.352]），定义三种传播机制（由谱半径ρ(G_N)决定），同质模型传播系数比跨模型弱3-5倍（处于抑制区），将评估委员会从k=1增至k=3可减少72.4%有效传染。开源了实验框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当大语言模型在多智能体系统中充当评估器时，其系统性评估偏差会通过智能体网络传播，影响评估结果可靠性。现有研究缺乏量化这种偏差传播的形式化方法，因此需要建立统一框架来度量和理解LLM评估偏差在智能体间的传染机制。

Method: 提出Contagion Networks形式化框架，定义Cross-Agent Contagion Matrix Gamma_N量化偏差传播。设计3-agent实验，使用DeepSeek-chat模型，设置三种评估偏差类型（结构化、平衡、基于证据），计算传染矩阵并分析谱半径ρ(Gamma_N)定义传播机制，最后测试评估委员会规模（k=1至k=3）的缓解效果。

Result: 1）评估偏差在智能体间持续传播（gamma∈[0.157,0.352]）；2）识别出三种传播机制（由谱半径ρ(G_N)定义）；3）同质模型（同一基础模型）智能体间的传染系数较跨模型弱3-5倍，处于抑制区；4）评估委员会从k=1增至k=3可减少72.4%有效传染。

Conclusion: LLM评估偏差在多智能体系统中会系统性传播，但可通过增大评估委员会规模显著缓解。同质模型传播相对可控（抑制区），为构建更可靠的多智能体评估系统提供了量化依据和可操作策略。

Abstract: When large language models serve as evaluators in multi-agent systems, their systematic evaluation biases propagate through the agent network. We introduce Contagion Networks, a formal framework for measuring how evaluator biases spread across interacting LLM agents. In a controlled 3-agent experiment using DeepSeek-chat with three distinct evaluator bias profiles (structured, balanced, evidence-based), we measure the Cross-Agent Contagion Matrix Gamma_3 and find that evaluator biases consistently propagate between agents (gamma in [0.157, 0.352]), even within the same underlying model. We identify three propagation regimes governed by the spectral radius rho(Gamma_N), and demonstrate that homogeneous-model agents produce contagion coefficients 3-5x weaker than cross-model coefficients observed in prior work (MM-EPC: gamma approx 0.85-1.3), placing them in the suppression regime. We show that increasing evaluator committee size from k=1 to k=3 reduces effective contagion by 72.4%, providing an actionable mitigation strategy. We release the open-source Contagion Network experimental framework.

</details>


### [231] [Execution-State Capsules: Graph-Bound Execution-State Checkpoint and Restore for Low-Latency, Small-Batch, On-Device Physical-AI Serving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20537)
*Liang Su*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出FlashRT，一种基于执行状态胶囊（execution-state capsules）的图绑定检查点与恢复机制，可在低延迟、小批量的设备端物理AI推理场景中，完整保存和恢复包括KV缓存、循环状态、卷积状态等在内的整个执行边界状态，实现亚毫秒级的GPU快照与恢复，相比冷启动预填充实现3.9倍至27倍的TTFT加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有主流LLM服务系统通过分页或基数KV缓存重用前缀工作，在高吞吐、高并发场景高效，但仅管理KV缓存这一个位置片段。然而在低延迟、小批量、设备端物理AI服务场景中（如交互式LLM智能体、语音系统、机器人策略），需要频繁分支、重置、中断和重新进入，且响应预算紧张，现有KV缓存重用机制不足以支持完整的执行状态复用。

Method: 提出执行状态胶囊（execution-state capsules），一种图绑定（graph-bound）的检查点与恢复机制，在提交边界（committed boundary）处保存完整的可恢复状态。FlashRT是一个白盒、面向后端的核运行时，其NVIDIA CUDA后端在连续的静态缓冲区上运行捕获的图计划，无需块表间接寻址。由于活跃状态是一组封闭的命名缓冲区，胶囊可以对整个执行边界（包括KV状态、循环状态、卷积状态、MTP状态和元数据）进行快照、恢复、分支或回滚，将重用从token寻址的KV片段迁移到图绑定的执行状态边界。

Result: 在RTX 5090上，胶囊恢复在存储状态级别是字节精确的，在贪心解码下是token一致的。仅KV的消融实验出现分歧，表明循环状态是关键的。GPU驻留的快照和恢复均在亚毫秒级完成，TTFT加速比从2k token时的3.9倍增长到16k token时的27倍。在Jetson AGX Thor和DGX Spark上也验证了相同的正确性和结构特性。

Conclusion: 执行状态胶囊并非取代高吞吐的KV缓存服务机制，而是定义了一种互补的、以延迟优先的服务端点，用于显式的执行状态复用。

Abstract: Mainstream LLM serving systems reuse prefix work mainly through paged or radix key-value (KV) caches. This is highly effective for high-throughput, high-concurrency serving, but it manages only one positional fragment of execution state: the KV cache. We study the opposite regime: low-latency, small-batch, on-device physical-AI serving, where interactive LLM agents, speech systems, and robot policies repeatedly branch, reset, interrupt, and re-enter under tight responsiveness budgets. We introduce execution-state capsules, a graph-bound checkpoint and restore mechanism for the complete restorable state at a committed boundary. FlashRT is a white-box, backend-facing kernel runtime whose evaluated NVIDIA CUDA backend runs captured graph plans over contiguous static buffers with no block-table indirection. Because the live state is a closed set of named buffers, a capsule can snapshot, restore, fork, or roll back the whole execution boundary, including KV, recurrent state, convolution state, MTP state, and metadata. This moves reuse from token-addressed KV fragments to graph-bound execution-state boundaries. On an RTX 5090, capsule restore is byte-exact at the stored-state level and token-identical under greedy decode. A KV-only ablation diverges, showing that recurrent state is load-bearing. GPU-resident snapshot and restore are sub-millisecond, and TTFT speedup over cold prefill grows from 3.9x at 2k tokens to 27x at 16k tokens. On Jetson AGX Thor and DGX Spark, the same correctness and structural properties hold. Capsules are not a replacement for high-throughput KV-cache serving; they define a complementary latency-first serving point for explicit execution-state reuse.

</details>


### [232] [Multi-Task Bayesian In-Context Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20538)
*Qingyang Zhu,Eric Karl Oermann,Kyunghyun Cho*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种多任务上下文学习框架，用于摊销层次化贝叶斯预测推理。该方法通过将先验信息显式表示为上下文数据集的前缀，训练Transformer在序列化的先验与目标任务上学习跨先验族自适应预测。在多种难度评估（包括分布外先验和高维潜结构先验）中，该方法匹配了Oracle贝叶斯预测器且速度快数个数量级，并在真实时空温度预测任务上验证了实用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 贝叶斯预测推理虽然理论上优雅，但精确推理难以处理，现有近似方法要么计算昂贵，要么需要限制性建模假设导致预测性能下降。已有的先验数据拟合和上下文模型虽然提供了摊销替代方案，但与训练先验支持紧密耦合，缺乏测试时自适应新先验的显式机制，导致在分布偏移下鲁棒性不足。

Method: 提出多任务上下文学习框架，将先验信息显式编码为上下文数据集的前缀（prefix），训练Transformer在由先验任务和目标任务组成的序列上进行学习。模型通过序列化方式学习跨不同先验族的自适应预测能力，实现摊销的层次化贝叶斯推理。

Result: 在包括元分布外先验和高维潜结构先验在内的多种难度评估中，该方法匹配了Oracle贝叶斯预测器的性能，同时计算速度提升数个数量级。在真实世界时空温度预测基准上验证了实际应用价值。

Conclusion: 所提出的多任务上下文学习框架能够有效实现摊销层次化贝叶斯预测推理，兼具计算效率与预测精度，且对先验分布偏移具有鲁棒性，为实际贝叶斯推理应用提供了可扩展的实用方案。

Abstract: Bayesian predictive inference provides a principled framework for uncertainty quantification, data efficiency, and robust generalization. However, exact inference is often intractable, and scalable approximations may remain computationally expensive or require restrictive modeling assumptions that degrade predictive performance. Prior-Data Fitted and in-context models have recently emerged as an amortized alternative by learning to map datasets directly to predictive distributions, but existing approaches are tightly coupled to the support of the training prior and lack explicit mechanisms for adapting to new priors at test time, resulting in limited robustness under distribution shift. We introduce a multi-task in-context learning framework for amortized hierarchical Bayesian predictive inference that explicitly represents prior information as a prefix of in-context datasets. A transformer trained on sequences of prior and target tasks learns to adapt its predictions across families of priors. On a suite of evaluations with increasing difficulty, including out-of-meta-distribution priors and priors with high-dimensional latent structures, our method matches oracle Bayesian predictors while being orders of magnitude faster. We further demonstrate its practical relevance on a real-world spatiotemporal temperature prediction benchmark. Code is available at https://github.com/martianmartina/multi-task-bayesian-icl/.

</details>


### [233] [Predictability as a Fine-Grained Measure for Privacy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20546)
*Linda Lu,Karthik Sridharan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出"基于可预测性的隐私"（predictability）框架，通过量化攻击者在观察算法输出后对未知个体敏感信息的预测能力增益来衡量隐私泄露，与差分隐私（DP）形成互补。该框架在特定攻击者模型下比DP更具细粒度，并利用广义矩估计（GMM）分析渐近可预测性，为ERM设计了可预测性校准的输出扰动方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 差分隐私的 worst-case 性质导致隐私-准确性权衡代价高昂，缺乏针对特定攻击者知识和敏感信息的细粒度隐私度量。

Method: 提出"可预测性"隐私框架，将攻击者核心知识、被攻破数据集（随机过程生成）和查询族显式纳入模型；利用广义矩估计（GMM）分析平稳、遍历、混合过程下的渐近可预测性；基于此设计可预测性校准的输出扰动方案用于ERM。

Result: 可预测性与差分隐私一般不可比较（一方小另一方可大）；在除一人外全部被攻破且所有二元查询敏感的极端情形下，可预测性蕴含互信息DP。

Conclusion: 可预测性提供了比DP更细粒度的隐私度量，可与DP协同使用以实现精细的隐私控制。

Abstract: Differential privacy (DP) ensures rigorous individual-level privacy guarantees against even the most knowledgeable attackers, but its worst-case nature can impose a costly privacy-accuracy tradeoff. We introduce privacy via predictability, a fine-grained framework that explicitly incorporates the attacker's core knowledge, a compromised portion of the dataset generated by a stochastic process, and a specified family of queries. Predictability measures privacy leakage as the incremental gain in an attacker's ability to predict sensitive information about unknown individuals after observing the algorithm's output, beyond what can already be inferred from the compromised data. We show that predictability and DP are generally incomparable: each can be small while the other is large. However, in the worst-case regime where all but one individual is compromised, and all binary queries are considered sensitive, predictability implies mutual-information DP. More generally, predictability provides a finer-grained privacy metric tailored to specific sensitive information and specific attacker models. We introduce a general framework, using the generalized method of moments (GMM), to analyze asymptotic predictability when the compromised data is generated by a stationary, ergodic, mixing process. Using this analysis, we derive a predictability-calibrated output perturbation scheme for ERM. Our approach is complementary to DP and can be used alongside DP to provide fine-grained privacy control.

</details>


### [234] [Optimal Deterministic Multicalibration and Omniprediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20557)
*Georgy Noarov,Aaron Roth*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文解决了多校准中随机化是否对最优样本复杂度必要的问题，给出了一个达到极小最大最优样本复杂度的确定性预测器算法，并推广到结果不可区分性、全知预测器和泛预测器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前所有达到极小最大最优样本复杂度的多校准预测器都是随机化的，确定性预测器的样本复杂度明显更差。随机化是否对最优样本复杂度必要是一个公开问题。

Method: 提出了一个达到极小最大最优样本复杂度的确定性多校准算法，并将其推广到输出不可区分性（OI），以及全知预测器和泛预测器。

Result: 给出了确定性预测器，达到与随机化预测器相同的 $\widetilde O(\varepsilon^{-3})$ 极小最大最优样本复杂度，并解决了多个公开问题。

Conclusion: 随机化并非多校准最优样本复杂度的必要条件，确定性预测器同样可以达到最优。

Abstract: A model is multicalibrated on a collection of group weights $G$ if it is calibrated -- i.e. unbiased even conditional on its prediction -- not just overall, but also after reweighting contexts by each $g \in G$. It is a useful property for many downstream applications and is a basic desideratum of trustworthy machine learning. Before this work, all predictors known to attain the minimax-optimal $\widetilde O(\varepsilon^{-3})$ sample complexity rate for $\varepsilon$-multicalibration were randomized, while deterministic predictors were known only with substantially worse sample complexity. Whether randomization is necessary for optimal sample complexity in multicalibration was explicitly asked by [CLNR26] and implicitly in several prior works.
  We resolve this open problem by giving a minimax-optimal multicalibration algorithm that outputs a deterministic predictor. We then generalize the algorithm to produce optimal deterministic predictors that satisfy outcome indistinguishability (OI) with respect to finite or finitely covered collections of tests. As an application, this also gives deterministic omnipredictors and panpredictors with optimal sample complexity, resolving open problems posed by [OKK25] and [BHHLZ25].

</details>


### [235] [How Transparent is DiffusionGemma?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20560)
*Joshua Engels,Callum McDougall,Bilal Chughtai,Janos Kramar,Senthoran Rajamanoharan,Cindy Wu,Arthur Conmy,Asic Q Chen,Jean Tarbouriech,Min Ma,Brendan O'Donoghue,João Gabriel Lopes de Oliveira,Rohin Shah,Neel Nanda*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了DiffusionGemma（一种扩散语言模型）的推理透明度，将其分解为变量透明度和算法透明度两个维度。尽管扩散模型在连续潜空间中计算更多，但通过可解释的token瓶颈压缩信息，可将不透明序列深度降至仅Gemma 4的1.1倍。然而算法透明度上扩散模型更差（因所有token可同步变化）。作者通过可解释性案例研究发现了非时序推理、token/序列涂抹、中间上下文推理等新现象，并证明DiffusionGemma的可监控性与Gemma 4相当。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究扩散语言模型（DiffusionGemma）在连续潜空间中进行大量计算是否会导致推理透明度降低，这是一个关键的安全与可解释性问题。

Method: 将透明度分解为变量透明度（中间状态可理解性）和算法透明度（可重建推理过程）；通过构建可解释的token瓶颈（不降低下游性能）映射去噪步骤间的信息流；开展一组可解释性案例研究；对比评估可监控性。

Result: 变量透明度：通过token瓶颈将扩散模型的不透明序列深度从28.6倍Gemma 4降至1.1倍；算法透明度：扩散模型因所有token同步更新而更难解释；发现了非时序推理、token/序列涂抹、中间上下文推理等扩散特有现象；可监控性与Gemma 4相当。

Conclusion: 虽然扩散模型的算法透明度面临独特挑战（所有token同时变化），但其变量透明度可通过token瓶颈显著改善，且可监控性与自回归模型相当，表明扩散语言模型可以在不牺牲透明度关键维度的情况下获得优势。

Abstract: LLM reasoning transparency is a critical affordance for understanding model decisions, mitigating misuse and misalignment, and debugging surprising model behaviors. However, DiffusionGemma performs a larger fraction of its computation in a continuous latent space; does this make its reasoning less transparent? We study this question by decomposing transparency into two components: variable transparency, whether we understand intermediate snapshots of a model's computational state; and algorithmic transparency, whether we can use these snapshots to reconstruct the process by which the model arrived at its outputs. Naively, DiffusionGemma has poor variable transparency: its opaque serial depth, the amount of serial computation that occurs in between interpretable model states, seems at first 28.6X higher than the corresponding autoregressive Gemma 4 model. However, we show that we can map the information flowing between denoising steps through an interpretable token bottleneck with no decrease in downstream performance. Treating these intermediate states as interpretable reduces the opaque serial depth to just 1.1X that of Gemma 4. Algorithmic transparency is harder for diffusion models than for autoregressive models because all token predictions in the canvas can change at every denoising step, giving the model the power to implement complicated distributed algorithms during the denoising process. To begin bridging this gap, we conduct a suite of interpretability case studies, uncovering initial evidence of novel diffusion-specific phenomena such as non-chronological reasoning, token and sequence smearing, and intermediate-context reasoning. Finally, we test monitorability, a key application of transparency that measures whether model outputs are useful for downstream tasks. We find that DiffusionGemma is similarly monitorable to Gemma 4.

</details>


<div id='cs.CR'></div>

# cs.CR [[Back]](#toc)

### [236] [Secure Coding Drift in LLM-Assisted Post-Quantum Cryptography Development: A Gamified Fix](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19474)
*R. D. N. Shakya,C. P. Wijesiriwardana,S. M. Vidanagamachchi,Nalin A. G. Arachchilage*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出"后量子密码学中的安全编码漂移"这一新概念，描述因持续依赖LLM生成代码而导致安全编码实践逐渐退化的社会技术漏洞模型，并提出一种游戏化、LLM增强的安全编码框架作为解决方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 后量子密码学（PQC）的迁移需要严格的常时间执行、侧信道抵抗和精准参数化等安全编码实践，而LLM在软件工程中广泛应用但经常生成不安全代码。现有研究仅关注静态漏洞，缺乏对长期人机交互中安全编码退化这一动态现象的认识。

Method: 提出Secure Coding Drift in PQC社会技术漏洞模型，将安全风险概念化为纵向行为现象；设计游戏化、LLM增强的安全编码框架，集成对抗性评估、行为反馈和安全评分。

Result: 提出了一种将LLM从被动助手转变为主动安全协作者的新框架，但文中未给出具体的实验验证结果。

Conclusion: 通过将安全机制嵌入开发流程，该框架有助于在AI中介环境下实现更安全的PQC实现。

Abstract: The transition to Post Quantum Cryptography (PQC) introduces considerable implementation complexity, requiring strict adherence to constant-time execution, side channel resistance, and precise parametrisation. Simultaneously, large language models (LLMs) are heavily embedded in software development workflows, including cryptographic engineering. While LLMs improve productivity, evidence shows that they frequently generate insecure or suboptimal code, particularly in security critical domains. This paper introduces Secure Coding Drift in PQC, a novel socio technical vulnerability model capturing the gradual degradation of secure coding practices due to sustained reliance on LLM-generated code. Unlike prior work that focuses on static vulnerabilities, we conceptualise security risk as a longitudinal behavioural phenomenon rising from human AI interaction. To mitigate this, we propose a gamified, LLM augmented secure coding framework that embeds adversarial evaluation, behavioural feedback, and security scoring into development workflows. Our approach reframes LLMs from passive assistants into active security co-pilots, contributing toward safer PQC implementation in AI mediated environments.

</details>


### [237] [FloatDoor: Platform-Triggered Backdoors in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19535)
*Nils Loose,Jonas Sander,Felix Mächtle,Thomas Eisenbarth*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出FloatDoor，首个输入无关、平台触发的针对生成式LLM的后门攻击。通过两个轻量级LoRA适配器放大跨平台数值差异并绑定恶意行为，攻击者可在特定部署平台上触发模型输出恶意结果（如生成漏洞代码），且在其他平台表现正常，利用模型审计与部署之间的TOCTOU间隙实现攻击。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在不同部署平台（如GPU、TPU、CPU）上因浮点运算非结合性和内核实现差异会产生输出差异，这种平台依赖性目前尚未被系统性地从安全角度研究。作者旨在揭示这一现象带来的新型攻击面。

Method: FloatDoor由两个轻量级LoRA适配器组成：(1) 放大适配器——放大不同平台间的数值发散差异，形成可区分的平台签名；(2) 行为绑定适配器——将平台签名与恶意下游任务绑定。攻击利用模型审计（time-of-check）与实际部署（time-of-use）之间的时间差，使模型在审计时表现正常，在目标部署平台触发恶意行为。

Result: 在Qwen3-4B模型上，针对NVIDIA GPU、Google TPU、AWS Graviton、阿里云Yitian-710等多种部署目标进行实验，成功实现了平台触发的后门攻击。案例研究表明，FloatDoor能可靠地在目标平台上诱导生成可利用的代码漏洞。

Conclusion: FloatDoor建立了一类新的LLM部署攻击，揭示了跨平台数值差异可被恶意利用的安全风险，强调了在敏感LLM应用中建立可信模型供应链的迫切性。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly deployed in sensitive settings such as software engineering, where their outputs directly shape downstream artifacts. Recent work has shown that an identical model can produce measurably different outputs depending on the deployment platform, a consequence of non-associative floating-point arithmetic and divergent kernel implementations. We study the security implications of this platform-dependent variability and uncover a novel attack surface on LLM deployments. We introduce FloatDoor, the first input-independent, platform-triggered backdoor attack against generative LLMs. The compromised model exhibits adversary-chosen behavior when served on a target platform and is otherwise benign. FloatDoor is realized through two lightweight LoRA adapters, one that amplifies inter-platform numerical divergence and one that binds the resulting platform signature to a malicious downstream task, while leaving aggregate model utility largely intact. FloatDoor exploits a pronounced time-of-check, time-of-use gap between model auditing and serving. We demonstrate FloatDoor on Qwen3-4B across a broad range of deployment targets, including NVIDIA GPUs, Google TPUs, AWS Graviton, and Alibaba Yitian-710. As a final case study, we show that FloatDoor reliably induces exploitable code vulnerabilities on a chosen target platform. Our results establish a new class of attacks on LLM deployments and underscore the pressing need for trusted model supply chains in sensitive, LLM-powered applications.

</details>


### [238] [G-Lox: Group-Adaptive, Privacy-Preserving Bridge Distribution with Two-Party Computation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19620)
*Baigang Chen,Nicholas Hopper*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: G-Lox是一个保持Lox式分发者盲化的组自适应桥分发系统，通过双服务器隐私墙实现隐藏的、有状态的组级自适应，在保持低客户端开销的同时提升了抵御封锁和Sybil攻击的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有桥分发系统（如Lox）虽然实现了分发者盲化，但缺乏组级自适应能力；而引入自适应通常需要暴露组标识符或分配信息，破坏了隐私保证。因此需要一种既能保持盲化特性，又能实现隐藏的、有状态的组级自适应桥分发方案。

Method: G-Lox将自适应分配逻辑置于双服务器隐私墙之后，使得任何单一服务器都无法获知组标识符或组到桥的分配关系。私有状态访问和状态相关更新采用双服务器DPF/FSS协议和安全两方计算（2PC），支持封锁报告、传输感知重分配和隐私保护的组拆分。

Result: 在C++/EMP实现中，私有状态访问的客户端可见开销很低：状态规模达2^16时，每次迭代通信量保持在低KiB范围。在M=1024时，客户端发送1,968字节、接收1,280字节，每次迭代完成约0.25秒。仿真结果显示，在维持广泛分发的系统中，G-Lox相比Lox和rBridge类基线具有更强的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: G-Lox成功在保持分发者盲化的前提下实现了隐藏的组级自适应，使得桥分发系统能够更鲁棒地应对组特异性封锁和Sybil枚举攻击，同时维持较低的客户端开销。

Abstract: We present G-Lox (group-adaptive Lox), a bridge-distribution system that preserves Lox-style distributor blindness while enabling hidden, stateful group-level adaptation. G-Lox places adaptive assignment logic behind a two-server privacy wall, so no single server learns group identifiers or group-to-bridge assignments. Private state access and state-dependent updates use two-server DPF/FSS protocols and secure two-party computation, supporting blockage reporting, transport-aware reassignment, and privacy-preserving group splitting.
  We evaluate G-Lox through system measurements and policy simulation. In our C++/EMP implementation over real TCP sockets, private state access has low client-visible overhead: across state sizes up to 2^16, communication remains in the low-KiB range per iteration. At M=1024, the client sends 1,968 bytes, receives 1,280 bytes, and completes an iteration in about 0.25 s. Simulations with group-specific blocking and Sybil enumeration show that G-Lox improves robustness over Lox- and rBridge-like baselines among systems that maintain broad issuance.

</details>


### [239] [PUFFERDOS: Efficient and Effective Attack String Generation for Regular Expression Denial of Service Vulnerabilities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19654)
*Shangzhi Xu,Ziqi Ding,Xiao Cheng,Yuekang Li,Nan Sun,Benjamin Turnbull,Shuangxiang Kan,Siqi Ma*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 针对现有ReDoS攻击字符串生成方法生成的攻击字符串在实际场景中不实用（假设不切实际的输入长度预算，且未在程序层面验证有效性），本文提出PUFFERDOS攻击字符串生成器，通过定义三种脆弱模式、合成攻击字符串，并结合ReDoS特定组合符号执行进行精炼和验证，生成在实际长度预算内可行且在程序层面验证有效的攻击输入。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有ReDoS攻击字符串生成方法存在两个主要缺陷：一是假设不切实际的输入长度预算，二是未在程序层面验证攻击的有效性和效率，导致生成的字符串在实际程序中无法触发漏洞，实用性受限。

Method: 首先基于观察和形式化验证定义三种脆弱模式；然后根据这些模式进行合成技术生成攻击字符串；最后通过ReDoS特定的组合符号执行（compositional concolic execution）对字符串进行精炼和验证，确保在实际程序中的可利用性。

Result: PUFFERDOS能够生成在现实长度预算内可行且在程序层面验证有效的攻击字符串，实现对真实世界程序中ReDoS漏洞的有效利用。

Conclusion: 通过结合模式定义、合成技术与组合符号执行，PUFFERDOS克服了现有方法在实际应用中的局限性，显著提升了ReDoS攻击字符串的实用性和有效性。

Abstract: ReDoS attacks constitute a critical class of resource-exhaustion vulnerabilities. In such attacks, adversaries exploit the pathological worst-case execution behavior of regular expression (regex) engines to induce highly asymmetric computational workloads, ultimately exhausting system resources and degrading service availability. To protect systems against ReDoS attacks, numerous detection techniques have been proposed that simulate the attack process by generating attack strings to proactively exploit ReDoS vulnerabilities at the early development stage and facilitate remediation. Existing techniques broadly fall into two classes: static analyses that search for pathological regex structures, and dynamic exploration methods that synthesize candidate attack strings. However, the generated attack strings are often impractical for real-world exploitation because they usually assume unrealistic input-length budgets and do not validate the effectiveness and efficiency of the attack at the program level. Therefore, many generated strings fail to trigger vulnerable regexes when applied to real-world programs, further limiting the practical utility. To address these shortcomings, we introduce an effective and efficient attack string generator, PUFFERDOS, designed to synthesize attack inputs that are both feasible within realistic length budgets and validated at the program level, enabling effective exploitation of ReDoS vulnerabilities in real-world programs. Specifically, we first define three vulnerable patterns based on our observation and formal verification. According to the patterns, PUFFERDOS conducts a synthesis technique to generate attack strings, and then refines and validates the strings with ReDoS-specific compositional concolic execution to guarantee real-world exploitability.

</details>


### [240] [A Layered Security Framework Against Prompt Injection in RAG-Based Chatbots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19660)
*Gulshan Saleem,Nisar Ahmed,Muhammad Imran Zaman,Ali Hassan*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出一种三层次提示注入防御框架，分别在用户输入、上下文组装和模型输出阶段拦截直接与间接注入攻击，在GPT-4o、Llama 3和Mistral 7B上将攻击成功率从71.4%降至11.3%，假阳性率4.8%，中位延迟开销61.2ms。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有防御手段仅在管线的单一阶段运行，输入过滤器无法检查检索文档，输出监控无法阻止恶意载荷到达模型，导致RAG聊天机器人容易受到间接提示注入攻击（即知识库中被污染的文档可危害所有检索到它的用户）。

Method: 三层次框架：Layer 1用基于规则的模式库和微调语义异常分类器筛查用户输入；Layer 2在上下文组装阶段执行基于来源的指令层次结构，阻止检索内容覆盖操作策略；Layer 3用策略规则引擎和语义漂移检测器审计模型输出；持续审计循环聚合结构化日志并支持分类器再训练以适配新攻击模式。框架为模型无关的中间件，不修改底层LLM。

Result: 在5,080个样本上对GPT-4o、Llama 3、Mistral 7B评估，ASR从71.4%降至11.3%，比最佳单层基线低27.3个百分点，比已有护栏系统低23.8个百分点，假阳性率4.8%，中位延迟开销61.2ms。消融实验证实三层保护互补且联合效果超过各层贡献之和。

Conclusion: 提出的三层次框架有效解决了RAG系统中直接和间接提示注入问题，通过多阶段协同防御显著提升了安全性，同时保持了较低的开销和误报率，且具有模型无关和易于部署的优势。

Abstract: Prompt injection is ranked as the most critical vulnerability in large language model (LLM) deployments by the OWASP Top 10 for LLM Applications, yet existing defenses operate at isolated pipeline stages and remain incomplete. Input filters cannot inspect retrieved documents, while output monitors cannot prevent malicious payloads from reaching the model. Consequently, retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) chatbots remain vulnerable to indirect injection, where a poisoned knowledge-base document compromises every user whose query retrieves it. We present a three-layer framework that intercepts both direct and indirect prompt injection throughout the inference pipeline. Layer 1 screens user input using a rule-based pattern library and a fine-tuned semantic anomaly classifier. Layer 2 enforces a provenance-based instruction hierarchy during context assembly, preventing retrieved content from overriding operator policy. Layer 3 audits model output using a policy rule engine and semantic drift detector before delivery. A continuous audit loop aggregates structured logs and supports retraining to adapt the classifier to emerging attack patterns. The framework is model-agnostic and deploys as middleware without modifying the underlying LLM. Evaluation on 5,080 samples across GPT-4o, Llama 3, and Mistral 7B shows that the framework reduces Attack Success Rate (ASR) from 71.4\% to 11.3\%, outperforming the best single-layer baseline by 27.3 percentage points and a published guardrail system by 23.8 percentage points, while maintaining a 4.8\% false positive rate and a median latency overhead of 61.2 ms. Ablation studies confirm that all three layers provide complementary protection and that their combined effect exceeds the sum of individual contributions.

</details>


### [241] [When Global Gating Is Enough: Admission-Time Hubness Control in Anisotropic Vector Retrieval Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19692)
*Prashant Kumar Pathak,Tarun Kumar Sharma*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种在RAG系统中通过准入控制（admission-time control）来防御向量中心性（vector hubness）投毒攻击的方法。通过使用哨兵查询（sentinel queries）对候选文档进行评分，并在插入前隔离类似中心（hub-like）的文档。在10万文档规模、5种编码器和不同攻击者/防御者查询集下，全局门控（global gate）在决定性嵌入空间点实现召回率1.0，有效范围≥0.92，HotFlip攻击下召回率0.91±0.07，假阳性率1%。阈值可增量维护，插入成本与语料库大小无关。在HNSW上，准入控制仅增加约3.1%的摄入延迟，评分扩展到10^6向量时仍保持平坦。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RAG系统中，向量中心性（少数点成为许多查询的最近邻）会产生投毒风险：一个注入的文档可以影响不相关的请求。现有防御使用周期性反向kNN扫描，存在暴露窗口和重复的全语料库工作，需要更高效的防御方法。

Method: 提出准入控制方法，在文档插入前用哨兵查询集对候选文档评分，将中心性过高的文档隔离。设计了全局门控（global gate）和逐主题门控（per-topic gate）两种变体，阈值可增量维护。使用两个10万文档语料库、五种编码器和分离的攻击者/防御者查询集进行实验。

Result: 全局门控在决定性嵌入空间点召回率1.0，有效范围≥0.92，HotFlip攻击召回率0.91±0.07，假阳性率1%。逐主题门控无可靠收益。在HNSW上，准入控制增加约3.1%摄入延迟，评分扩展到10^6向量时保持平坦，仅有1.2%的决策在近似索引下翻转，且均不涉及攻击。

Conclusion: 准入控制能有效防御RAG中的中心性投毒攻击，具有高召回率、低假阳性率和可扩展性。逐主题门控由于各向异性（anisotropy）导致本地和全局可见性耦合而无可靠收益。溯源（provenance）可作为自然或紧密领域中心的补充防御。

Abstract: Vector hubness, where a few points become nearest neighbors of many queries, creates a poisoning risk in retrieval-augmented generation (RAG): one injected document can influence unrelated requests. Existing defenses use periodic reverse-kNN scans, leaving an exposure window and repeated corpus-wide work. We study admission-time control, scoring each candidate against sentinel queries and quarantining hub-like documents before insertion. Across two 100,000-document corpora, five encoders, and disjoint attacker and defender query sets, a global gate achieves recall 1.0 at the decisive embedding-space point (>=0.92 across the effective range) and 0.91 +/- 0.07 on HotFlip attacks, with 1% false positives on general documents. A per-topic gate provides no reliable benefit, consistent with anisotropy coupling local and global visibility. Thresholds are maintained incrementally, with corpus-size-independent insertion cost and amortized deletion cost. On HNSW, admission adds about 3.1% to ingestion latency, scoring remains flat to 10^6 vectors, and 1.2% of decisions flip under approximate indexing, none involving attacks. Provenance complements the gate for natural or tight-domain hubs.

</details>


### [242] [SafeSpec: Fast and Safe LLM via Dynamic Reflective Sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19755)
*Haotian Xu,Zeyang Zhang,Linbao Li,Huadi Zheng,Yu Li,Cheng Zhuo*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: SafeSpec提出了一种安全感知的推测推理框架，通过在目标模型上附加轻量级安全头，在单个前向传播中联合评估语义有效性和安全性，并通过回滚和安全导向的反射多采样恢复安全延续，从而在不牺牲推测解码加速优势的前提下实现推理时安全对齐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有安全防御与推测推理不兼容，要么引入额外计算开销，要么破坏草稿-验证机制，抵消加速收益。当前安全方法与推测解码之间存在根本性的不兼容问题。

Method: 1) 在目标模型上附加轻量级潜在安全头，在单次前向传播中联合评估语义有效性和安全性；2) 检测到不安全生成时，执行回滚和安全引导的反射多采样以恢复安全延续，而非终止生成；3) 将越狱攻击建模为生成轨迹上的分布偏移，进行风险感知轨迹恢复。

Result: 在多个模型和对抗性基准上，SafeSpec显著提升了安全-效率权衡。在Qwen3-32B上，攻击成功率降低15%，同时在良性负载上保持2.06倍的推理加速。

Conclusion: 推测加速和推理时安全可以联合优化，SafeSpec证明了安全感知的推测推理框架能够在不牺牲加速收益的前提下实现有效的安全防御。

Abstract: Speculative inference accelerates large language model (LLM) decoding but provides no inherent safety guarantees. Existing safety defenses are largely incompatible with speculative inference: they either introduce additional computation or disrupt the draft-verify mechanism, negating acceleration benefits. This reveals a fundamental incompatibility between current safety methods and speculative decoding. We propose SafeSpec, a safety-aware speculative inference framework that integrates risk estimation directly into the verification process. SafeSpec attaches a lightweight latent safety head to the target model to jointly evaluate semantic validity and safety in a single forward pass. When unsafe generations are detected, SafeSpec applies rollback and safety-guided reflective multi-sampling to recover safe continuations rather than terminating generation. We model jailbreak attacks as distributional shifts over generative trajectories, where adversarial prompts increase the probability of harmful continuations without eliminating safe ones. Under this model, SafeSpec performs risk-aware trajectory recovery within the speculative decoding process. Across multiple models and adversarial benchmarks, SafeSpec achieves a substantially improved safety-efficiency trade-off. On Qwen3-32B, SafeSpec reduces attack success rates by 15% while preserving a 2.06x inference speedup on benign workloads, demonstrating that speculative acceleration and inference-time safety can be jointly optimized.

</details>


### [243] [DISARM: Target Electronic Device Informed Mitigation of Software Runtime Side-Channel Vulnerabilities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19807)
*Tasneem Suha,Tanzim Mahfuz,Rima Asmar Awad,Prabuddha Chakraborty*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: DISARM是一种联合硬件-软件的方法，利用真实嵌入式设备的时序值生成针对性软件修复，以缓解运行时侧信道漏洞，在22个标准基准测试中优于现有方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有运行时侧信道攻击缓解方案在修复过程中未考虑目标程序运行的底层硬件或设备，导致过度修复、修复不足甚至失败。

Method: 提出DISARM，一种联合硬件-软件方法，从真实嵌入式设备获取时序值，生成针对性的软件修复，支持C、C++和Java源码。

Result: 在22个标准基准测试及5种不同嵌入式/边缘设备上，DISARM在执行时间开销、代码大小开销和正确性方面均优于PENDULUM和DifFuzzAR等现有方案。

Conclusion: DISARM通过结合真实硬件时序信息进行软件修复，有效解决了现有方法不考虑底层硬件导致的各种问题。

Abstract: Program runtime or timing attacks exploit variations in a program's execution times to extract sensitive information from the program (e.g. encryption keys, sensitive variable data, intellectual property). State-of-the-art solutions to runtime side-channel attacks attempt to balance the execution time of the sensitive code for different control flow paths to eliminate the timing leakage. However, during the mitigation process, most techniques do not consider the underlying hardware or device on which the target program is supposed to run on. This can lead to over-fixing (unnecessary extra operations), under-fixing (not solving the imbalance properly), and even failures. We propose DISARM, a joint hardware-software methodology (unlike any existing solution) for mitigating runtime side-channel vulnerabilities that utilizes timing values from real embedded devices to generate targeted software fixes. We implement DISARM to support C, C++, and Java source codes and validate it across 22 standard benchmarks. DISARM outperforms state-of-the-art solutions such as PENDULUM and DifFuzzAR in terms of execution time overhead, code size overhead, and correctness on five different embedded or edge devices.

</details>


### [244] [Heterogeneous LLM Debate Under Adversarial Peers: Honest Gains, Replacement Costs, and Resilience](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19826)
*Prashanti Nilayam,Kiran Kumar Ramanna,Prashil Tumbade,Sankalp Nayak*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文研究异构LLM辩论中，诚实异构同伴能降低有害修订（如Llama-3.1-70B上从89%降至35%），而对抗性同伴则逆转此效果。异构性既是攻击面，也是防御手段——当已有同族对手时，诚实异构同伴能减少有害修订和正确答案丢失率（翻转率从31%降至6%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 异构LLM辩论虽有望通过多样化同伴相互纠正，但同一交流过程既传递纠正也带来对抗性影响。作者旨在量化哪种影响占主导，即诚实异构同伴是主导纠正还是引入有害影响。

Method: 通过追踪异构同伴如何改变诚实智能体的修订行为（修订频率、修订是有益还是有害），比较同质基线、诚实混合、对抗混合以及已受同族对手污染的混合面板，覆盖四个模型家族和三个推理基准。

Result: 诚实异构同伴显著降低有害修订率（Llama-3.1-70B在MATH-hard上从89%降至35%），对抗异构同伴则恢复至90%。条件率在弱防御者上隐藏伤害，但辩论结束翻转率可暴露。当已有同族对手时，诚实异构同伴将正确答案翻转率从31%降至6%。

Conclusion: 异构性不仅是攻击面，在已有对手时也是防御手段。模式跨模型家族和基准保持符号一致，幅度随防御者-基准组合变化。

Abstract: Heterogeneous LLM debate is motivated by the promise that diverse peers correct one another, but the same exchange that carries correction also carries adversarial influence. We measure which dominates by tracking how a heterogeneous peer changes the honest agents' revision behavior: how often they change their answer, and whether the change is corrective or harmful. We compare matched panels (homogeneous baseline, honest-mixed, and adversarial-mixed) and contaminated panels in which a malicious same-family peer is already present, spanning four model families and three reasoning benchmarks. An honest heterogeneous peer sharply lowers harmful revision, and an adversarial one reverses it. For Llama-3.1-70B defenders on MATH-hard, the honest-slot harmful-revision rate falls from 89% in the homogeneous panel to 35% with an honest peer, and an adversarial peer returns it to 90%. The conditional rate hides this damage on weak defenders, but the end-of-debate flip rate exposes it. The pattern keeps its sign across families and benchmarks while its magnitude varies with the defender-benchmark regime. We also measure the effects when an adversarial same-family peer is already present: an honest heterogeneous peer lowers both harmful revision and the rate at which initially-correct answers are lost. On the same Llama-3.1-70B setting, the added honest peer cuts the flip rate on initially-correct items from 31% under a same-family adversary to 6%. Heterogeneity is therefore not only an attack surface but, when an adversary is already present, also a defense.

</details>


### [245] [Low-Cost Multi-Precision Systolic Arrays for Accelerating FHE NTTs on AI ASICs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19866)
*George Alexakis,Dimitrios Schoinianakis,Giorgos Dimitrakopoulos*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出一种最小化修改的多精度脉动阵列架构，通过在阵列内部原生执行全精度输出重构，解决了FHE在TPU等AI硬件上因精度不匹配导致的性能瓶颈，在7nm工艺下实现1.33倍以上加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法通过矩阵分解在低精度矩阵引擎上执行NTT计算，但全精度重构需从矩阵引擎卸载到向量处理器，破坏了矩阵乘法的数据流，造成严重的性能瓶颈。

Method: 提出最小化修改的多精度脉动阵列，在阵列内部以统一数据流同步执行低精度矩阵乘法与全精度输出重构，实现原生全精度重构无需卸载计算。

Result: 在7nm工艺下硬件开销极小；对128x128矩阵引擎，在2^12至2^16变换规模上实现至少1.33倍加速。

Conclusion: 所提架构成功使标准AI硬件支持高精度FHE加速，且硬件修改极小，在数据隐私保护与计算效率之间取得了有效平衡。

Abstract: Fully Homomorphic Encryption (FHE) ensures robust data privacy but suffers from prohibitive computational overhead. Accelerating FHE on AI hardware like Tensor Processing Units (TPUs) is promising, yet fundamentally limited by a precision mismatch: TPUs are optimized for 8-bit arithmetic, whereas FHE and its critical parts such as the Number Theoretic Transform (NTT), demand high precision. Current approaches bridge this gap using matrix decomposition to execute NTT computations on low-precision matrix engines. However, reconstructing the full-precision results requires shift-and-add accumulation that does not match the dataflow of matrix multiplication. This forces offloading full-precision reconstruction from matrix engines to vector processors that disrupts the matrix multiplication dataflow, creating significant performance bottleneck. To resolve this limitation, we propose a minimally modified multi-precision systolic array that performs full-precision output reconstruction natively within the array in sync with low-precision matrix multiplication under a uniform dataflow. Synthesized at 7nm with OpenRoad, our design incurs negligible hardware overhead. Cycle-accurate simulations using SCALE-Sim demonstrate that natively executing NTTs on the proposed architecture achieves at least 1.33x speedup, for transform sizes 2^12 to 2^16 on 128x128 matrix engines, successfully enabling standard AI hardware to support high-precision FHE acceleration.

</details>


### [246] [FFinRED: An Expert-Guided Benchmark Generation and Evaluation Framework for Financial LLM Red-Teaming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19887)
*Chaeyun Kim,Daeyoung Park,Junghwan Kim,Jinyoung Jeong,Eunji Song,Yongtaek Lim,Minwoo Kim*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: FinRED是一个由金融专家指导的红色团队框架，用于金融LLM安全评估。它采用双层分类法映射全球金融标准（如FATF、EU DORA），通过可扩展流程将真实金融文档转化为上下文丰富的红队行为提示（种子），并提供专家验证的金融专用评估标准，已部署于韩国金融安全研究院（FSI）监管沙盒。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有安全基准针对通用对抗场景，但缺乏金融专用风险评估。金融LLM面临监管合规违规、欺诈助力和系统性信任侵蚀等特殊风险，亟需针对性的评估框架。

Method: 提出FinRED框架：1）构建两层级分类法，将全球标准（FATF、EU DORA）映射至金融威胁；2）开发可扩展流水线，将真实金融文档通过专家定义模式转化为红队行为提示（种子）；3）制定专家验证的金融专用评估标准，超越简单的免责声明检查。

Result: 专家验证确认种子提示的合理性和真实性；金融专用评估标准比静态通用标准更接近人类专家判断，将关键假阴性从28降至12；框架已部署于韩国FSI监管沙盒。

Conclusion: FinRED与国际风险管理与信息安全标准（如ISO/IEC 27001）对齐，通过门控访问机制缓解双重用途风险，为金融LLM安全评估提供了系统性解决方案。

Abstract: Existing safety benchmarks target general adversarial scenarios but miss finance-specific risks. Financial LLMs face regulatory compliance violations, fraud facilitation, and systemic trust erosion that require targeted evaluation. We introduce FinRED, an expert-guided red-teaming framework for financial LLM safety evaluation developed with financial experts. FinRED uses a novel two-level taxonomy mapping global standards (e.g., FATF and EU DORA) to threats ranging from regulatory evasion to complex fraud, integrated with a scalable pipeline that converts real financial documents into context-rich red-teaming Behavioral Prompts (seeds) through an expert-defined schema. Rigorous expert validation confirms seed plausibility and realism for meaningful LLM safety evaluation. We also provide an expert-validated, finance-specific rubric that goes beyond disclaimer checks, aligns more closely with human experts than static one-size-fits-all rubrics, and reduces critical false negatives from 28 to 12. Aligned with internationally adopted risk-management and information-security standards (e.g., ISO/IEC 27001), FinRED is deployed in South Korea's Financial Security Institute (FSI) regulatory sandbox for generative AI security evaluation in real financial services. To mitigate dual-use risks, the dataset, generation pipeline, prompt template, and evaluation framework are gated for qualified researchers at https://github.com/selectstar-ai/FinRED-paper and https://huggingface.co/datasets/datumo/FinRED.

</details>


### [247] [AutoTam: Specifying Secure Protocol Implementations with Tamarin Model Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19937)
*Johannes Wilson,Mikael Asplund,Niklas Johansson*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出一种语言优先的形式化验证方法，通过领域特定语言实现协议，自动生成Tamarin证明模型并验证迹属性，同时结合符号执行分析内存安全，在Diffie-Hellman签名协议和WireGuard VPN协议上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有形式化验证工具虽然减少了验证工作量，但建模对缺乏形式化背景的从业者仍然困难，且将验证结果迁移到具体实现需要专家知识。

Method: 提出语言优先的方法：设计领域特定语言（DSL）用于协议实现，自动生成Tamarin证明器所需的验证模型，证明已验证的通用迹属性可反翻译回实现，并集成符号执行分析内存安全。

Result: 实现了签名Diffie-Hellman协议和WireGuard VPN协议的准确模型，WireGuard实现与现有实现在解释器下可互操作且性能可接受，通过符号执行和Tamarin模型验证证明了实现的安全性。

Conclusion: 该语言优先方法降低了形式化验证的门槛，实现了从实现到验证模型再到安全证明的自动化流程，且验证结果可映射回具体实现。

Abstract: Formal verification is a challenging but important task for ensuring the security of cryptographic protocols. While modern protocol verification tools significantly reduce verification effort, modelling remains challenging to practitioners without a background in formal verification. In addition, transferring verification results to a concrete protocol implementation requires expert knowledge.
  In this paper, we present a novel language-first method for verification of trace properties using a domain-specific language for protocol implementations. We target the Tamarin prover for verification, and we prove that verified universal trace properties translate back to the implementation. We additionally integrate symbolic execution in order to analyse the memory safety of protocol implementations. We use our tool to implement and generate accurate models for a signed Diffie-Hellman protocol, and for the WireGuard VPN protocol. Our WireGuard implementation is interoperable with existing implementations when using our interpreter, and achieves acceptable performance. We formally prove our implementations secure using a combination of symbolic execution and verification of the generated Tamarin models.

</details>


### [248] [A Measurement Study of Cryptographic Misuse in Embodied AI Mobile Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19983)
*Junchao Li,Xuelei Wang,Yuhang Huang,Qi Wang,Boyang Ma,Xuelong Dai,Minghui Xu,Yue Zhang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文首次对具身智能移动应用中的密码学误用进行大规模测量研究，构建了包含507个真实应用的EAIAppZoo基准数据集，通过自动化语义分析发现12,975个密码学误用实例（精确率80.74%），揭示了EAI特有的工程约束导致的密码学缺陷，以及延迟敏感控制路径与本地硬编码凭证等结构性安全权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有安全研究主要关注具身AI设备和云基础设施，而移动控制层作为关键攻击面被严重忽视。随着EAI移动应用从辅助界面演变为主动控制路径组件，移动端密码学安全直接关系到网络物理系统的信任基础，亟需系统性研究。

Method: 构建EAIAppZoo基准数据集（507个应用，覆盖6个EAI领域），开发自动化语义感知分析流水线，测量五大类密码学失效模式的普遍性和特征。

Result: 检测到12,975个密码学误用实例，评估精确率为80.74%；发现这些缺陷源于EAI特有的工程约束而非随机开发错误；揭示了延迟敏感控制路径削弱传输保护、离线设备配置和遗留IoT SDK导致本地凭证硬编码等结构性安全权衡。

Conclusion: 移动应用已成为网络物理系统中脆弱但被忽视的密码学信任边界，移动端密码学缺陷可绕过标称的网络防护，使攻击者能够拦截命令通道并劫持EAI实体的物理控制。

Abstract: Embodied AI (EAI) mobile applications are evolving from auxiliary user interfaces into active control-path components, directly linking mobile-side cryptographic security to cyber-physical trust. Despite this shift, existing security research predominantly focuses on embodied AI devices and cloud infrastructures, leaving the mobile control layer largely unexplored as a critical attack surface. To bridge this gap, we present the first large-scale measurement study of cryptographic misuse within the EAI mobile ecosystem. We construct EAIAppZoo, a benchmark of 507 real-world applications across six EAI domains, and employ an automated semantic-aware analysis pipeline to measure the prevalence and characteristics of five major cryptographic failure modes. Our measurement yields 12,975 misuse findings (with an evaluated precision of 80.74\%), revealing that these cryptographic failures are driven by EAI-specific engineering constraints rather than random developer errors. We uncover structural security trade-offs: latency-sensitive control paths systematically weaken transport protection, while the heavy reliance on offline device provisioning and legacy IoT SDKs exacerbates the local hardcoding of authentication credentials. Through real-world case studies, we demonstrate how these mobile-side cryptographic flaws bypass nominal network protections, enabling adversaries to intercept command channels and hijack the physical control of EAI entities. Ultimately, our findings highlight that mobile applications have become a fragile, yet overlooked, cryptographic trust boundary in cyber-physical systems.

</details>


### [249] [Accelerating Trust Convergence in IIoT: A ML Approach for Dynamic Network Conditions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20214)
*Aymen Bouferroum,Valeria Loscri,Abderrahim Benslimane*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为TCA（信任收敛加速）的动态信任管理方法，通过集成机器学习预测信任收敛所需时间单元，并动态调整信任模型中的转移概率，在工业物联网（IIoT）资源受限及网络质量波动的环境下，实现了高达28.6%的信任收敛时间缩减，同时提升了对恶意节点的评估鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统信任模型忽视了IIoT环境中网络质量波动对信任收敛速度和评估准确性的影响，导致收敛慢、评估不准，亟需一种能适应动态网络条件的信任管理方案。

Method: 提出TCA方法，利用机器学习基于关键网络指标预测信任收敛所需时间单元，动态调整信任模型中的转移概率以加速收敛；仿真基于IEEE 802.11标准的真实Wi-Fi信道条件进行验证。

Result: 在恶劣网络条件下，信任收敛时间最多减少28.6%；在存在恶意节点的场景下，信任评估准确性得到提升。

Conclusion: TCA提供了一种可扩展、自适应的信任框架，能够在动态工业环境中确保IIoT系统在不同网络条件下的鲁棒性能。

Abstract: In Industrial Internet of Things (IIoT) environments, trust management plays a vital role in securing systems, especially when dealing with resource-constrained devices. Traditional trust models often overlook the impact of fluctuating network quality, leading to slower trust convergence and inaccurate assessments. In this paper, we propose a dynamic trust management solution, known as the Trust Convergence Acceleration (TCA) approach, which integrates Machine Learning (ML) to accelerate trust convergence under poor network conditions. Our model predicts the number of time units needed for trust convergence based on key network metrics and dynamically adapts transition probabilities in the trust model to enhance convergence speed. Using a simulation framework that incorporates realistic Wi-Fi channel conditions based on the IEEE 802.11 standard, we demonstrate the effectiveness of the TCA-based approach, achieving up to a 28.6% reduction in trust convergence time under challenging conditions. Furthermore, the proposed solution exhibits resilience in scenarios involving malicious nodes, improving trust evaluation accuracy. This work provides a scalable and adaptive trust framework for IIoT systems in dynamic industrial environments, ensuring robust performance under varying network conditions.

</details>


### [250] [GNSS Spoofing Threat for V2X communications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20215)
*Adolfo P. Jimenez,Juan Arquero-Gallego,Mario P. Luna,Jose E. Naranjo,Felipe Jimenez Alonso*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种使用低成本软件定义无线电（SDR）对GNSS信号进行物理欺骗的方法，并在真实V2X设备（Commsignia OBU和RSU）上通过三种速度场景（90、145、200 km/h）进行了实验验证，结果表明V2X通信易受GNSS欺骗攻击且无法被检测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GNSS信号极易受到欺骗攻击，而V2X通信依赖GNSS提供精确的PNT服务以生成协作感知消息（CAM）。现有研究缺乏在真实V2X硬件设备上验证GNSS欺骗攻击可行性的工作，因此需要证明该攻击的实际威胁性。

Method: 使用HackRF One SDR，通过Haversine距离计算、时间离散化模拟匀速运动、线性插值生成高保真GPS基带信号，构建坐标生成流水线，对Commsignia OBU和RSU设备执行物理层GNSS欺骗攻击，在三种速度（90、145、200 km/h）下模拟合成轨迹。

Result: 实验验证了在三种速度场景下，欺骗攻击成功使真实V2X设备接收虚假位置信息，导致服务降级，且攻击未被检测机制发现。

Conclusion: V2X通信缺乏对GNSS欺骗攻击的安全防护，存在严重安全隐患，需要引入额外的安全机制来检测和防御此类攻击。

Abstract: Global Navigation Satellite Systems (GNSS) constitute a core technology for delivering crucial positioning, navigation, and timing (PNT) services in the Vehicle-to-Everything (V2X) domain, where they are indispensable for generating Cooperative Awareness Messages (CAM) that uphold network reliability and vehicular safety. Yet, GNSS signals are acutely exposed to spoofing, an advanced attack in which an adversary transmits crafted signals that replicate legitimate satellite characteristics, misleading the receiver into computing a false position. This work presents a methodology for conducting physical spoofing with inexpensive Software Defined Radio (SDR), describing a coordinate generation pipeline that employs Haversine-based distance calculations, temporal discretization to emulate constant velocity, and linear interpolation to produce high-fidelity GPS baseband signals. The proposed attack is experimentally validated on real Commsignia OnBoard Unit (OBU) and RoadSide Unit (RSU) devices using a HackRF One across three scenarios that emulate synthetic trajectories at steady speeds of 90 km/h, 145 km/h, and 200 km/h. The most significant contribution of this paper is the demonstration that V2X communications are not secured, as they are susceptible to GNSS spoofing attacks, which cause service degradation without being detected.

</details>


### [251] [TrustMix: How to Mix Messages in a Mobile Ad-hoc Network](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20251)
*Yu Shen,Aiswarya Walter,Stefanie Roos*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: TrustMix 是一种无需中央可信方即可在移动自组织网络（MANET）中运行的混合协议，通过分组转发和洗牌机制实现匿名性，并利用可链接环签名限制消息速率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有混合网络通常面向基础设施网络，无法直接应用于MANET；而少数MANET解决方案需要预先了解拓扑结构或依赖可信中央方，这限制了其适用性。

Method: TrustMix 中用户加入多个分组，消息通过多个分组转发；用户只需找到附近可信的节点，将消息转发至该节点所在分组，分组内所有成员共同参与洗牌，使得原始消息与转发消息无法被关联；即使选中的节点是恶意的，只有其所在分组全部成员均为恶意时才能破坏匿名性；同时使用可链接环签名实现消息速率限制，检测超量发送而不暴露身份。

Result: 在随机预言机模型下证明了协议安全性；通过现有混合网络模拟器评估了匿名性，表明TrustMix显著改善了消息匿名性；在5台移动设备上实现了概念验证的Android原型，吞吐量可接受。

Conclusion: TrustMix 是首个无需中央可信方、不依赖预先拓扑知识的MANET混合协议，在保障匿名性的同时实现了速率限制，并通过理论与实验验证了其安全性和可行性。

Abstract: Mix networks are a highly effective way to achieve anonymity, defending against a wide range of traffic-analysis attacks. However, mix networks are usually designed for infrastructure networks and cannot be directly applied in the context of mobile ad hoc networks (MANETs). The few existing solutions for MANETs require advance knowledge of the topology or a trusted central party.
  In this paper, we present TrustMix, a mix protocol for MANETs that operates without any central trusted party. In TrustMix, parties join groups and then messages are forwarded via multiple groups to provide anonymity. With TrustMix, users only need to find a party nearby that they consider trusted. They then forward the message to this party's group, and the party shuffles messages before forwarding to other groups, meaning that the original message and the forwarded message cannot be linked. Furthermore, even if the chosen party is adversarial, they can only break the anonymity if all parties in their group are adversarial as all of them contribute to the shuffling. In addition to anonymity, TrustMix also enforces rate limits on the number of messages through the use of linkable ring signatures, which allows detecting that parties send more messages that allowed without revealing identities.
  We prove the security of our protocol in the random oracle model. We evaluate its anonymity using an existing mix-network simulator and show that TrustMix significantly improves message anonymity. Finally, we present a proof-of-concept Android implementation and show that TrustMix achieves acceptable throughput with 5 mobile devices.

</details>


### [252] [Quantization as a Malicious Task: Removing Quantization-Conditioned Backdoors via Task Arithmetic](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20254)
*Kaihsun Yang,Min-Yan Tsai,Chia-Mu Yu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出QVec，从参数空间视角防御量化后门攻击，仅需一次量化前向传播和轻量超参数搜索，无需重训练或触发样本，即可抑制后门激活并保持模型干净性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有量化后门防御方法通常修改量化过程或校正激活统计量，引入额外计算开销且依赖特定量化设置，缺乏高效通用防御手段。

Method: 将全精度模型与量化模型的权重差异视为恶意任务向量（而非随机量化噪声），通过受控的参数校正（抵消该恶意方向）实现防御，仅需一次量化前向传播估计参数偏移和轻量超参数搜索。

Result: 在图像分类基准和多个大语言模型攻击场景下，QVec持续抑制后门激活，同时保持干净性能。

Conclusion: QVec无需重训练、无需触发样本，仅需少量计算即可有效防御量化条件后门，为资源受限设备上的安全部署提供了实用方案。

Abstract: Model quantization is widely adopted to reduce memory usage and inference cost when deploying deep neural networks on resource-constrained devices. However, recent studies have revealed a new security threat known as Quantization-Conditioned Backdoors (QCBs), where a model behaves normally in full precision but activates malicious behavior only after quantization. Existing defenses typically modify quantization procedures or correct activation statistics, often introducing additional computational overhead or relying on specific quantization settings. Here, we present QVec, a parameter-space perspective for defending against QCBs. We observe that the weight difference between a full-precision model and its quantized counterpart encodes a structured behavioral shift, which can be interpreted as a malicious task vector rather than random quantization noise. Based on this insight, QVec counteracts this malicious direction through controlled parameter correction prior to deployment. QVec requires no retraining, no trigger samples, and only a single quantization pass to estimate the parameter shift, together with a lightweight hyperparameter search. Extensive experiments across image classification benchmarks and multiple Large Language Model (LLM) attack scenarios demonstrate that QVec consistently suppresses backdoor activation while preserving clean performance.

</details>


### [253] [LLM agent safety, multi-turn red-teaming, jailbreak benchmarks, adversarial robustness, safety-critical systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20408)
*Hanwool Lee,Dasol Choi,Bokyeong Kim,Seung Geun Kim,Haon Park*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出NRT-Bench，一个针对LLM智能体在安全关键系统（核电站控制室）中多轮红队攻防的基准测试。研究发现：自适应多轮攻击可可靠地使操作团队越过安全界限（8.7%-12.1%的会话导致关键安全功能丧失）；不同模型的脆弱性几乎互不相交（149次攻击中无一次击败全部四个模型）；防御效果高度依赖模型，同一防御对某模型有效但对另一模型可能适得其反。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管LLM智能体越来越多地被用作安全关键系统的监督组件，但其在持续、自适应对抗压力下的鲁棒性尚未被充分刻画。

Method: 构建NRT-Bench基准：模拟核电站控制室环境，包含五个角色（由可配置LLM驱动）的操作团队和六个关键安全函数（CSFs）；攻击者通过四个通道在多轮会话中注入消息，每轮有反馈；使用固定攻击配对回放协议评估四个前沿操作模型。

Result: 四个模型在8.7%-12.1%的攻击会话中失去关键安全函数；不同模型的失败案例几乎不重叠（149次攻击中无一次击败全部四个模型，但三分之一击败至少一个）；防御效果高度模型依赖，同一防御对某模型降低攻击成功率却对另一模型提高成功率。

Conclusion: LLM智能体作为安全关键系统监督组件时存在显著且模型间脆弱性不重叠的安全风险；防御措施效果因模型而异，甚至可能产生反效果；需基于具体模型评估防御策略。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents are increasingly proposed as supervisory components for safety-critical systems, yet their robustness under sustained, adaptive adversarial pressure remains poorly characterized. We present NRT-Bench, a benchmark for multi-turn red-teaming of LLM agents acting as operators of a safety-critical system, instantiated in a simulated nuclear power plant control room. A five-role operator team, each backed by a configurable LLM, runs a plant governed by six critical safety functions (CSFs), while adversaries inject messages over four channels in bounded multi-turn sessions with per-turn feedback. Harm is an objective signal rather than LLM-judged text: a run terminates the moment any CSF is lost, attributed to the causing message. Evaluating four frontier operator models under a fixed-attack paired-replay protocol, we find that adaptive multi-turn attacks reliably push the operator team past a safety limit: across the four models, between 8.7% and 12.1% of attack sessions end with the plant losing a critical safety function. Although the four models look almost equally robust by this aggregate rate, their failures barely overlap: of $149$ sessions, none defeat all four models while a third defeat at least one, so vulnerabilities are nearly disjoint across models rather than nested. The effect of added defences is strongly model-dependent: the same guardrail stack or safety-advisor agent that lowers attack success for one model can raise it for another. We release the simulation venue, attack dataset, and replay tooling for reproducible safety evaluation of LLM agents.

</details>


### [254] [Multi-View Decompilation for LLM-Based Malware Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20436)
*Bercan Turkmen,Vyas Raina*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出多反编译器提示方法：通过同时提供Ghidra和RetDec两种反编译器的伪C视图给LLM，可以提升恶意软件分类的F1分数（主要是召回率），且无需额外训练。实验表明不同反编译器会暴露同一二进制的不同特征，其错误具有互补性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于LLM的恶意软件分析管线通常依赖单一反编译器的输出，但反编译器是有损启发式工具，单一视角可能丢失关键信息，导致分类脆弱。作者希望探索多反编译器视角能否提供互补证据以提升分类效果。
