Method: 构建包含良性工具和恶意程序的基准数据集，每个样本分别用Ghidra和RetDec编译并反编译，得到匹配的伪C视图。在多个主流LLM家族上比较单一反编译器视角与多视角（联合输入两种伪C）的恶意分类性能。

Result: 提供两种反编译器视图能够提升恶意样本分类的F1分数，主要提升体现在召回率上。一致性分析显示Ghidra和RetDec产生的错误部分不同，证实了反编译器输出具有互补信息。

Conclusion: 多反编译器提示是一种简单、无需训练的方法，可以有效提升基于LLM的恶意软件分类在实际场景中的表现。

Abstract: Malware analysts often inspect compiled binaries through decompiled pseudo-C, when source code is unavailable. Recent work suggests that large language models (LLMs) can assist this process by classifying decompiled code as benign or malicious, but existing pipelines typically rely on a single decompiler view. We argue that this assumption is fragile: decompilers are lossy heuristic tools, and different decompilers can expose different artefacts of the same binary. We curate a benchmark of benign utilities and malicious programs spanning a range of threat behaviors. Each sample is compiled and decompiled with both Ghidra and RetDec, yielding matched pseudo-C views. Across a range of LLMs from major model families, we find that providing both decompiler views improves malicious-class F1, mainly by increasing recall on malicious samples. Agreement analyses further show that Ghidra and RetDec make partially different errors, supporting the view that decompiler outputs provide complementary evidence. Our results suggest that multi-decompiler prompting is a simple, training-free way to improve LLM-based malware triage in practical settings.

</details>


### [255] [Image Encryption Algorithm Based on Convolutional Neural Networks and Dynamic S-Box Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20444)
*Ans Ibrahim,Fadhil Abbas Fadhil,Mahameed Reza Feizi Derakhshi,Maryam Mahdi Alhusseini,Nikolai Safiullin*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出一种结合CNN与经典密码学的动态图像加密方法，通过CNN训练生成自适应S盒，替代传统固定S盒，提升混淆特性和抗攻击能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统固定S盒易受线性攻击和差分攻击，缺乏输入图像依赖性，安全性和灵活性不足。

Method: 利用CNN提取图像特征，基于学习到的特征生成自适应S盒，用于像素替换加密。

Result: 通过熵、直方图分析、相关性、NPCR、UACI等指标评估，CNN生成S盒表现出更高的非线性、唯一性和输入依赖性。

Conclusion: 该动态加密方案比传统方法更具弹性和灵活性，能有效抵抗统计攻击和结构攻击。

Abstract: The paper proposes a dynamic approach to image encryption, combining the use of Convolutional Neural Networks (CNNs) and classical cryptography to improve the security and flexibility of image encryption. The main concept is to create adaptive Substitution boxes (S-boxes) based on characteristics that are learned by a trained CNN. The CNN-based S-boxes can be relied on for more non-linearity, uniqueness, and input image dependence than the conventional fixed S-boxes because they are susceptible to the linear and differential attacks. This dynamic behaviour enhances the confusion property and makes it more resistant to statistical and structural attacks. The encryption algorithm consists of CNN-based feature extraction and the creation of a personalised S-box to replace the pixels. Entropy, histogram analysis, correlation, NPCR, and UACI enable security assessment of generated S-boxes based on the CNN, indicating that the scheme is more resilient and flexible than traditional ones.

</details>


### [256] [Analyzing Defensive Misdirection Against Model-Guided Automated Attacks on Agentic AI Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20470)
*Reza Soosahabi,Vivek Namsani*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文通过概率模型分析传统“检测-拦截”防御在自动化攻击下会失效（攻击成功率随查询预算趋近1），提出“检测-误导”策略，通过受控的误导性响应诱导攻击者判断器产生假阳性错误，使攻击成功率有界。基于该策略实现的上下文误导渐进交互方法（CMPE）可将攻击成功率上限降低两个数量级，并在PAIR和GPTFuzz攻击中几乎消除验证成功的攻击。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着Agentic AI系统越来越多依赖语言模型组件，提示注入和越狱攻击的后果愈发严重，尤其攻击者采用模型引导自动化进行规模化探测、提示优化和响应评估。传统检测-拦截防御存在根本性缺陷，需要新的防御思路。

Method: 建立概率模型分析目标系统、防御机制和攻击者自动判断器的交互关系；提出“检测-误导”策略（detect-and-misdirect），对检测到的恶意交互返回受控的、非操作性响应以诱导攻击者判断器产生假阳性错误；实现CMPE方法：用轻量级对话误导替代可预测的拒绝文本，产生安全但策略性误导的响应。

Result: 理论分析表明检测-拦截在查询预算增长时ASR趋近1；检测-误导可降低攻击者候选选择的阳性预测值，使渐近ASR有界。CMPE实验将ASR上限降低两个数量级，在PAIR和GPTFuzz攻击中几乎完全消除验证成功的攻击。

Conclusion: 检测-误导策略优于传统检测-拦截，为自动化越狱攻击提供了更鲁棒的防御范式，CMPE是实现该策略的有效轻量级方法。

Abstract: Agentic AI systems increasingly rely on language-model components to interpret instructions, process external data, invoke tools, and coordinate with other agents. These capabilities make prompt-injection and jailbreak attacks more consequential, especially as attackers adopt model-guided automation to scale probing, prompt refinement, and response evaluation. This work analyzes the resulting attack-defense setting through a probabilistic model of a target system, its defense mechanism, and the attacker's automated judge. Our analysis shows that conventional detect-and-block defenses can allow attacker success rate (ASR) to approach one as the query budget grows, since predictable refusals provide useful feedback to automated search. We then examine detect-and-misdirect, where detected malicious interactions receive controlled, non-operational responses designed to induce false-positive errors in the attacker's judge. This strategy reduces the positive predictive value of attacker-selected candidates and yields a bounded asymptotic ASR. We evaluate a proof-of-concept realization of this strategy through Contextual Misdirection via Progressive Engagement (CMPE), a lightweight conversational misdirection method designed to replace predictable refusal text with safe but strategically misleading responses in automated jailbreak settings. On jailbreak benchmarks, CMPE reduces estimated ASR upper bounds by up to two orders of magnitude and nearly eliminates verified attack success in end-to-end PAIR and GPTFuzz attack runs.

</details>


### [257] [A-COMPASS: Formal Foundations for Anonymity Analysis in Microdata](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20492)
*Tamara Tagliavia,Silvia Ghilezan*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出A-COMPASS语言，扩展COMPASS以支持微数据表（一条记录对应一个人）的隐私合规验证，支持匿名条件检查与匿名化操作，并验证k-匿名和l-多样性等属性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有隐私模型的形式化验证研究不足，COMPASS语言仅能操作预处理后的分组表（一组对应多人），无法直接处理微数据表（一人一条记录），且缺少匿名化执行能力。

Method: 将COMPASS语言改造为A-COMPASS，使其操作对象从分组表变为微数据表；新增匿名化操作执行功能；给出语法和语义定义，并证明确定性（determinism）和组合性（compositionality）等语义性质；提供k-匿名和l-多样性的验证机制。

Result: 成功定义A-COMPASS语言的语法与语义，证明了语义的确定性和组合性，并实现了对k-匿名和l-多样性等匿名属性的形式化验证。

Conclusion: A-COMPASS弥补了COMPASS无法直接处理微数据表及缺少匿名化操作的不足，为隐私模型的形式化验证提供了更实用的工具。

Abstract: In the information age, one of the leading problems is how to ensure individual's privacy. Depending on the context in which privacy is considered, various data privacy models have emerged. However, the domain of formal verification of these models is still not sufficiently explored even when it comes to the most basic models. An attempt to verify privacy requirements is the Compliance Assertion Language (COMPASS). In COMPASS, one can specify an anonymity condition that a table needs to satisfy, and an action that will modify the table if the condition is not satisfied. It is designed to operate on preprocessed tables in a form one record - one group of people. In this paper, we modify the COMPASS language in order to operate on microdata tables in their usual form of one record - one person. The modified language is called A-COMPASS. Along with checking of previously applied anonymity conditions, A-COMPASS enables the execution of anonymization actions as a new feature. We further provide the syntax and the semantics for the A-COMPASS language. We also prove the most important properties of the introduced semantics like determinism and compositionality. Finally, we provide a mechanism to verify anonymity properties, such as k-anonymity and l-diversity.

</details>


### [258] [Calibration Without Comprehension: Diagnosing the Limits of Fine-Tuning LLMs for Vulnerability Detection in Systems Software](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20502)
*Arastoo Zibaeirad,Marco Vieira*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出CWE-Trace框架，基于834个Linux内核漏洞样本评估LLM的漏洞检测能力。研究发现：数据污染无法带来可测量优势（84%的污染样本无可利用记忆信号），模型定向先验主导微调效果（DFI范围-85.5至+94.8个百分点），微调仅改变输出阈值而非决策策略。最佳检测得分仅52.1%，CWE Top-1准确率低于1.3%，表明当前LLM缺乏可靠的安全推理能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM在漏洞基准测试中取得高分，但其是否真正理解安全概念（而非模式匹配或数据污染）仍不清楚。需要严格的评估框架来区分真正的安全推理与记忆性表现。

Method: 构建CWE-Trace框架：(1) 834个手动筛选的Linux内核样本，覆盖74个CWE；(2) 严格时间分割（2025年前历史集/截止后无泄露集）；(3) 上下文感知的脆弱-补丁对；(4) 引入DFI（方向性失败指数）和HDD（层次距离与方向）两种诊断指标。评估8个原始LLM和15个LoRA微调变体，涵盖非目标检测、目标检测和CWE分类任务。

Result: (1) 数据污染无可测量优势：84%的污染样本缺乏可用记忆信号，~31%的污染样本CWE分类错误；(2) 模型定向先验主导微调：DFI范围-85.5至+94.8个百分点，从历史数据到截止后数据保持稳定；(3) 微调仅改变输出阈值而非决策策略——校准而非理解；(4) 最佳检测得分52.1%（仅高于随机基线2.1个百分点），CWE Top-1准确率低于1.3%。

Conclusion: 当前LLM缺乏针对系统软件（如Linux内核）的可靠安全推理能力，无论采用何种微调策略。检测与理解是解耦的能力，模型展现出"校准而不理解"的行为特征，即输出分布适应训练数据但底层安全推理缺失。

Abstract: Whether LLMs scoring well on vulnerability benchmarks genuinely reason about security or merely pattern-match on contaminated data remains unresolved. We present CWE-Trace, a framework for LLM vulnerability detection built from 834 manually curated Linux kernel samples spanning 74 CWEs. The framework enforces a strict temporal split (pre-2025 historical set / post-cutoff leakage-free set), preserves context-aware vulnerable--patched pairs, and introduces two diagnostic metrics: the Directional Failure Index (DFI) and Hierarchical Distance and Direction (HDD). We evaluate eight vanilla LLMs and 15 LoRA fine-tuned variants across non-targeted detection, targeted detection, and CWE classification. Our analysis yields two key results. First, data contamination provides no measurable advantage. Function-level analysis shows that 84% of nominally contaminated samples carry no usable memorization signal: vulnerable functions are absent or cross-mapped across datasets, and ~31% of contaminated samples carry CWE misclassification. Second, backbone directional priors dominate fine-tuning. Models exhibit stable, systematic failure modes (DFI ranging from -85.5 to +94.8 pp) that persist from historical to post-cutoff data and resist correction. Fine-tuning shifts the output threshold without changing the decision policy. This is calibration without comprehension: output distributions adapt to training data while the underlying security reasoning remains absent. The weakest backbone at binary detection (DeepSeek-R1) gains the most in coarse CWE classification, revealing that detection and understanding are decoupled capabilities. The best detection score reaches only 52.1% (+2.1 pp above chance); exact CWE ranking remains below 1.3% Top-1 accuracy, confirming that current LLMs lack reliable security reasoning for systems software, regardless of fine-tuning strategy.

</details>


### [259] [Efficient and Sound Probabilistic Verification for AI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20510)
*Alaia Solko-Breslin,Pramod Kaushik Mudrakarta,Mihai Christodorescu,Somesh Jha,Krishnamurthy Dj Dvijotham*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 针对在复杂数字环境中运行的AI智能体，现有运行时监控方法局限于确定性策略，无法处理概率性谓词或状态转移（如带失败概率的解分类器或PII检测器）。本文提出基于分布鲁棒优化的框架，在不依赖独立性假设的情况下，为策略违反概率计算严格上界，在终端和工具调用智能体基准测试中优于现有方法，并改善了安全-效用权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI智能体安全策略监控方法仅支持确定性策略，无法应对实际应用中存在的概率性谓词（如带失效概率的PII检测器）和状态转移，且无法满足先前概率推理工作所需的独立性假设，亟需一种不依赖独立性假设的概率策略违规验证方法。

Method: 提出基于分布鲁棒优化（Distributionally Robust Optimization）的框架，通过计算策略违反概率的严格上界（sound upper bounds），无需假设谓词间的独立相关性，实现对概率性安全策略的高效验证。

Result: 在标准终端和工具调用智能体基准测试上，该方法优于现有技术，改善了安全性与效用性之间的权衡，同时保证了对策略违反概率的严格有界约束。

Conclusion: 本文提出的分布鲁棒优化框架有效解决了AI智能体在复杂环境中面临概率性安全策略验证问题，不依赖独立性假设即可提供可靠的违规概率上界，具有较好的实用性和性能优势。

Abstract: Securing AI agents that operate in complex digital environments has become a critical need, and runtime monitoring approaches that formulate and enforce policies expressed in a formal language like Datalog offer a promising solution. However, existing approaches are restricted to deterministic policies. In many practical applications of AI agents, there is a need to enforce security policies in the face of ambiguity, leading to probabilistic predicates or state transitions (for example, a declassifier or Personally Identifiable Information (PII) detector that has some failure probability on each invocation). Furthermore, in many such applications, one cannot easily make the independence assumptions necessary to invoke prior work on probabilistic inference in Datalog. We address this by introducing a sound and efficient framework for such verification based on distributionally robust optimization, computing sound upper bounds on the probability of policy violation regardless of possible correlations between predicates. On standard benchmarks for terminal and tool calling agents, we demonstrate that our approach outperforms prior art and improves the security-utility trade-off while ensuring rigorous bounds on the probability of policy violation.

</details>


### [260] [Sovereign Execution Brokers: Enforcing Certificate-Bound Authority in Agentic Control Planes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20520)
*Jun He,Deying Yu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出 Sovereign Execution Broker (SEB)，一个运行时强制边界，用于将认证授权与执行分离，确保生产环境的变更操作必须经过证书验证、实时状态检查、作用域身份签发，才能调用基础设施 API，从而将认证权限转化为短生命、可撤销、可审计的运行时能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有访问控制机制（基于身份的授权）与保障层（动作认证）各自独立运行，缺乏在变更执行时刻的强制认证执行点，导致非确定性推理过程中不应拥有的生产变更权限可能被滥用。

Method: SEB 作为运行时执行边界，消费来自 Sovereign Assurance Boundary (SAB) 颁发的证书，验证请求变更与认证执行合约匹配，检查有效期、策略纪元、撤销纪元和实时状态漂移，签发作用域执行身份，调用基础设施 API，并记录签名的决策与结果记录。通过将提议、准入和执行分离，实现认证权限的运行时管控。

Result: 在 AWS 和 Kubernetes 集群上进行了原型评估，测量了延迟开销、撤销传播、漂移检测和故障注入下的安全性。

Conclusion: SEB 通过将认证授权转化为短生命、可撤销、可审计的运行时能力，有效解决了生产环境变更操作中缺乏强制认证执行点的问题，前提是生产变更 API 拒绝非代理身份。

Abstract: Autonomous agents are increasingly connected to cloud, deployment, and data-control workflows, but production mutation authority should not reside inside non-deterministic reasoning processes. Existing access-control mechanisms authorize identities, while assurance layers certify proposed actions; neither alone provides a mandatory enforcement point for certified authority at the moment of mutation. This paper introduces the Sovereign Execution Broker (SEB), a runtime enforcement boundary for certificate-bound agentic infrastructure. SEB consumes certificates issued by the Sovereign Assurance Boundary (SAB), verifies that the requested mutation matches the certified execution contract, checks validity windows, policy epochs, revocation epochs, and live-state drift, mints scoped execution identity, invokes infrastructure APIs, and records signed decision and outcome records. By separating proposal, admission, and execution, SEB turns certified authority into a short-lived, revocable, auditable runtime capability, provided that production mutation APIs reject non-broker identities. We present the SEB execution model, certificate and replay-verification predicates, scoped identity semantics, bypass-prevention deployment patterns, failure behavior, and a concrete prototype implementation. We evaluate the prototype on AWS and Kubernetes clusters, measuring latency overheads, revocation propagation, drift detection, and security under fault injection.

</details>


### [261] [From Efficiency to Leakage -- Privacy Backdoor in Federated Language Model Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20553)
*Shanghao Shi,Chaoyu Zhang,Heng Jin,Yang Xiao,Yevgeniy Vorobeychik,William Yeoh,Ning Zhang,Y. Thomas Hou,Wenjing Lou*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出NeuroImprint攻击，恶意参数服务器可在联邦学习的PEFT过程中植入后门，通过为每个训练样本分配专属记忆神经元并约束单次更新，实现从适配器中封闭式恢复客户端训练文本，在多个模型和数据集上达到59%-79%的高语义保真度重建。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦学习中使用PEFT（参数高效微调）已成为主流实践，但现有研究尚未系统探究恶意参数服务器能否在PEFT适配器中植入后门来窃取客户端训练数据的隐私。本文旨在揭示这一安全漏洞，证明即使只训练少量适配器参数，攻击者仍能实现高保真的训练数据重建。

Method: NeuroImprint攻击方法：为每个训练样本分配专属记忆神经元，并约束每个神经元在本地微调轨迹中最多更新一次。通过设计避免大batch size导致的跨样本碰撞以及Adam/AdamW等有状态优化器引起的跨步骤混合，使得每个参数更新仅对应单一训练样本。微调后，通过对孤立参数更新进行封闭式解析求逆，恢复文本嵌入并确定性映射回token序列。

Result: 在BERT、GPT-2、Qwen2、Llama3.2四个语言模型及四个不同领域数据集上验证，NeuroImprint能够重建59%到79%的微调样本，且具有高语义保真度，同时不降低模型效用。

Conclusion: 本文证明了联邦学习PEFT场景中存在严重隐私泄露风险：即使只训练少量适配器参数，恶意参数服务器仍可通过精心设计的后门攻击实现客户端训练数据的大规模重建。该发现对联邦学习的安全部署提出重要警示。

Abstract: Federated learning (FL) enables multiple parties to collaboratively fine-tune language models for domain-specific tasks without sharing raw data. Since full model fine-tuning is often prohibitively expensive for FL clients, parameter-efficient fine-tuning (PEFT) has become the de facto approach in practice, freezing the base model and training only a small set of adapters. In this paper, we show that a malicious parameter server can stealthily corrupt a PEFT adapter into a privacy backdoor that implicitly memorizes the client's training samples as isolated per-sample parameter updates stored in separate neurons, without degrading model utility. Concretely, our attack, NeuroImprint, assigns a dedicated memorization neuron to each training sample and constrains that each neuron is updated at most once along the local fine-tuning trajectory. This design mitigates both cross-sample collisions and cross-step mixing introduced by large local batches and stateful optimizers (e.g., Adam/AdamW) in language-model fine-tuning. After fine-tuning, the resulting isolated per-sample updates can be analytically inverted in closed form to recover text embeddings, which are then deterministically mapped back to token sequences. To understand the generality of our method, we implemented NeuroImprint on multiple language models (BERT, GPT-2, Qwen2, and Llama3.2) and evaluated it across four fine-tuning datasets spanning diverse domains. The results demonstrate that our attack can reconstruct 59% to 79% of all finetuning samples with high semantic fidelity.

</details>


<div id='cs.DB'></div>

# cs.DB [[Back]](#toc)

### [262] [REMOP: REmote-Memory-aware OPerator Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19576)
*Shiquan Zhang,Yunhao Mao,Yuqiu Zhang,Gengrui Zhang,Jeyhun Karimov,Hans-Arno Jacobsen*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: REMOP提出了一种面向远程内存的算子优化框架，通过引入传输轮次感知的内存策略，在DuckDB中优化阻塞嵌套循环连接、外部归并排序和外部哈希连接等算子的溢出执行，最高减少97%传输轮次和48%算子运行时间，端到端TPC-H和TPC-DS查询性能分别提升22.7%和26.4%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 远程内存和分解式内存层级扩展了分析数据库引擎的有效内存容量，但改变了内存溢出查询处理的成本结构。经典算子分析和缓冲区分配启发式策略主要针对磁盘溢出优化（最小化总I/O量），在远程内存场景下可能因触发过多传输轮次而导致次优性能。

Method: 将传输轮次纳入延迟成本模型，推导算子特定的缓冲区划分策略，并在DuckDB中为阻塞嵌套循环连接、外部归并排序和外部哈希连接三种算子实现了该方法的实例化。

Result: 在双节点计算-内存测试床上，REMOP在溢出密集的微基准测试中最多减少97%的传输轮次和48%的算子运行时间；端到端TPC-H和TPC-DS查询的平均运行时间分别降低22.7%和26.4%。

Conclusion: 面向远程内存的传输轮次感知内存策略能有效提升内存预算紧张下的查询执行性能，优于传统面向磁盘溢出的优化策略。

Abstract: Remote and disaggregated memory tiers expand the effective memory capacity of analytical database engines, but they also reshape the cost structure of out-of-memory query processing. When an operator spills beyond local DRAM, moving pages to remote memory incurs both data-transfer time and a fixed round-trip latency per transfer. Classical operator analyses and buffer-allocation heuristics primarily target disk spilling by minimizing total I/O volume. Under remote memory, these strategies can be suboptimal because they may trigger excessive transfer rounds. We present REMOP, a remote-memory-aware operator optimization framework that uses transfer-round-aware intra-operator memory policies to improve out-of-memory execution under tight memory budgets. REMOP introduces the number of transfer rounds into the latency cost model and derives operator-specific buffer-partitioning strategies, instantiating the approach for blocked nested-loop join, external merge sort, and external hash join in DuckDB. Our evaluation on a two-node compute-memory testbed shows that REMOP reduces transfer rounds by up to 97% and operator runtime by up to 48% on spill-heavy microbenchmarks, and lowers the average runtime of spilling TPC-H and TPC-DS queries by 22.7% and 26.4% end-to-end.

</details>


### [263] [DeQL: A Decision Query Language for Prescriptive Analytics over Relational Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19751)
*Matteo Brucato,Fjodor Kholodkov,Soren Little,Jakob Mayer,Duc Nguyen*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: DeQL（决策查询语言）扩展了SQL，通过CREATE CANDIDATES和DECIDE两个核心结构，使用户能够基于关系数据、策略约束和可度量目标表达并计算最优决策方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有SQL无法直接表达决策优化问题（如从关系数据中选择最优方案），用户需要手动编写复杂的求解逻辑。作者希望将SQL"用户声明什么、引擎决定如何做"的设计原则扩展到决策场景。

Method: 在SQL中引入两个新结构：CREATE CANDIDATES（从关系数据源定义候选方案空间）和DECIDE（声明决策变量、命名约束和目标函数）。设计了正式语法、执行模型，并支持子集选择、分配、调度等多种决策类型及不确定优化扩展。

Result: 给出了完整的语言规范，包括设计原则、语法、形式化文法、执行模型，并通过多个示例展示了表达能力。该规范是首个版本，核心结构已固定。

Conclusion: DeQL将SQL的声明式查询范式扩展到决策优化领域，实现了"用户声明目标，引擎自动求解"的愿景，为后续版本奠定了核心基础。

Abstract: DeQL (Decision Query Language) extends SQL to express decision queries: given options drawn from relational data, constraints from policy, and a measurable objective, a DeQL query computes the best course of action. Two constructs carry the extension: CREATE CANDIDATES, which defines the space of options from relational sources, and DECIDE, which declares decision variables, named constraints, and an objective over them. The design follows SQL's principles: the user states what to optimize while the engine chooses how to solve it, every query consumes and produces relations, and the structure of a problem stays visible to the engine. This document specifies the language (its design principles, syntax, formal grammar, and execution model) with examples spanning subset selection, allocation, assignment, scheduling, and decisions at multiple levels of aggregation, and extensions for optimization under uncertainty, inline model scoring, and time- and quality-bounded solving. It is the first version of the specification; the language is under active development, and this version fixes the core constructs on which later revisions will build.

</details>


### [264] [Policy-aware Vector Search: A Vision for Fine Grained Access Control in Vector Databases](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19803)
*Lakshmi Sahithi Yalamarthi,Primal Pappachan*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 本文提出向量数据库中细粒度访问控制（FGAC）的愿景，形式化定义了策略模型与实施问题，比较了多种实施策略，并指出了关键开放挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 向量数据库在安全敏感场景（如RAG和AI管道）中广泛应用，但缺乏细粒度访问控制（FGAC）支持，导致数据访问无法遵循用户特定策略。

Method: 形式化定义了向量数据库中的FGAC策略模型及策略实施问题；比较了不同的策略实施方法；通过初步实验评估各策略在正确性、召回率和延迟之间的权衡。

Result: 初步发现表明，FGAC的正确实施与高ANN搜索召回率和低查询延迟之间存在固有矛盾；不同实施策略在三个维度上存在显著权衡。

Conclusion: 提出了Policy-aware Vector Search的研究愿景，明确了向量数据库中FGAC的关键开放挑战，为未来研究指明了方向。

Abstract: Vector databases are increasingly used in security sensitive contexts with Retrieval Augmented Generation and organizational AI pipelines; however, their security capabilities remain limited. Specifically, Fine-grained Access Control (FGAC) which is required to ensure that data access adheres to user-specific policies is not fully supported in modern vector databases. Unlike relational databases, vector databases combine structured and unstructured attributes to provide semantic, approximate query results, which complicates FGAC implementation. This creates an inherent tension between enforcing FGAC policies correctly, achieving high ANN search recall and maintaining low query latency. In this paper, we present a vision for Policy-aware Vector Search by formalizing the FGAC policy model in vector databases as well as the enforcement problem. We compare various enforcement strategies, present preliminary findings, and identify key open challenges for future research in policy-aware vector search.

</details>


### [265] [Query-aware Routing for Filtered Approximate Nearest Neighbors Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19898)
*Qianqian Xiong,Mengxuan Zhang*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种查询感知的路由框架，通过轻量级ML模型预测各候选方法在特定查询上的召回率，结合离线基准表选择最优召回-QPS权衡的方法，在多个数据集上实现了比现有过滤式ANN基线更优的性能，且额外延迟可忽略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有过滤式ANN方法在不同数据集和谓词类型下表现不一，不存在单一主导方法，且同一数据集内不同查询的最佳方法也存在差异，因此需要一种查询感知的自动路由机制来动态选择最优方法。

Method: 提出查询感知路由框架：1) 轻量级ML模型（回归而非分类）预测各候选方法在查询上的召回率；2) 路由器查询离线基准表（记录各方法和参数设置对应的召回率与QPS）；3) 综合预测召回率和实测QPS，选择最佳权衡的方法。通过消融研究将22个候选特征缩减为3个核心特征。

Result: 在6个真实数据集上训练模型，在5个未见过的验证数据集上测试。路由器在所有5个验证数据集上相比现有过滤式ANN基线实现了最先进的召回率与QPS平衡，且额外延迟开销可忽略不计。

Conclusion: 查询感知路由是解决过滤式ANN方法异构性问题的有效途径，通过少量特征进行回归预测即可实现跨数据集的泛化能力，在召回率和查询性能之间取得优异平衡。

Abstract: Filtered ANN search, which combines vector similarity with attribute predicates, is a core primitive in modern vector databases and retrieval-augmented generation. We benchmark all major categorical filtered ANN methods across multiple datasets under three predicates and find that no single method dominates. Moreover, even within a single dataset and predicate type, the best method for a query can vary. Therefore, we propose a query-aware routing framework. A lightweight ML model predicts each candidate method's recall on the query, and the router consults an offline benchmark table that maps every method and parameter setting to its measured recall and QPS, then selects the method with the best recall--QPS trade-off. Our ablation study narrows 22 candidate features to a minimal set of three and we adopt regression rather than classification as the prediction target to sharpen accuracy. Our model is trained on six real-world datasets and applied to five unseen validation datasets. The final result shows that our router achieves state-of-the-art recall and QPS balance across all five validation datasets compared to existing filtered ANN baselines, while incurring negligible latency overhead.

</details>


### [266] [The Bi-Channel Networking Paradigm for Database Systems in the Cloud](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19969)
*Georg Kreuzmayr,Muhammad El-Hindi,Benjamin Wagner,Tobias Ziegler,Viktor Leis*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 本文提出用于数据库系统的双通道通信范式，将用户空间UDP用于高性能数据路径，内核TCP用于可靠控制路径，实现高吞吐低开销的通信，在200 Gbit/s分布式shuffle和百万级消息复制KV存储上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代高速云网络中，传统内核TCP协议栈的CPU开销成为数据库系统的性能瓶颈，而直接替换为UDP又需要重新实现可靠性等关键保证，亟需一种兼顾性能与可靠性的新通信范式。

Method: 提出双通道范式（bi-channel paradigm），将通信分离为两条通道：高性能数据路径（采用用户空间UDP，利用现代NIC能力）用于延迟和带宽敏感操作，可靠控制路径（采用内核TCP）用于协调和恢复。两种通道可以灵活组合实现。

Result: 在分布式shuffle中，用3个CPU核心即可饱和200 Gbit/s网络带宽；在复制键值存储中，每秒可处理数百万条消息。

Conclusion: 数据库系统不应再将网络视为黑盒，而应与数据库操作协同设计。双通道范式能够高效且简洁地解决高速网络下的CPU开销问题，兼顾性能与可靠性。

Abstract: When network links were slow, cloud and distributed database systems could rely on generic kernel abstractions and treat network communication as a black box. With today's fast cloud networks, this approach breaks down: database performance becomes limited by the CPU overhead of the kernel TCP stack. Replacing TCP with user-space UDP can reduce this overhead, but it requires reimplementing essential guarantees, such as reliability and ordering. To solve this conundrum, database systems should no longer treat networking as a black box but co-design it with database operations. We propose the bi-channel paradigm for database systems, which separates communication into two channels: A high-performance data path for latency- and bandwidth-sensitive operations, and a reliable control path for coordination and recovery. We implement the paradigm by combining user-space UDP and kernel-based TCP, though other stack combinations are possible. This design exploits modern NIC capabilities while preserving TCP's reliability. We demonstrate the paradigm's efficiency and simplicity in two representative settings: a distributed shuffle saturating 200 Gbit/s with three CPU cores, and a replicated key-value store processing millions of messages per second.

</details>


### [267] [AgenticDB: Agentic Performance Reconfiguration for Database Workloads](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20318)
*Xinyue Yang,Chaozheng Wang,Chen Zheng,Heng Zhang,Yanjun Wu*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 提出AgenticDB，一个基于智能体的数据库工作负载重配置框架，通过运行时交互与上下文感知机制，在DBMS和OS层面实现自主诊断瓶颈、探索配置空间、安全执行变更并积累经验，在MySQL和PostgreSQL上相比最强基线平均提升118.1%性能，减少22.6%的达到最优时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动调优器将调优形式化为对DBMS knob值的迭代搜索，导致执行成本高、过早缩小配置空间，且未能充分满足实际部署需求：无法从系统反馈诊断运行时瓶颈、无法探索OS级重配置机会、无法稳健执行变更、无法从先前试验和任务中学习。

Method: 提出AgenticDB框架，核心是上下文接地的harness，与环境交互：提出DBMS级和OS级变更、在安全约束下应用变更、观察工作负载性能和运行时状态、利用执行反馈指导后续决策。通过运行时交互实现瓶颈诊断、探索更广泛的配置空间、避免不安全/不支持的操作、在任务内和跨任务积累经验。

Result: 在MySQL和PostgreSQL上使用YCSB、Sysbench、TPC-H工作负载进行实验，AgenticDB在所有评估工作负载上取得最佳最终性能，平均超过最强基线118.1%，达到最优的时间减少22.6%。OS级动作空间、稳健执行生命周期和记忆增强规划贡献了更有效和实用的数据库重配置。

Conclusion: AgenticDB将数据库调优转变为一种自我改进的重配置过程，运行时反馈迭代优化后续决策，实现了更高效、更实用的数据库配置调优。

Abstract: Database configuration tuning is critical for workload performance, but practical tuning on real deployments remains difficult. Existing automatic tuners mostly formulate tuning as iterative search over DBMS knob values. This formulation leads to high execution cost, prematurely narrows the configuration space, and leaves practical requirements insufficiently addressed: diagnosing runtime bottlenecks from system feedback, exploring OS-level reconfiguration opportunities, executing changes robustly, and learning from previous trials and tasks.
  We propose AgenticDB, an agentic framework for database workload reconfiguration. AgenticDB implements a context-grounded harness that interacts with the target database environment by proposing DBMS- and OS-level changes, applying them under safety constraints, observing workload performance and runtime states, and using execution feedback to guide subsequent decisions. This runtime interaction enables AgenticDB to diagnose bottlenecks, explore a broader DBMS- and OS-level reconfiguration space, avoid unsafe or unsupported actions, and accumulate experience within and across reconfiguration tasks. As a result, AgenticDB turns database tuning into a self-refining reconfiguration process in which runtime feedback iteratively improves later decisions.
  We conduct extensive experiments on MySQL and PostgreSQL using YCSB, Sysbench, and TPC-H workloads. The results show that AgenticDB achieves the best final performance on all evaluated workloads, improving over the strongest baseline by 118.1% on average and reducing aggregate time-to-best by 22.6%. The results also demonstrate that its OS-level action space, robust execution lifecycle, and memory-enhanced planning contribute to more effective and practical database reconfiguration.

</details>


### [268] [Caching for Dollars, Not Hits: An Exact Offline Reference for Cloud-Egress Caching and the Crossover That Decides When It Pays](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20539)
*Madhulatha Mandarapu,Sandeep Kunkunuru*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 本文针对云对象存储的缓存问题，指出传统缓存优化失效率（miss rate）的目标与云计费模式（按GET请求次数和出站字节数计费）不匹配。作者提出了以“美元成本”为优化目标的离线最优策略，并通过线性规划实现精确解（均匀大小页面）或近似解（可变大小页面，误差约4%）。基于真实云价格和Twitter trace的基准测试发现：①异质性遗憾定律——LRU的美元遗憾随缺失成本分散度上升（Spearman 0.87），而成本感知的GreedyDual可将其降低约一个数量级；②竞争前沿——GreedyDual的残差遗憾在预算恰好覆盖昂贵工作集时消失；③闭式交叉点s* = GET费用/出口费率（S3约4KB，GCS约330B），用于预测何时需要美元感知缓存。价格向量本身即可使工作负载跨越s*，改变缓存策略选择。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 云对象存储的计费模式按GET请求次数和出站字节数收费，而非延迟。传统缓存优化目标是降低失效率，但这与云环境下最小化美元成本的目标不一致：低频但昂贵的对象比高频但便宜的对象可能多花费数千倍。缺乏衡量现有启发式策略与美元最优离线策略之间差距的基准测试。

Method: 提出基于积分区间线性规划的精确离线最优策略（均匀大小页面），并将流式离线界（cost-FOO）扩展到可变大小页面场景（NP-hard）。构建了可复现的计费忠实基准，比较LRU、GreedyDual等策略与离线最优策略在真实云价格下的美元成本差距。

Result: ①LRU的美元遗憾与缺失成本分散度强相关（Spearman 0.87），GreedyDual将其降低约10倍；②GreedyDual的残差遗憾在预算匹配昂贵工作集时趋近于零；③推导出闭式交叉点s* = GET费用/出口费率（S3约4KB，GCS约330B），低于该值的对象应使用美元感知缓存。Twitter trace验证了价格向量迁移可改变缓存策略需求。

Conclusion: 传统以失效率为目标的缓存策略在云计费场景下并非最优，应考虑异构缺失成本的美元影响。提出了有效的离线基准和实用的美元感知缓存策略（GreedyDual），并给出了指导何时需要此类策略的闭式判据。

Abstract: When a cache miss fetches from cloud object storage, the bill is per GET request and per byte of egress, not latency. Classic caching minimizes the miss rate, the wrong objective: a rarely but expensively fetched object can cost thousands of times more dollars than a frequently but cheaply fetched one. Generalized-caching theory bounds the miss-cost objective, but no reported benchmark measures how far deployed heuristics sit from the dollar-optimal offline policy on real cloud prices. We supply that reference. For uniform-size page caches with heterogeneous miss costs the offline dollar-optimum is exact in polynomial time via an integral interval linear program -- validated against brute force; variable sizes are NP-hard, so we extend the flow-based offline bound from the hit-ratio objective to dollars (cost-FOO), tight to about four percent. Against this reference we find: (i) a heterogeneity-regret law -- LRU's dollar-regret rises with miss-cost dispersion (Spearman 0.87) while cost-aware GreedyDual cuts it to roughly a tenth; (ii) a contention frontier -- GreedyDual's residual regret collapses to near zero exactly when the budget fits the expensive working set, and is the open slice otherwise; and (iii) a closed-form crossover s* = GET_fee/egress_rate (about 4 KB on S3, 330 B on GCS) that predicts which deployments need dollar-aware caching at all. On a real Twitter trace the price vector alone moves the workload across s*, shifting the regime as predicted. The artifact is a reproducible billing-faithful benchmark; heuristics and bounds it builds on are prior work, credited.

</details>


<div id='cs.PL'></div>

# cs.PL [[Back]](#toc)

### [269] [Effect Systems as Abstract Interpretations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19686)
*Colin S. Gordon*

Main category: cs.PL

TL;DR: 本文建立了效应系统（effect systems）与抽象解释（abstract interpretation）之间的形式化关系，通过将效应量词（effect quantales）嵌入抽象域，并从事件发生角度将效应量词重新解释为抽象解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已知类型系统可被抽象解释所捕获，但类型-效应系统与抽象解释之间的形式化关系尚未明确建立，现有研究缺乏对两者之间一般性形式关系的探索。

Method: 将效应量词嵌入到抽象域中；提出从事件发生（event occurrences）角度而非状态或值角度来恢复效应量词作为抽象解释的一般形式。

Result: 成功建立了效应系统与抽象解释之间的形式化关联，证明效应量词可以作为基于事件发生的抽象解释来理解。

Conclusion: 效应系统与抽象解释之间存在严谨的形式化关系，从事件视角出发为效应系统的抽象解释语义提供了统一框架。

Abstract: Many forms of static reasoning about program behaviours are known in the literature, yet formal relationships are studied surprisingly infrequently. While most type systems are well-known to be captured by abstract interpretations, the situation for type-and-effect systems is, in the general case, unsettled despite strong hypotheses and occasional framing of effect systems as abstract interpretations.
  We develop a formal relationship between abstract interpretations and a general class of effect systems. First, we describe an embedding of effect quantales into abstract domains. Second, we recover the general form of an effect quantale as an abstract interpretation -- not on states or values, but on event occurrences.

</details>


<div id='cs.NE'></div>

# cs.NE [[Back]](#toc)

### [270] [Weight Adaptation for Improving Parallel Performance of Adaptive Stochastic Natural Gradient](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19861)
*Yutaro Yamada,Kento Uchida,Shinichi Shirakawa*

Main category: cs.NE

TL;DR: 本文提出WA-ASNG，通过在ASNG中引入权重自适应机制，动态调整种群权重参数以最大化自然梯度更新方向信号，从而在二进制优化问题中显著优于PBIL和ASNG，尤其在存在强噪声和大种群规模（25-100）场景下表现高效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有概率模型进化算法（如ASNG）虽能自适应学习率实现高效优化，但在并行评估需求增长下，如何为更大种群规模设置合适的权重参数仍不明确。

Method: WA-ASNG通过累积自然梯度估计更新方向的信号，并采用梯度上升法最大化该信号以自适应更新权重参数；学习率自适应保证目标期望值单调改进的充分条件，权重自适应则旨在最大化该改进。

Result: 在二进制优化问题中，种群规模25-100的各种设置下，WA-ASNG性能优于PBIL和ASNG，且在强噪声环境下仍能高效运行。

Conclusion: 权重自适应机制有效提升了ASNG在大种群场景下的优化性能与鲁棒性。

Abstract: Probabilistic model-based evolutionary algorithms are promising for black-box optimization. Specifically, the adaptive stochastic natural gradient (ASNG) adaptively updates its learning rate, a typical hyperparameter in probabilistic model-based evolutionary algorithms, thereby realizing efficient and robust optimization. Although weight parameters are common hyperparameters, with the increasing demand for parallel evaluation of time-consuming tasks, it remains unclear how to set suitable weights for larger population sizes. In this paper, we propose Weight Adaptation ASNG (WA-ASNG), which incorporates a weight adaptation mechanism into ASNG. We calculated the estimated signal of the update direction from the accumulations of the natural gradient. Then, to maximize the signal, WA-ASNG adaptively updates its weight parameters by a gradient ascent over the optimization. While the learning rate adaptation plays a role in satisfying a sufficient condition for monotonic improvement of the expected objective value, the mechanism of weight adaptation is intended to maximize this improvement. The experimental results demonstrate that WA-ASNG outperforms PBIL and ASNG across various settings with population sizes ranging from 25 to 100 for binary optimization problems. Furthermore, WA-ASNG can perform efficiently in the presence of strong noise. Our code is available at https://github.com/shiralab/WA-ASNG .

</details>


### [271] [Hybrid ANN-SNN Pipeline with Local Plasticity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20151)
*Denis Larionov,Khairutin Shtanchaev,Mikhail Kiselev,Mikhail Korovin,Ivan Tugoy*

Main category: cs.NE

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种混合ANN-SNN流水线，通过将预训练的人工神经网络（EfficientNet编码器）与脉冲神经网络分类器（CoLaNET）结合，利用速率编码将激活转换为脉冲序列，并使用局部生物启发学习规则训练SNN，在64类ImageNet任务上达到99.09%的准确率，实现了与深度网络相当的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 利用预训练人工神经网络丰富的嵌入表示来提升脉冲神经网络的性能，同时保持生物合理性和计算效率，避免端到端梯度传播的局限性。

Method: 构建混合ANN-SNN流水线：使用预训练的EfficientNet作为编码器，通过速率编码将其激活转换为脉冲序列，再输入CoLaNET脉冲分类器，采用局部生物启发学习规则（而非反向传播）训练SNN部分。

Result: 在64类ImageNet基准测试上达到99.09%的准确率，性能媲美传统深度网络。

Conclusion: 该工作展示了一种生物 plausible 且高效的框架，能够将强大的预训练编码器适配到下游脉冲神经网络任务中，在保持生物合理性的同时实现了高性能。

Abstract: This work proposes a hybrid ANN-SNN pipeline that effectively leverages the rich embeddings of pretrained artificial neural networks (ANNs) to enable high-performance spiking neural networks (SNNs). The architecture couples a pretrained EfficientNet encoder with a CoLaNET spiking classifier. We convert the encoder's activations into spike trains via rate-coding and train the subsequent SNN classifier using local, biologically inspired learning rules, bypassing end-to-end gradient propagation. This approach achieves 99.09% accuracy on a 64-class ImageNet benchmark, demonstrating performance on par with conventional deep networks. The work presents a biologically plausible and efficient framework for adapting powerful pretrained encoders to downstream spiking neural network tasks.

</details>
